# Later source-unit findings and qualifications
This is a source-backed research catalogue, compiled October 29, 2026. The evaluation chat began September 2, 2026. Coverage is against the frozen registers listed in PUBLICATION.json; it is not a claim to have replayed every historical chat turn or independently checked every primary source.

The entries overlap. A file discrepancy, a reported event, an allegation, a missing input, a model-dependent result and a failed control are different kinds of record. Counts are not independent anomalies or probabilities of conspiracy. Source-level checking, historical authenticity, physical replication, intent and cause remain separate. Read each note with its limits and the linked owning record. Existing stable IDs are retained; this is navigation, not a replacement authority.

External citations are inherited from the held research. They are not represented as freshly fetched or independently authenticated in this compilation. Local citations point to pinned entry copies and, where resolvable, their owning reports. Missing or unavailable originals remain open.

These are held owning-unit README summaries found at publication paths at this compilation cutoff. Inclusion does not assert a new main-branch merge, independent corroboration or completion of native-record tests. The source report is controlling for detailed reading scope and original TODOs.

<a id="unit-steel-inventory-transmittals-2026-10-29-4b467619"></a>
## Steel transfer instructions: a new Clearwater record, distinct objects and missing operative terms

October 29, 2026. Existing B188/H005, AUD011/AUD016, SPT01–SPT08 and STL01–STL04 remain open. **One newly acquired official municipal source advances the transfer-record trail. It does not recover the artifact inventory or identify a disputed WTC 7 specimen.** The December 13, 2010 Clearwater work-session minutes, physical/printed pp. 3–4, item 3.2, identify artifact **G-0006**, citing a **January 31, 2007** inventory. They describe a 36-inch tube without visible identification marks and conditional cutting into two pieces. The item seeks authorization to sign/accept; it does not by itself prove adoption, execution, shipment, cutting or receipt. Page 4 also uses **“holds neither”** in describing indemnification. Preserve the wording; its own operative agreement is needed before assigning legal effect. CW01–CW03 in COMPARISON.json. The earlier Beverly Hills packet concerns a different artifact, visually read **I-0137** (raw extraction **1-0137**). Staff reports nonalteration on p. 2. Its reproduced order, pp. 11–12, reserves inspection/testing for litigation parties or their representatives upon ten days notice. The preceding review found no equivalent express nonalteration clause in the attached letter/order; any separate instruction remains unacquired. These accounts and source layers are retained unchanged. This unit newly re-viewed p. 2 and pp. 11–12; the remainder relies on the earlier qualified review.

<a id="unit-faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30-c4733d4c"></a>
## Received update057 — Edition-qualified FAA transcript policy and concrete original-record targets

**Acquisition and actual scope:** Two already-held Order 8020.11B editions were inspected without redownload. The 2000 document is 389 pages, SHA-256 4a079eef3618704785a50283ca6a28fb26be694191570a7f0b8549893ef8e3cf; the FAA-hosted edition including May 12, 2003 Change 1 is 397 pages, SHA-256 8144fe5bdb3badb2131a000875d0a7709036c2b5bcb4f0e804d68a4a0cbe00a4. Only ten selected images were read: 2000 physical 51/58/59/60 and 2003 physical 2/3/51/60/61/62. Selected policy-text extraction followed image reading; this is not whole-order reading or a case-compliance finding.

**Policy and contrary context:** 77a (2000 physical58/printed322, selected-text22–36) distinguishes partial subject-aircraft selection from full recorded communications at a position over the defined period; positions are separately transcribed unless integration requested. Labels alone do not measure missing duration or make two positions’ contents identical. 77d(1) prescribes first-page memorandum, date, certification and legend fields; 77c describes recording-copy transcription with original available for checking. These rules do not authenticate two-page memoranda as complete bodies or resolve aircraft/case/footer mismatches.

**Timing, uncertainty and copies:** 77d(3)(a) (physical60/printed324,22–24) calls for a timing-method remark in certification when time announcements are absent; actual equipment and remarks are required before asserting noncompliance or a clock offset. 77d(4) (same page,32–37) distinguishes unintelligible notation from parenthesized/asterisked interpreted garbling with an uncertainty footnote. Sector55’s declined and line1078’s requested footnotes remain distinct conditional judgments. 72c(11) (physical51/printed315,4–9) sanitizes personal numbers in copies subject to an exception, while retaining them in originals; it does not authorize destruction of original records or alteration of our sources.

**Edition and organization differences:** Seven selected clause comparisons collapse whitespace only: four matches and three literal differences, including line-end/glyph/punctuation differences left unchanged. The 2003 physical62/printed328,13–23 explicitly adds optional grouping of four or more quiet minutes and transmissions beginning on a minute. Do not backdate that rule to 2002 corrections. The page-control chart is preserved. Form/personnel/weather section numbering differs across policy and review documents; labels alone establish no deletion or governing exception. Two delimiter failures were preserved and repaired without changing sources; 20 later integrity/reproduction checks do not erase that history.

**Open TODO:** Inspect actual transcript certification/timing-method remarks, uncertainty markings and original→copy→transcript/check chain, applicable version/exception/index directions and referenced 76a NOTE, not newly read here. The captured finding aid rows3125–3130 supplies dated uninspected locators: AEA1130/7598367, AEA1126/7598364 (transcript label but MEMO filename), AEA1125/7598363 and AEA1127–1129. Later units below advance selected acquisitions without authenticating native versions, exact enclosures, receipt or final approval. Actual May 2003 draft/corrected QFR/delivery and signed administrative/recommendation outcomes remain open.

**Acceptance boundary:** All five tracks and eighteen substantive criteria remain open/partial. Source acquisition, scoped reproduction, publication and substantive acceptance are distinct. Telephone native joins; recorder authenticated configuration/custody/paired output/clocks; materials specimen/native/calibration/same-specimen joins; steel preservation/transfer/inventory/attribution/selection inputs remain TODOs. Prior technical tests are neither duplicated nor relabeled unperformed. Overview integration checks the published derivative, not a fresh independent primary-image, audio, clock, custody or historical-compliance test.

**Citations:** [fixed published report](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2e38da9806a4b3bc2550d66718831488aea76a6c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30/README.md); [complete selected ledger/crosswalk](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2e38da9806a4b3bc2550d66718831488aea76a6c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30/RECORD-CROSSWALK.json); [exact source/page/line pins](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2e38da9806a4b3bc2550d66718831488aea76a6c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30/SOURCE-PINS.json); [original verification including failures](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2e38da9806a4b3bc2550d66718831488aea76a6c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30/VERIFICATION.json); [original study text changes](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2e38da9806a4b3bc2550d66718831488aea76a6c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-transcript-policy-crosswalk-2026-10-30/TEXT-CHANGES.diff).

# ASCE letter: conflicting dates confirmed in the reproduced page images

November 2, 2026. Existing STL01/STL04, SPT01–SPT08/SR01, B188/H005 and AUD011/AUD016 remain open. This supplement completes a previously deferred page-image check. It does not recover native correspondence or decide which date is correct.

## What the pages show

| Record | Exact visible date | Location |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Letter first page | **November 15, 2001** | Printed 163, below ASCE letterhead and above the addressee. [Page image source](https://books.google.com/books?id=R1GrCiT1US8C&pg=PA163) |
| Continuation 2 of 3 | **September 15, 2001** | Printed 164, upper-left continuation header. [Page image source](https://books.google.com/books?id=R1GrCiT1US8C&pg=PA164) |
| Continuation 3 of 3 | **September 15, 2001** | Printed 165, upper-left continuation header. [Page image source](https://books.google.com/books?id=R1GrCiT1US8C&pg=PA165) |

These pages reproduce one ASCE letter addressed to Commerce Secretary Donald Evans. The reproduced body thanks NIST's laboratory for assistance and seeks support for a public/private research program. That context was already available in the text export; it is not a new funding or technical finding.

The [preceding study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e0e2c12fb5363eaf15f6ea2be2e4679d6afa79e4/investigation/broader-evidence/steel-cooperation-records-2026-11-01/OPEN-RECORDS.md) preserved these dates as unverified text-layer observations. The three newly acquired images visibly repeat them. Both representations remain preserved, without selecting a controlling value. Three pages, two representation types and repeated dates do not constitute separate letters or independent corroboration.

## What this means and does not establish

The verified addition is narrow: the public reproduction presents inconsistent date fields across its first page and continuation headers. Confidence is high in that visible reading; the native letter's date and the reason for the discrepancy remain unknown.

Possible causes include drafting/header reuse, different page versions or later assembly/reproduction differences. These are untested alternatives, not findings. The observations do not establish backdating, fabrication, coordination, concealment or an error in NIST's collapse calculations. An innocuous document-production issue could explain the record; native versions and transmission records are needed to test that possibility alongside the others.

## Scope and next records

All three displayed page images were acquired and visually examined; the three date lines in the held text export were rechecked. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-78fa9ed45f5f4f0e697a) records URLs, acquisition times, exact page/header locations, image dimensions and SHA-256 values. No complete-book review, native-letter authentication, signature analysis or independent edition comparison was performed.

[TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-6dc743d4bd3985a70ebc) retain native ASCE/Commerce correspondence, version history, dispatch/receipt and the congressional submission/assembly chain. The earlier steel custody/attribution, FAA correction/disposition and other original-record requirements remain open. No outreach or request was sent. The earlier reports and conflicting source bytes remain unchanged. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c9256527d5578a69acb2), [verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-72439eefe733a2992576) and [addition diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-65a3792c213a02e1bae2) distinguish the performed check from unresolved historical questions.

# Beverly Hills recorded approval of the WTC steel transfer

**November 3, 2026.** The restored share made the preserved September 8, 2011 council minutes readable. They record adoption of the steel-transfer ratification as **resolution 11-R-12833**. The related consent-calendar vote lists **five ayes and no noes**. This supplies a decision record missing from our earlier agenda-packet review. Physical delivery, specimen identification and testing remain unverified.

Research identifiers: STL01–STL04; AUD011, AUD016; B188, H005; T01–T07; BH-M01–BH-M04. This is a public-source research update, not a court filing or a scientific test.

## What the minutes say

The [official minutes](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1) identify the September 8, 2011 **Adjourned Regular Meeting**. The archive associates it with the Formal Meeting, clip **2444**; the same-date Study Session has a different clip, **2442**. Physical page 1 lists all five council members present, with none absent. Its separate **F-1** document marker is not the steel item's number.

| Exact source location | Recorded observation | Limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Physical page 5, opening consent motion](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1#page=5) | Gold moved, Bosse seconded: remove **F-8** for a separate vote; remove **F-14I and F-14J** for discussion; approve all consent-agenda items **except F-8** by reference. | Preserve the separate exception and discussion wording. F-7 is not named as removed. The final characters in F-14I and F-14J are letters. |
| [Physical page 6, item F-7 and disposition](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1#page=6) | Ratification of the Port Authority agreement transferring a WTC steel artifact for the Beverly Hills 9/11 Memorial Garden. The disposition reads **“ITEM F-7: ADOPTED RES# 11-R-12833.”** | The comment describes future display at the garden. It does not establish shipment, arrival time or physical receipt. |
| [Physical page 10, “Vote on Consent Calendar”](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1#page=10) | Ayes: **Gold, Bosse, Mirisch, Brien, Brucker**. Noes: **None**. **CARRIED**. | Association with F-7 depends on the page 5 motion's scope and page 6 disposition, not the vote count alone. |
| Physical pages 6–7, separate F-8 motion and vote | F-8 has its own motion, vote and agreement disposition. | This different item is not counted as a second steel approval. Its similarly unanimous vote is not substituted for the consent-calendar vote. |
| [Physical page 11, approval block](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1#page=11) | The minutes record approval and adoption on **September 20, 2011**, with visible clerk/mayor attestation marks. | Signature marks were observed, not forensically authenticated. September 20 labels approval of the minutes; September 8 labels the meeting and item action. |

The PDF is a scan served by the official city archive. It is not a separately obtained signed resolution, native authoring file or verbatim meeting recording. The sentence concerning original signed minutes under **G. Commission Synopsis** on page 10 is not used to establish custody of this council minute or resolution: its surrounding section concerns commission records.

## Preserve both versions and their different fields

The [previously held agenda packet, physical pages 4–5](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/MetaViewer.php?clip_id=2444&meta_id=136233&view_id=11#page=4), remains unchanged. Keep these observations side by side:

| Agenda's proposed-resolution copy | Later minute record |
| --- | --- |
| Resolution-number field stops at **11-R-**. | Item F-7 is marked adopted as **11-R-12833**. |
| **Adopted:** field and mayor/clerk adoption-attestation lines are blank. | Minutes record the item as adopted; the minutes themselves record September 20 approval and visible attestation marks. |
| Approval-as-to-form and Fire Chief approval marks are visible elsewhere. | These separate marks are not completed or authenticated using the minutes. |
| Section 2 identifies steel **I-0137** and the Port Authority's WTC Artifacts Inventory Archive. | F-7 does not itself print that artifact number. Association is through the same meeting/clip and agreement subject. |
| Section 5 directs entry of the resolution and certification into the **Book of Resolutions**. | The minutes supply a resolution number with which to seek that separate record. The entry is not reproduced in the minutes. |

No blank field was completed, source replaced or earlier assessment rewritten. The earlier report correctly recorded what the then-held packet did not verify; the newly read minutes add a separate observation. These records have different roles. Their existence does not by itself establish why a particular field was blank, authenticate every version, or demonstrate file substitution.

The earlier visual **I-0137** versus extracted **1-0137** caution remains preserved. This review does not adjudicate that reading or turn it into a new independent discrepancy. Likewise, **September 8 meeting/item action** and **September 20 minutes approval** retain their event labels; no legal defect or concealment is inferred from the dates alone.

## What the finding changes

**BH-M01 is complete to its bounded reading scope:** the source is accessible, matches its acquisition hash, and the pertinent motion, item, vote and approval block have been inspected. **BH-M02 advances partially:** we now have recorded adoption and a resolution number. The actual signed resolution, certification, approved attachments and version history remain unacquired.

Recorded ratification is evidence against a blanket assertion that this transfer had no documented council authorization. It does not prove that all preservation decisions were sound, every condition was implemented, or other transfers were properly authorized. Agenda, minutes, agreement and copied order are related chain records, not independent measurements of the steel's origin or condition.

Nothing read here joins the municipal artifact to **W14A/W14B, C12a/b, FEMA Sample 1**, a retained NIST specimen, a particular tower or WTC 7. Nor does it establish dispatch, delivery, condition, later alteration, retained coupons or a completed test. The earlier copied court-order testing provision remains separate from evidence of its implementation or present legal enforceability. It is not treated as a testing right for every public requester.

## Missing records remain TODOs

1. **BH-M02 — signed decision:** obtain resolution **11-R-12833**, clerk certification, Book of Resolutions entry, approved attachments and amendment/version records. Compare them with both preserved copies without overwriting either.
2. **BH-M03 — physical chain:** obtain the I-0137 application/inventory record, dispatch and bill of lading, recipient receipt, condition/installation/maintenance/alteration records, and later custody or disposition. Future-display wording is not receipt evidence.
3. **BH-M04 — testing implementation:** obtain item-specific testing notices, responses, examination and sample-retention records, and later modifications to the copied order. A written condition is not a completed test.
4. **STL02/STL03 — scientific identification:** obtain native inventories and marking, cutting, coupon, intake and transfer histories, with the actual building-attribution rationale. Generic shape or an artifact number does not establish the missing join.
5. **Other original criteria:** preserve STL01/STL04 preservation/contractor-review dependencies and the FAA, telephone, recorder and materials TODOs. This municipal record does not complete any of the original eighteen substantive acceptance criteria across the five tracks.

No outreach, new request, fee, filing, physical handling or experimental test occurred. No inference about historical collapse mechanism, conspiracy, motive or NIST's scientific credibility is drawn from this vote. The strongest limit is that an administrative approval record cannot substitute for the incomplete physical and scientific chain.

## Provenance and actual reading scope

| Source | Acquisition time, UTC | Preserved bytes / pages | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Official council minutes | November 3, 2026, 03:15:05.626912; saved 03:15:06.496626 | **1,019,036 / 11** | `207802196bb1c2df80ffed5b0275e22dc58767a1c7d4df458c97b1b4bd365dba` |
| Earlier agenda packet | October 27, 2026, 22:06:12.038318 | **369,384 / 12** | `140f527241183d46aba69b89533e115ef7a06d15b7a3c17f7d0a7355066a0b3a` |

The restored minute source was rechecked against its acquisition hash before reading. The former share outage was an access condition, not evidence of historical deletion or withholding. Different page counts describe two different documents; they do not demonstrate missing pages.

All eleven minute pages were inspected **as navigation thumbnails**. Only **physical pages 1, 5, 6, 7, 10 and 11** received the declared full-face inspection at 160 dpi. The earlier agenda's pages **4–5** were re-inspected at 160 dpi; the earlier twelve-page review was not repeated as a new acquisition. Unrelated comments/contracts and gallery photographs are outside the adopted findings. Ordinary text extraction yielded no useful target text. No new OCR, audio transcription, handwriting authentication, native-file recovery or independent/blinded reading was performed.

The publication includes [source pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-9507c45375858a95c1c5), [observations](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ca693c7ef3ea5718f462), [source-join checks](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d62c903f42c10b3681d1), [reading scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0f05f4903900c6d36be2), [open TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-491c1713e677a762949f), [exact locators](CITATIONS.md#nov04-57619e547ac8ac40e61b), [request checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0ab8f70d625b10b638bf), [verification scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-de86b2e6c93c5f24adb7), [bundle hashes](CITATIONS.md#nov04-27aa3678a11947b69684) and the additive [text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-87420360905ac473f1a1). # Resolution 11-R-12833: signed first page located; certification and exhibits remain unverified

**November 3, 2026.** The city’s official records search returned one exact resolution record for **11-R-12833**, dated **09-08-2011**. Its viewer identifies **application 115 / document 24099** and labels the record as nine pages. The complete first page was preserved as the actual JPEG response served by that viewer and read directly. It bears the resolution number, **“Adopted: September 8, 2011”**, and a signature image above **Barry Brucker, Mayor**. This advances the earlier minutes-only approval trail. It is not acquisition of the entire nine-page record, the original signed paper or a native signed PDF.

Research identifiers: **BH-M02; STL01–STL04; AUD011, AUD016; B188, H005; T01–T07.** Earlier research and source copies remain unchanged. Missing certification, approved attachment contents and physical-chain records remain TODOs.

## Where the record was found

The official [Agreements, Ordinances, Resolutions page](https://beverlyhills.org/161/Agreements-Ordinances-Resolutions) links both the [city search form](https://apps.beverlyhills.org/cityclerk/searchagreementsordinancesresolutions/) and AppEnhancer. The [exact-number search result](https://apps.beverlyhills.org/cityclerk/searchagreementsordinancesresolutions/web.jsp?SEARCHTYPE=ALL&DOCQUERY=&QUERYTYPE=AND&VENDOR=&NUMBER=11-R-12833&DATE=&TYPE=&action=Search) displayed **one record**, type **RESOLUTION**, number **11-R-12833**, date **09-08-2011**. No alternate portal query was needed.

The result's link opened the official [resolution viewer query](https://aedocs.beverlyhills.org/AppEnhancer/datasources/AX-COBH_City/ISubmitQuery?DocNavBlocked=True&DispMode=11&AppName=CITY-RESOLUTIONS_ORDINANCES_AGREEMENTS&ViewMode=ResultSet&SearchAllRevisions=True&PublicQuery=False&FulltextQuery=&FulltextQueryType=And&FulltextOperation=AND&FulltextThesaurus=False&DATE=09-08-2011&DOC%20TYPE=RESOLUTION&NUMBER%20(ORD%2FAGR%2FRESO)=11-R-12833), which displayed document **24099**, page **1 / 9**. The stable search/query locators are retained; temporary query-result and export-job identifiers are excluded from the public payload. A nine-page viewer label is not verification that all nine pages were acquired or read.

## What the complete first page establishes at the document level

| Source location: viewer page 1, printed page -1- | Directly observed content | What remains unestablished |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Heading and title | **RESOLUTION NO. 11-R-12833**; ratification of the Port Authority agreement for a WTC steel artifact and the Beverly Hills 9/11 Memorial Garden. | Native authoring history, signature authenticity and completeness of the full archived record. |
| Section 1 | Reports a Fire Department application to receive an artifact for display at the city memorial location. | The application itself, dispatch, delivery and receipt. |
| Section 2 | Reports approval of the request in **2009**, describes court permission to transfer legal ownership of steel identified as **I-0137**, and says a copy of the court order and letter agreement are attached as **Exhibit A**. | Independent verification of the reported 2009 acts and the actual contents, completeness, execution or version of those attachments in this archive record. A statement that papers are attached is not inspection of the papers. |
| Section 3 | Ratifies the letter agreement described as executed by the Fire Department on the city’s behalf. | The actual executed agreement, its signature history and physical performance. |
| Section 4 | Records acceptance of the artifact transfer on the city’s behalf. | Shipment/arrival date, condition, custody and physical specimen identification. |
| Section 5 | Directs the clerk to certify adoption and cause the resolution and certification to be entered in the **Book of Resolutions**. | A direction to certify is not the certification or proof that the separate book entry was made. |
| Bottom adoption/signature block | **Adopted: September 8, 2011**; mayoral signature image above the printed name **Barry Brucker** and mayor title. | No forensic handwriting analysis, original-paper inspection, cryptographic signature validation or signature-date authentication was performed. |
| Footer | Printed **-1-** and `B0785-0001\1387886v1.doc`. | The printed filename is a source locator, not possession or authentication of that native Word file. |

The first page contains Sections 1–5 and the adoption/signature block; it was read as a complete face, not inferred from the initially visible upper portion. Other pages' thumbnails and page-count labels are not adopted as substantive evidence about certification, signatures or exhibit contents.

## Preserve the earlier fields alongside the new ones

The earlier [agenda packet](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/MetaViewer.php?clip_id=2444&meta_id=136233&view_id=11), physical pages 4–5, had a proposed-resolution heading ending **11-R-**, a blank **Adopted:** field and blank mayor/clerk adoption-attestation lines. Separate approval-as-to-form and Fire Chief marks were visible; it was not wholly unsigned. Those observations and original bytes remain unchanged.

The later [minutes](https://beverlyhills.granicus.com/DocumentViewer.php?file=beverlyhills_e8dee5d0768d9bb4bc2d443199135f32.pdf&view=1), physical page 6, record **F-7 adopted as 11-R-12833**. Their consent-calendar vote is on page 10; the minutes record their own approval on **September 20, 2011** on page 11. The [preceding published review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/4ac0f984339daabcf92b2ffe71c046d0d3d0dff5/investigation/broader-evidence/bh-steel-ratification-minutes-2026-11-03/README.md) preserves the motion, exceptions, separate F-8 vote and reading limits.

The newly held resolution face instead prints the full number, **September 8 adoption**, and the mayoral signature image. Keep all three records and their labels. No earlier blank is completed, source replaced or cause of a difference adjudicated. September 8 resolution adoption and September 20 approval of the minutes retain their different event roles. No legal defect, concealment, backdating or substitution is inferred from those dates or fields.

The agenda is a **12-page PDF**, the minutes an **11-page PDF**, and the new archive viewer labels its record **nine pages**. They are different record representations. This comparison does not establish missing, removed or additional attachment pages. No complete page-by-page or byte-identity comparison of the nine-page record has been performed. The earlier visual **I-0137** versus extracted **1-0137** caution remains preserved; the new first page visually reads **I-0137**, without adjudicating the earlier extraction conflict.

## Acquisition limits and the access boundary

The viewer’s all-pages PDF export did not produce a captured download. One attempt to save its displayed completed-export link also timed out. A single unauthenticated retrieval of that exact save link returned **HTTP 403**. During subsequent visible page navigation, an access check returned **403** with a login redirect. No credentials were entered, denied route retried after that boundary, security flag changed or authentication bypass attempted.

The already served **HTTP 200 image/jpeg** response for document **24099 / page 1 / version 1** was preserved from the browser’s incoming response buffer. This did not issue a new request to the denied export or lock endpoints. The image contains the full first page at **1700 × 2200 pixels**, **464,622 bytes**, SHA-256 **`2c703a9c32432650f1593d9d93e6d72d5a398c66d99c7cf5e199358639c77c6d`**. It is a rendered archive image, not an acquired PDF or authenticated native scan master. The hash identifies those saved response bytes, not the truth of every statement in them.

The official navigation and exact-number index HTML entities were also preserved with URLs, acquisition UTC, byte counts and hashes. Their original-source line/block locators and the report’s own line references are recorded separately. No invented OCR lines are assigned to the image. The raw entities, signature image, response internals and temporary acquisition details remain outside the public payload.

Only the complete first resolution face was substantively read in this batch. Pages **2–9** are **unread/unacquired as full faces**. The agenda and minute comparisons reuse the preceding preserved readings and hashes; no fresh complete reading of those PDFs or their attachments is claimed. No new OCR, audio transcription, native-file recovery, independent/blinded review, steel handling or physical test occurred. Temporary browser layout and diagnostic settings were restored.

## TODOs and next discriminating records

1. **BH-M02-CERT — clerk certification:** obtain and read the actual attestation/certification face or separate certification record. Preserve its wording, signature/seal observations, date, vote and source relationship. Section 5 alone does not satisfy this.
2. **BH-M02-ATT — approved attachments:** obtain all remaining full faces of document 24099 or an approved complete export, identify the actual Exhibit A boundary, and compare the letter agreement/court order with the preserved agenda copies. Check signatures, object identifiers, conditions, schedules and amendments; retain any differences. No claim about these unread pages’ contents is made now.
3. **BH-M02-BOOK/VERSION — book entry and source versions:** obtain the Book of Resolutions entry, complete approved record, posting/version history and any native or certified copy that the records holder lawfully makes available. The current capture is one rendered page.
4. **BH-M03 — physical chain:** application/inventory, dispatch/bill of lading, receipt, condition, installation/alteration and subsequent custody/disposition remain missing.
5. **BH-M04 — testing:** actual testing notices, responses, examinations, retained samples and later order modifications remain missing. Neither a referenced attachment nor authorization language is evidence that testing occurred.

This is a **partial BH-M02 advance**, not completion of that acquisition task or any of the original eighteen substantive criteria across the five tracks. A further official record bearing a signature is evidence against a blanket claim of no recorded approval, but related agenda/minutes/resolution statements are not independent scientific corroboration. The paper trail does not identify a NIST/FEMA specimen, establish a particular building of origin, validate preservation adequacy or show a conspiracy, motive or historical collapse mechanism.

[Source pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c309938407e235ab930f), [observations](CITATIONS.md#nov04-994aa3b9fab7f58da7e4), [preserved fields](CITATIONS.md#nov04-783cc7b6543c46e4126e), [reading scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-77d61db824dabd52b232), [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-997b845b5d2af8e8b684), [locators](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d6edb99491cfd65fffdb), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e473200484f90abbcbb5), [verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-77c66df09c96bc80cdfb), [bundle hashes](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7893f22fc25be2bce1e5) and the additive [revision diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-4f25e4ac1a0f66b410e4) accompany the redundant public research copy. Publication verification is separate from source authentication and substantive acceptance. No outreach, new request, fee, filing, private-repository push, overwrite or deletion occurred.

### FAA collection certification and reproduction records: what was asked, copied and actually evidenced

# FAA collection certification and reproduction records: what was asked, copied and actually evidenced

November 1, 2026. B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04. Three formerly catalog-only FAA records were acquired from their actual NARA download links and read in full. They expose specific collection instructions, reported production quantities and a historical procedure excerpt. **They do not establish complete collection, successful delivery, suppressed speech or a cover-up.** Earlier sources and conflicting descriptions remain unchanged.

## Sources and actual scope

| Official released scan | Access and response entity | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [A90-74](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf) | 2026-11-01T20:46:32.019719+00:00; HTTP 200; 917,957 bytes; 8 pages | `a76efe92ee6d4c6932f92b46ae3cc35c3ce59926076439401886d146258b328b` |
| [A90-98](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-98-Email-on-9-11-Commission-Data-Collection.pdf) | 2026-11-01T20:46:32.025903+00:00; HTTP 200; 24,765 bytes; 1 pages | `3a8fc6ee8fc351cbcd32ea4e66da81b37b52737fe720c52fe320528e1f6c8b42` |
| [A90-99](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/5/9115/34911505/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-99-Reproducing-Documentation-Info-REDACT-SEALED.pdf) | 2026-11-01T20:46:32.025938+00:00; HTTP 200; 1,427,431 bytes; 6 pages | `315028f4a0b1f89a2b705c99aa857188b0d49a0de25cd38d8691411d8497d28f` |

Physical page references below are one-based. All **15** new page faces were rendered at 2600 pixels and inspected. [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b703d8d8c2c00c0f2580) pins original response entities, catalog snapshots, reading rasters and reused context. Raw PDFs and images remain preserved separately; this public derivative excludes personal names, signatures, contact details, individual pay data and redacted content. These are official released scans, not independently authenticated native emails or original signatures. A hash identifies examined bytes, not historical truth.

## Certification concerns asking employees, not certifying complete production

[A90-74 physical page 2](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=2), dated **October 15, 2003**, goes from Manager, Boston TRACON, A90-1 to Director of Air Traffic, AAT-1. It certifies that employees who were at the facility on September 11 had been asked for relevant documentation. The examples include government records, emails, radar/audiotapes and personal notes.

That is affirmative evidence of a reported collection inquiry. The actual sentence does **not** certify that every employee answered, that each answer was accurate, that all responsive records were received, or that the Commission received every item. Treating it as an independently verified completeness declaration would overstate it. Conversely, its limited wording is not evidence that someone refused or concealed records.

[Physical pages 3–4](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=3) attach a sign-on log with a printed window of **9/10/01 10:00:00 PM–9/11/01 10:00:00 PM** and headers **9/12/01 7:27:58 AM** and **7:28:00 AM**. [Physical pages 5–6](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=5) contain two presentations of questions covering records received/generated from **September 11 at midnight through 11:59 pm**. These are differently described scopes and clocks; do not silently convert one into the other, identify header meaning without system records, or declare the roster incomplete. The source of each list, timezone, scope instructions and response crosswalk remain needed.

The question sheet distinguishes **government records**, which employees are described as **required** to provide, from **personal notes**, which they are **strongly encouraged** to provide. The deadline blanks are unfilled in the inspected form. That distinction is a concrete completeness question: ask what records were classified in each category and what responses were retained. It is not proof personal notes existed or were intentionally excluded. The manager's certification includes personal notes among the requested material.

[Physical page 8](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=8) is a staffing sheet with handwritten marks, including no/none entries and checks. Its annotation key, question, authorship and row-to-response meaning are not established here. Do not turn those marks into a response percentage or infer an employee had no relevant knowledge. Names and signatures are not reproduced in this derivative.

The fax cover on [physical page 1](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/99/9114/34911499/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-74-Facility-Certification-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=1) is dated **10/15/03** and states **four pages including the cover**. The released PDF has **eight physical pages**, including alternate/enlarged presentations of signed material and a staffing sheet; page 5 expressly announces an alternate presentation on the following page. Preserve both counts. The current eight-page archive object is not necessarily the original four-page fax job. No machine transmission report, exact original enclosure map or recipient acknowledgment was acquired. This difference alone establishes neither a missing historical enclosure nor four additional records sent.

## The collection email separates cover documents from data and tracks staff time

[A90-98 page 1](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-98-Email-on-9-11-Commission-Data-Collection.pdf#page=1) is a printed email dated **10/10/2003 11:26 AM**, subject **Tapes, Memos, Certification Letters   Track time**. It asks for copies of the memo, index or letter as data are sent onward, and directs certifications to **AAT-20**, referring to a teleconference.

Preserve the exact sentence **“I/we do not what the data.”** It is printed that way. Reading what as want is plausible, but it has not been authenticated as an intended correction. The surrounding request clearly asks for copies of cover documents. That makes a separate administrative-document trail a useful retrieval target; it does not prove the sender prohibited preservation of data or that data were not delivered to another recipient.

The final paragraph requests weekly accounting of **time expended at each facility**, with an early-next-week/midday-Wednesday reporting reference. This is personnel-effort tracking, not a timeline of recorded September 11 events. The scan has no native mail headers, actual attachment identifiers or item-level delivery acknowledgment. Do not use its printed sent label alone to authenticate receipt or identify an entire downstream packet.

## Production report: 30 reported staff hours, about 1,000 pages and two tapes

[A90-99 physical pages 1–2](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/5/9115/34911505/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-99-Reproducing-Documentation-Info-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=1) show two presentations of an email dated **10/10/2003 01:58 PM**, subject **Time**. Page 1 includes overlaid handwritten work notes; its presentation notice points to page 2. The typed report and repeated clean presentation are one underlying account, not two independent witnesses.

| Printed category | Stated people | Printed hours |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| CPC | 4 | 12 |
| MSS-1 | 2 | 8 |
| MSS-3 | 1 | 7 |
| MSS-4 | 1 | 3 |

The four displayed hour figures sum to **30**, matching the stated total. That is an arithmetic check of the printed report, not verification of payroll, actual effort or production completeness. The text reports **approximately 1,000 pages** and **two tapes of rerecordings**. Neither the approximate page count nor the labor hours measures the duration or completeness of audio.

A postscript separately describes one MSS-1 spending **six hours** on today's exercise. Its inclusion in, or addition to, the stated 30 is not explicit. Preserve both; do not report a verified 36-hour total. The handwritten work/rate notes on pages 1 and 3 remain preserved in the source, but are not transcribed here or used to reconstruct individual compensation.

### Two tapes versus the separate landline attachment list

The [earlier preserved A90-71 memorandum](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/665d6502ab844e1eb15e83b6403205a6f896936a/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-a90-landline-delivery-2026-11-01/README.md) names **TMU: one tape; OSIC: two tapes; OS: no visible numeral**. Its two explicit counts already sum to three, plus an unspecified OS count; singular tape makes four overall plausible, still an inference. A90-99's typed report says two.

Keep these quantities and their source locations side by side. The reports do not identify the same output set through barcodes, enclosure lists, channels, dates of production or copy-generation records. Same-day proximity is insufficient to join them. Different batches, scopes or reporting cutoffs are untested possibilities; so is a reporting error. We cannot infer that two tapes disappeared, choose a controlling number, or call the mismatch resolved. Native manifests, preparation logs and dispatch/receipt records would discriminate.

## Historical procedure excerpt supplies specific preservation and copying targets

[A90-99 physical pages 4–6](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialive/5/9115/34911505/content/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-e-other/5-A90-99-Reproducing-Documentation-Info-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=4) include an FAA **7210.3R** extract, with visible date **2/24/00**, section 8 and printed pages **4-8-1/4-8-2**. Page 4 has an overlaid preparation/review note and a presentation notice; page 5 is the alternate, unobscured presentation; page 6 continues the text. Both presentations stay preserved. No hidden text or redaction was recovered.

| Exact provision | What the excerpt describes | Limit on applying it here |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 4-8-1(a), page 5, upper left | Direct copying from an original with prior **ATD and AAT-20 approval**; qualified FAA employee retains custody and attends copying; continuous recording, minimizing original starts/stops to the maximum extent possible | Obtain the applicable version, approvals, request, copying method and original/copy identifiers before asserting that this provision governed a particular Commission derivative |
| 4-8-1(b), page 5, left middle | Certified rerecording of portions copied; facility retention under applicable criteria; original returned to service | A general provision is not a source-specific disposition record or proof any September 11 original was overwritten |
| 4-8-2, pages 5–6 | Preservation requests trigger removal from service and a warning label; written 20-day notice and a conditional additional 10-day notice before the described return-to-service outcome | Requires actual request, notices, approval/release, other holds and applicable accident-retention criteria; not an automatic 30-day destruction rule |
| 4-8-3, page 5, upper right | Printed reductions of computer data carry a disclaimer distinguishing internal processing from the exact control-position display | Need native data, configuration, processing and display reconstruction; a printed table alone cannot authenticate an exact controller-screen reproduction |
| 4-8-4, pages 5–6 | Separate copy/media/computer-cost provisions | Historical examples are not a current fee quote, Commission invoice or authority to incur fees |

The computer-data disclaimer reads: **“This document is derived from computer magnetic recordings of internal computer processing. It is not an exact representation of the control position display.”** The excerpt therefore supplies a specific reason to keep native data, processed output and a reconstructed display distinct. It does not establish an actual display error or deceptive reconstruction.

### Continuous direct copies beside disclosed skip-silence processing

The direct-copy provision's continuity language belongs beside A90-71's explicit disclosure that skip silence shortened the landline rerecordings. Those statements raise a **specific applicability and copy-generation question**, not an established violation. The rule text has a specified direct-request copying context; A90-71 reports a Commission-related output. The full applicable order and amendments, special instructions, approvals and identities of source/output copies remain unjoined. Do not erase either statement or assume the provision either necessarily applied or necessarily did not apply.

The strongest objection to an adverse reading is that a continuous preserved original and a shortened listening copy could serve different purposes under different authorized instructions. That remains untested; the excerpt does not prove such an arrangement existed. Obtain both generations and their instructions, compare surviving speech and time anchors, and report actual differences. No native audio comparison was possible or performed in this unit.

## What advanced and what remains open

The new records improve the collection/administrative and copying-policy map. None is the actual corrected **May 2003 QFR**, its delivery receipt, a signed action/no-action determination, or the later OIG recommendation response/implementation/closure. The two recommendations on report page 12 remain distinct from those continuing on page 13. Those FAA outcomes remain TODOs rather than being replaced by these 2003 records.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5f55331972c356eadb9d) lists the original employee-response crosswalk, instructions/teleconference record, native email, output manifests, approval/custody/hold/notices and original-versus-rerecording tests. [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-11cdbeb5d57314b38f05) keeps source wording, quantity differences and limitations explicit. All five work-plan tracks and eighteen substantive criteria remain open or partial. Existing Keyton/zero-duration mirrors remain acquired/inspected; canonical provenance, call/routing joins and native records remain open. Previously performed recorder tests retain their performed status. No duplicate recorder/materials experiment or independent-audit adoption is claimed.

[verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-902564a2764e83334319) reproduces the declared source-byte checks, page/count distinctions and printed-hour sum. It does not verify actual production, speech retention, historical clocks, collection completeness, delivery, procedural compliance or historical intent. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-388b01aab96d9ff372aa) and [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-64e2ea401b8e51716fbe) preserve accountability and the exact new text. Original scans, alternate presentations, previous reports and conflicting observations remain unchanged. No outreach, request, fee, mailbox/private-case use, filing, deletion, private push or history rewrite occurred.

### A90 landline rerecordings: disclosed silence removal, differing time scopes and an index without receipts

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 2f856f7d2721b703074c480bb6c603658873be00af0ba439408035ba3a442f24 -->
# A90 landline rerecordings: disclosed silence removal, differing time scopes and an index without receipts

November 1, 2026. Existing B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04. Two previously catalog-only public records were acquired and read in full. This advances the source/copy and delivery-record inquiry; native audio, correction/receipt and later administrative outcomes remain open.

**The October 10, 2003 memorandum explicitly reports using “skip silence” to shorten rerecordings.** Its opening paragraph describes `0800–1200 local`, while the certification describes source data received `1200–1605 UTC`. Those stated intervals span four hours and four hours five minutes. Preserve both descriptions and their distinct labels. No actual audio gap, suppressed speech, elapsed-clock error or amount of removed material has been measured.

The associated four-page index supplies useful tab names. **It does not supply a delivery acknowledgment or identify each recording.** Its AAL11 and UAL175 tab letters differ, so joining records by letter alone would produce wrong associations. Repeated content labels also must not be silently deduplicated or counted as independent corroboration.

## Sources and reading scope

| Official released source | Acquisition, entity and SHA-256 | Actually read |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [A90-71 / NARA7599620](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-71-Memo-with-Re-Recordings.pdf) | 2026-11-01T20:30:50.234004+00:00; HTTP200; 40,254 bytes; `1a744e43b7a36e21cf01cd572acb18c73cc89a133fc5357604da0393fcdf1a66` | All1physical page, including scope, processing/certification and attachment footer |
| [A90-87 / NARA7599635](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-87-Index-of-Data-Provided-9-11-Commission.pdf) | 2026-11-01T20:30:50.237343+00:00; HTTP200; 101,936 bytes; `16c09e7d0b3d404d362d047e95c82773cf2dcc1aacedc6b19b53046ac602b1e9` | All4physical pages; all90visible table rows transcribed |

Download URLs came from the actual public catalog links. Each response entity and acquisition receipt is preserved unchanged, separately from research/public derivatives. These are official released scans, not independently authenticated native originals or signature/custody chains. The hash pins the bytes examined; it does not establish historical truth.

Five whole-page 2600-pixel images were read. Physical page numbers below are one-based. A90-87 physical1–2 are printed1of2/2of2; physical3 and4 are separate flight indexes each printed1of1. [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-77426c12e887f8371e0d) records the exact inputs. [INDEX-ROWS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1a6d2428b40c7b485d77) preserves all row labels and duplicates; only layout whitespace is simplified. No new OCR, hidden-layer extraction, redaction recovery, audio acquisition/listening/decoding, source editing or native clock fit occurred. Prior [A90-67/report context](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/73cccf17d825861ba62161cd631454446bed550c/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-a90-original-copy-trail-2026-11-01/README.md) is reused, not a new acquisition or independent corroboration.

## What A90-71 actually reports

[A90-71 physical1](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-71-Memo-with-Re-Recordings.pdf#page=1), header and four body/footer locations:

| Location | Preserved assertion | What remains unverified |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Header/from/to | October10,2003; Manager,BostonTRACON,A90-1 → Manager,Evaluations and Investigation Staff,AAT-20 | Actual dispatch and recipient acknowledgment |
| Opening scope paragraph | Certified rerecordings attached from all landlines in TMU, OSIC and OS positions, September11,2001, `0800 to1200 local` | Actual channels, contents, enclosure completeness and which local-clock convention applies |
| Processing paragraph | “skip silence” was used; the memo describes skipping periods where nothing was said and compressing tape length | Actual settings, thresholds, time announcements and whether every relevant utterance survived processing |
| Certification paragraph | Data derived from Digital Voice Recording System data received September11,2001, `1200 UTC`–`1605 UTC` | Native export identity, source/date/timecode/coverage/correction history |
| Attachments footer | `TMU–1 tape`; `OSIC–2 tapes`; `OS–tape`, with no numeral visible for OS | A complete numeric attachment total and actual items received |

The two explicit numerals sum to **three**, plus an OS line with no visible numeric count. That is **not a verified three-tape total**. The singular word tape makes one OS tape, and therefore four overall, a plausible reading; it remains an inference rather than an explicit complete numeric manifest or proof of receipt. Three named position categories are not necessarily three channels. The one-page PDF contains the memorandum; no audio enclosure was acquired in this batch. That does not prove an enclosure was missing from the actual dispatch or from another released item.

The disclosed processing matters for reconstruction: playback elapsed time of a silence-shortened derivative cannot simply be treated as elapsed historical wall-clock time. That methodological implication is conditional on identifying an actual file as a descendant of these rerecordings. We have not identified or tested such a file here. The memo's explanation is affirmative contrary evidence to treating every shortened pause as covert splicing; it also does not independently prove that only silence was removed or that every utterance was preserved.

**Required discriminating records/tests:** continuous original/channel exports, each certified-copy identifier, preparation/export logs and silence settings, retained time announcements/timecode, custody and actual enclosures. A later comparison should map each surviving speech interval to the original, report skipped intervals and uncertainty, and anchor clocks independently. No such experiment has been performed in this unit.

## Time descriptions: preserve the five-minute span difference

Both descriptions refer to September11,2001, but the text labels their clocks and scopes differently:

| Description | Printed endpoints and clock label | Arithmetic within that description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Opening paragraph's attached-copy scope | `0800`–`1200`, **local** | 240 minutes |
| Certification's source-data-received scope | `1200`–`1605`, **UTC** | 245 minutes |

The difference between those stated spans is **five minutes**. No timezone assumption is needed to subtract endpoints within each same-day description. If one hypothesizes that both pairs describe exactly the same boundaries under one constant clock offset, the starts require a numeric offset of240minutes and the ends245minutes. That conditional hypothesis does not make both endpoint pairs exactly equal. It does **not** measure clock drift, a five-minute gap, the silence-removal amount, or a concealed interval.

Untested possibilities include a nominal requested window versus a longer actual source-data boundary, extra lead-in/out, drafting error, or genuinely different described coverage. None is adopted. Native copy/export records, contemporaneous instructions and the recordings could distinguish them. Do not relabel local as a specific timezone, substitute a preferred endpoint, normalize1605to1600, or apply an offset to other evidence.

## Comparison with October8A90-67

[The held October8memorandum](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-A90-67-Memo-Certifying-Re-recording.pdf#page=1) reports a certified ID/SL rerecording for `1209–1228 UTC`; broader DATs held at BostonTRACON but then unreproducible due to equipment problems; and a certification based on `DVRS` data received. October10A90-71 names **TMU/OSIC/OS landlines**, a different scope, and spells out **Digital Voice Recording System** as its source.

These are two dated source accounts. Same facility, two-day proximity, similar signatures or the shared acronym do not prove that the same original was used, the DAT problem was repaired, or the October8statement was false. Conversely, the later reported ability to make some rerecordings cannot establish that all source media were readable. Both assertions and their distinctions remain; copy-generation history, equipment records and native identifiers are missing. The earlier source and published assessment remain unchanged.

## What the four-page A90-87 index supplies

[Physical1–2](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-87-Index-of-Data-Provided-9-11-Commission.pdf#page=1): BostonTRACON(A90), numbered **TAB1–65**, with65visible rows. Physical1has1–36;physical2has37–65. It includes advisories, directives, GENOT/NOTAM entries, logs, procedures, security, press and stress-related material. These are index labels; no underlying item contents were acquired from a title alone.

[Physical3](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-87-Index-of-Data-Provided-9-11-Commission.pdf#page=3): BostonTRACON AccidentPackage–**AAL11**, **A–M**,13category rows. [Physical4](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/5-Miscellaneous/e-Other/5-A90-87-Index-of-Data-Provided-9-11-Commission.pdf#page=4): BostonTRACON **UAL175**, **A–L**,12category rows. This totals **90index rows across three scopes**, not90documents, tapes, pages, independent pieces of evidence or successfully delivered items. TABnumbers are a package-organizing namespace, not the NARA folder suffix in A90-87.

| Category or letter | AAL11 physical3 | UAL175 physical4 | Join implication |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H | ATCSCC East Area Log | FOIA Freedom of Information Act | Same letter has different content |
| I | Fax Transmission Verification Reports | Headquarters Guidance and Communications | Same letter has different content |
| Headquarters Guidance and Communications | J | I | Same category has different letters |
| Flight Progress Strips | K | J | Same category has different letters |
| Certified Re-recordings | L | K | Same category has different letters |
| RADAR Data | M | L | Same category has different letters |

The two flight indexes share11literal category labels. AAL11 additionally lists ATCSCCEastAreaLog and FaxTransmissionVerificationReports; UAL175 additionally lists FOIAFreedomofInformationAct. That is a bounded difference in index descriptions, not proof one investigation lacked other records or one packet was defective. A listed fax-verification category is a concrete retrieval lead, not itself a retrieved transmission report or proof of these particular audio deliveries.

Eight content labels recur within the65numbered rows, which have55distinct literal labels after layout-space transcription. Examples: `Genot1/36` at10/13, `NOTAM1/0989` at33/35/36/39, and `ORDER7110.65M` at44/46. Keep each row and tab. The same title could refer to copies, acknowledgments, distinct versions or different enclosures; those are untested possibilities. No item bytes have been joined or deduplicated. Source spelling such as `Procudures General` at57 remains in the row data; it is not a substantive suspicious finding.

**What the index does not prove:** the four pages have no signed recipient acknowledgment, delivery date, tape barcode/serial/hash, copy-to-original crosswalk, per-recording channel/time list or enclosure receipt. Its catalog title says data provided, but a catalog description cannot independently certify each item was sent and received. Nor does the absence of those fields on an index establish that proof of delivery never existed. This source improves package navigation while leaving the receipt and native-content tests open.

## Outstanding inputs and publication limits

Original sources and prior publications remain unchanged. No outreach, fee, request, mailbox/private-case use, litigation edit, deletion, private push or history rewrite occurred.

### A90 source recordings, rerecordings and a reported reproduction barrier

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY f7f1936acbf501579170f31d42edf9791db4f086ffa2c3b9c27a7ce335838565 -->
# A90 source recordings, rerecordings and a reported reproduction barrier

November 1, 2026. Existing B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04 operational-record trail. This unit acquired and read a previously unacquired public memorandum and inspected adjacent held certifications/logs. It does not complete native recording authentication or the outstanding May 2003 correction and administrative-disposition investigation.

**New primary record:** an October 8, 2003 Boston TRACON memorandum reports an attached certified rerecording for two positions over `1209 UTC`–`1228 UTC` on September 11, 2001. It separately reports broader Digital Audio Tape recordings held in the accident file, then unreproducible because of equipment problems. A final certification describes the limited data as derived from `DVRS` data received by the facility. These assertions must be retained together; the underlying recordings, copy-generation history, equipment records and delivery proof have not been acquired here.

This gives a specific original-versus-copy retrieval target. It does **not** establish that the DATs were destroyed, permanently unreadable, suppressed, or inconsistent with the certified copy. It also does not prove all relevant channels were delivered. Both an available certified derivative and a reported source-media access limitation appear in the same record.

## Sources, acquisition and actual scope

**New source — [A90-67, NARA7598345](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-A90-67-Memo-Certifying-Re-recording.pdf).** Catalog item: [official entry](https://catalog.archives.gov/id/7598345). Acquired November 1, 2026 at 2026-11-01T20:15:20.135005+00:00, HTTP 200/application-pdf, **36,088 bytes**, one physical page. SHA-256: `ce72cce78d5aff847284f9df4761b62dd33e6dbb5c0b27d8385d934e3aac68e5`. The URL was taken from the rendered catalog download link, not guessed. The HTTP entity and its retrieval receipt remain preserved; this is not an original on-wire capture or native-media authentication. The catalog interface displayed `14 KB`, while the acquired entity is `36,088` bytes. These source-layer observations are retained; no earlier PDF entity/hash is available here to test versions or substitution.

**Held source — [AEA1130, NARA7598367](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf).** Acquired October 30, 2026; 15,505,493 bytes, 237 physical pages, unchanged SHA-256 `5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179`. This unit visually inspected physical **9–23**: **14 previously unread pages plus a recheck of 16**. It also read the new A90-67 one-page entity. Whole-page renders and a higher-resolution index recheck were used. No hidden PDF extraction, OCR, redaction recovery, audio acquisition/listening/decoding or physical-clock fitting occurred. Printed page numbers and dates differ by contributing facility; physical PDF page numbers below are one-based.

The [NARA finding aid](https://www.archives.gov/research/foia/faa-finding-aid) led to the new memorandum and five further item-level targets. Those five target PDFs remain **unacquired and unread**. A direct attempt to preserve the original finding-aid HTML returned HTTP 502; six relevant rendered table rows were subsequently saved through the public browser. That browser derivative is not raw HTML. The web tool also could not open the catalog item, while the public browser and linked PDF retrieval worked. These access outcomes are tool/route observations, not evidence of removal or concealment.

[Source hashes and locators](CITATIONS.md#nov04-272a3fec691ac79648db) · [Selected observations](CITATIONS.md#nov04-10cc712152c5d4994dbc).

## The October 8, 2003 memorandum: what it actually certifies

[A90-67 physical 1](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-A90-67-Memo-Certifying-Re-recording.pdf#page=1), subject/from/to fields, three substantive passages and Attachments footer:

| Passage | Preserved source report | Qualification |
| --- | --- | --- |
| From/to and subject | Manager, Boston TRACON, A90-1 → Acting Manager, Air Traffic Division, ANE-500; ZNY-ARTCC-148 / UAL175 | Office routing and subject are visible. No transmission or recipient acknowledgment has been authenticated. |
| First paragraph | Certified rerecording attached for Initial Departure `(ID)` and Lincoln `(SL)`, September 11, `1209 UTC`–`1228 UTC` | The one-page PDF contains the memorandum, not an acquired audio attachment. This does not establish that audio was absent from the actual dispatch or another released item. |
| Second paragraph | Digital Audio Tape recordings from September 10, `2359Z`, through September 12, `0001Z`, contained in the accident file at Boston TRACON; equipment problems prevented reproduction at that time | This is a historical location/access assertion as of October 8, 2003, not present-day custody, media health or proof no other copy existed. |
| Certification paragraph | Limited data derived from the `DVRS` data received by the facility, September 11, `1209 UTC`–`1228 UTC` | Preserve `DVRS` separately from the paragraph's DAT terminology. The source-to-copy relationship, generation history and technical meanings are not independently established. |

Arithmetic on the stated endpoints gives a **19-minute certified interval** and a **24-hour, 2-minute DAT date/time span**. These are labelled spans, not measurements of audio duration, continuous coverage, channel count or a missing percentage. The broad span and narrow certified scope cannot be subtracted into a finding that the remaining speech was withheld. No actual recording was tested.

**Untested competing possibilities:** existing derivative availability despite an original-playback problem; different underlying media paths or copy-generation dates; or a broader unrecovered preservation/access failure. These are proposed explanations, not selected conclusions. The original DATs and identifiers, DVRS exports, channel/copy manifests, playback/repair/service logs and actual enclosures/receipts could distinguish them. The memorandum's express equipment explanation is material contrary evidence to assuming deliberate suppression; its explanation remains a source report requiring underlying support.

## Four facility indexes are distinct records

[AEA1130 physical 13–20](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=13) is the complete local Section 3 certification block, bounded by the divider at 13 and Section 4 divider at 21.

| Physical page | Facility and date | Certification scope and selected audio labels |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 14 | Boston ATCT / BOS, December 21, 2001 | Listed originals on file; `Original Voice Recording(s)` and `Certified Cassette Rerecording(s)` |
| 16 | Boston TRACON / A90, September 19, 2001 | Listed originals on file; `Original Voice Recording(s)` and `Certified Cassette Rerecording(s)` |
| 18 | Boston ARTCC / ZBW, September 21, 2001 | Listed originals on file; includes `Original Voice Recordings` and radar data |
| 20 | New York ARTCC / ZNY, September 11, 2001 | Certifies listed **copies** as true copies of originals on file; includes labels naming original recordings and specific data tapes |

An item called Original Voice Recordings inside a certified-copy list does not itself deliver or authenticate the original medium. Different offices, dates and certification scopes cannot be collapsed into a single current custodian or a shared media identity. All are historical assertions; signatures and today's holdings are not authenticated by this scan.

**Correction to our predecessor:** the [earlier public review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/ce9df2c6e1a6dcb11bf1a31578586d00440508ee/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-position-log-crosscheck-2026-11-01/README.md) called the final A90 list item `Certified Cassette Recording(s)`. The source actually says **`Certified Cassette Rerecording(s)`**. That was our transcription/abbreviation error, not a disagreement between source versions. The earlier public commit and source bytes remain unchanged. [The explicit addendum](CITATIONS.md#nov04-9066e306c2bc8dd0c329) preserves both readings and their exact locations. This report does not silently rewrite the predecessor.

## Named native-media and custody targets

[Physical 20, ZNY Certified Index](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=20), middle/lower list:

| Printed label | Identifier or stated range |
| --- | --- |
| Original SAR Tape | `NY1480-1491` |
| Original AMPP Tape | `NY2044` |
| Original Core Tape | `NY2116` |
| DSR Tape | `ZNY-0226, -0241, -0230`; September 11, `0621-2359 UTC` |
| DARC Tape | `ZNY-0400, -0319, -0402`; September 11, `0846-2359 UTC` |
| SATORI Digital Audio Tape `(DAT)` | No individual identifier or coverage range printed in that list item |
| Chain of Custody Documents | A record category is listed, without supplying the particular media/copy chain in this unit |

These identifiers make a more discriminating search/request target than a generic demand for all tapes. They do not prove present-day survival, actual contents, independent clocks or completeness. No acronym is expanded from memory, no SAR/AMPP/Core/SATORI/DSR/DARC medium is silently equated with the A90 DAT/DVRS material, and none is counted as an acquired native recording.

## Contrary and contextual records preserved beside the indexes

Normal Service Statements at physical **15 / Boston ATCT / September 20**, **17 / Boston TRACON / September 19**, and **19 / Boston ARTCC / September 25, 2001** state normal service and no pertinent transmissions. The same compilation contains operational chronologies at **9–12** describing communications, handoffs, warnings and aircraft reports. Preserve the wording beside those actual accounts. The procedural meaning and certification basis of pertinent require the applicable instructions and preparation records. Neither a global claim that no communications existed nor intentional misrepresentation follows from that word alone.

The [ZNY Daily Record, physical 22–23](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=22), provides another document representation. Selected Time/Remarks rows on 22 read:

| Printed time | Reported entry, summarized |
| --- | --- |
| `1238` | Informed of possible AAL11 hijack |
| `1250` | Informed of another possible hijack, UAL175 |
| `1250` | Advised an aircraft crashed into the World Trade Center |
| `1304` | Advised a second aircraft crashed into the World Trade Center |
| `1305` | ZNY declared ATC zero; international traffic stopped, domestic-destination traffic diverted to Canada, and qualifying oceanic overflight continued |

The source's header says `09/11/01 00:38`. No UTC label is visible on the selected log face; the header's role as creation/entry/checking time is unverified. Do not assign those semantics or convert clocks from expectation. The log's `1238` stays beside the prior statement's visible `1240` struck-through / `1238` adjacent values; its `1305` stays beside prior accounts of distinct `1305`/`1315` actions. Agreement is not independent corroboration where author, preparation and source dependence remain unverified. It is not a first-ever institutional-knowledge measurement or proof that a direction was fully executed. Both log pages were viewed; unrelated personnel/operational details are not republished.

Additional bounded observations are retained in OBSERVATIONS.json:

- Physical 11's printed chronology places `1255:52` before `1255:18`. That is an observable ordering issue; it is not a measured backwards clock or missing 34 seconds.
- Physical 12's continued personnel table uses `RA55` for the same visible identity appearing as `D55` in the previously inspected statement/report. Preserve the labels; native role definitions and actual duty scope remain unresolved.
- Physical 11 prints `DAL2483` in the `1254:16`, `1254:38`, `1255:18` and `1257:08` entries. This is an additional candidate for the prior [DAL2433 / DAL2437 lead](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6e846c1ea159fde25e1291c1c5046e70e2e4de80/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-notification-personnel-originals-2026-11-01/README.md). Those earlier values remain inherited context, not a new same-flight/event join or a preferred identity selected here.
- Physical 10 says ZNY accepted a handoff at `1237`; physical 9 says ZBW completed a handoff and transferred frequency at `1239`. Those descriptions concern different actions, so their unequal labels do not automatically establish an inconsistent event time or two-minute failure.

## Deferred inputs and next feasible sources

The [finding aid](https://www.archives.gov/research/foia/faa-finding-aid) identifies **A90-71 / 7599620**, **A90-74 / 34911499**, **A90-87 / 7599635**, **A90-98 / 7599645** and **A90-99 / 34911505** as specific follow-on records. Only their selected catalog rows were read; their PDFs and any audio are unacquired. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1d70d7cdbc981c431584) gives each exact link and discriminating use. No request or contact is authorized or has occurred.

This batch changes the next action by recovering a dated source-access statement and exact media/copy targets. It leaves the actual corrected May 2003 QFR, delivery/receipt, signed action/no-action decision and separate recommendation implementation/disposition chain unacquired. It does not substitute a 2001–2003 operational certificate for a later administrative outcome. All five work-plan tracks/eighteen substantive criteria remain open or partial; the technical evaluation and independent-audit owners retain their assignments.

Byte verification and labelled-interval arithmetic are documented separately from historical authentication, speech/copy comparison, independent clocks and scientific acceptance. Sources and previous records remain unchanged. Eligible research is prepared as an additive public branch; an unmerged PR will expose the explicit prior-wording qualification without overwriting history. Do not infer publication or PR creation from this prospective statement: separate receipts verify actual actions.

### FAA notification form and personnel statements: what was recorded, and what remains unverified
