# FAA notification form and personnel statements: what was recorded, and what remains unverified

November 1, 2026. Supplement to **B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04**, not a replacement claim register or a completed correction/disposition inquiry.

The held official accident packet contains a **military-authority notification entry at 1239**, alongside personnel accounts that identify concrete source-matching targets. It also contains uncertainty, relayed information, differing call-sign readings and repeated narrative material. These records help test who reported knowing what; they do not establish the call's contents, a calibrated notification timeline, deliberate deception or an executed intercept.

## Source and actual reading

This review used the already acquired **AEA1130 / NARA7598367**, a 237-page official public scan. Its original bytes remain unchanged: **15,505,493 bytes**, SHA-256 **5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179**. Acquisition was October 30, 2026; this new reading was November 1. [Official PDF](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf); [source and page/field locators](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f330eee9cf9271ae12e2).

All physical pages **191–206** were visually read as whole-page, 1800-pixel Poppler renders. Pages191 and193 are section dividers;192 is the notification form;194–196 and198 form the first statement block;200–201 the second;202–204 the third, including repeated narrative;197 and199 are crossed-through largely uncompleted forms;205 is visually blank apart from scan artifacts. Page206 begins another statement whose continuation was **not read**. No substantive conclusion from that partial fourth statement is used here. No hidden text extraction, redaction reconstruction, new OCR, audio authentication or clock fit was performed.

The initial twelve-page scope was extended prospectively by four adjacent leaves because page202 began an account continuing beyond that boundary. This completed the surrounding context rather than treating the first leaf as the entire account. The full237-page packet and all remaining personnel statements have not been read by this unit. Prior reading and publication remain separate milestones.

## 1. A recorded notification is not a content-authenticated call

On **physical192**, the military-authority row has **1239** in the time column and **Huntress ID** in the recipient field. The Washington Operations Center row also records **1239**, with **Via ROC** in its routing field. Several other rows contain **N/A**. Preserve these entries individually: N/A in one row does not erase a recorded entry in another.

The military row cites **FAA Order8020.11, paragraph66**; the form footer identifies **8020-3 (1-86)** and **Updated June13,2001**. The date field reads **9-11-01**. Those are different fields, not an authenticated time of form creation or a demonstrated policy violation. This page's time column does not itself specify a time zone. No UTC conversion, elapsed delay or first-ever military-notification time is computed here. [Form, physical192](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=192).

The surrounding UAL175 packet contains explicit **AAL11** accounts. Filing under UAL175 does not establish that the1239 entry conveyed knowledge of UAL175's hijacking, rather than AAL11 or another content scope. The entry qualifies a blanket claim that no military notification was recorded. It does not prove completed receipt, who actually heard the call, what was said, or a resulting operational action. The source recording, counterpart log, completion/receipt information and record-preparation history are the next discriminating inputs.

## 2. The later Huntress exchanges remain separate records

The [previously published OMIC review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/dcdc9f647130139c60128ae3c131d40955536b34/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-omic-body-completion-2026-10-31/README.md) pins actual Huntress-labelled dialogue at physical164–169, printed17–22. Its **1326** and **1329** exchanges discuss qualified, changing reports about American11. Both the new form entry and those later transcript labels remain preserved.

They are not automatically one contradictory call or one continuous knowledge state. Different events, content, channels and record conventions may be involved; that possibility remains untested. Conversely, the form cannot be used to explain away the later disagreement. A native source-to-call join is required before calculating delay, attributing a false statement or deciding that these reports conflict about the same event. This unit read191–206; it references the earlier verified reading of164–169 rather than claiming a new independent reading or witness.

## 3. Primary-target labels and visual reports have distinct origins

The R42 statement block on **194–196 and198** says another controller pointed out a primary target attributed through Boston to AAL11. It describes starting a track using **AAL11A** to follow the target. A tracking label assigned in that account is not, by itself, an independent aircraft identification.

On195, a reported visual observation associated with **USA583** suggests **FL290**; a later passage says the aircraft was not then seen, including on TCAS. On196, the writer reports another aircraft not seeing the primary target, then recounts loss of radio contact with UAL175, a changed code, and a later Delta-pilot report of United colors and **B767200**. On198, the account describes observing a descent from **28,400 to2,200 feet**, the latter being its last observed target/altitude. These are statements about display and relayed observations, not an independently decoded trajectory, authenticated aircraft identity or measured impact altitude. [194](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=194) · [195](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=195) · [196](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=196) · [198](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=198).

The distinctions matter in both directions. A failed visual search does not prove the aircraft was absent; a later identification report does not retrospectively make every earlier primary label certain. Underlying position recordings, pilot reports, radar/track data and their source/time associations are required. This does not perform or duplicate the separate recorder-decoding work.

## 4. Preserve the Delta call signs without choosing one

Selected handwritten occurrences on **195** read **DAL2433**; later occurrences on **196 and198** read **DAL2437**. Both readings remain recorded. No preferred call sign, cause of the difference, single-aircraft identity or second-aircraft theory is selected. Different aircraft, handwriting/transcription and recollection are possible explanations, not established results.

The native R42/radio recording, flight strips, handoff records and the original statement leaves are needed to distinguish those possibilities. These are visual readings of the released scan; no handwriting expertise or native-paper authentication is claimed. Exact locations are in SOURCE-PINS.json. The previously read OMIC altitude/source qualifications remain unchanged.

## 5. Event headings, statement dates and memory limitations are not interchangeable

The R42 form on194 has a **September11,2001 /1303UTC** incident heading and a signature-date field **9/20/01**. The RA42 form on200 has the same incident date/time but a signature-date field **10/3/01**. The RA42 account on201 reserves the ability to add or change information as remembered. Both accounts state that equipment settings were not remembered. These are source qualifications, not evidence that a change actually occurred or that either source is false. [194](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=194) · [200](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=200) · [201](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=201).

The AreaB/CIC account on202 bears an event-date heading and a separate circled **5/1/02** annotation. The annotation's author and purpose have not been established; it is not silently adopted as a creation, revision or signature date. Its body reports approximately1240Z information about AAL11, a relay to the OMIC and the OMIC's claimed prior awareness. Its continuation reports a later UAL175/code-change relay and hearing a request for military aircraft. An overheard request does not establish the recipient, actual scramble, execution or calibrated time. [202](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=202) · [203–204](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=203).

The strongest contrary reading to a coordinated-deception inference is that these accounts concern different tasks and moments, contain openly stated uncertainty, and distinguish remembered reports from direct display observations. That reading does not erase their differing entries. Native versions, preparer instructions, SISO records, source audio and actual routing/receipt records could strengthen or weaken concerns.

## 6. Repeated narrative and unused forms affect counting

Pages **203 and204** reproduce substantially the same handwritten continuation;204 includes a signature/name area absent from203's corresponding lower area. Their raster hashes differ. They remain two preserved leaves, not two independent witnesses or two verified revisions. The crossed-through forms197/199 and blank205 do not by themselves establish that a statement was removed, destroyed or suppressed. Their reason for inclusion is unknown. [203](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=203) · [204](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=204).

## Remaining work and publication boundaries

This adds source-level notification and witness-account roots. It does **not** recover the corrected May2003 answer and delivery receipt, signed administrative action/no-action determination, recommendation implementation/closure, native recorder/clock or materials inputs, or steel attribution decisions. All five tracks and eighteen substantive requirements remain open/partial. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1b30096375aa39c8f594) retain those dependencies.

Raw PDF/raster/signature/contact data and private coordination remain outside this public payload. No employee names, personal/contact fields or obscured identities are reproduced. The original source, preceding reports and every conflicting value remain unchanged. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-060e789de10b5e43869a), [additive diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a904a5a43e50a3913dea), [verifier](CITATIONS.md#nov04-642ae9294e77de1c15e1) and [actual verification scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-741a99a6e5f430ca676d) separate preservation checks from historical conclusions. Publication is neither main-branch adoption nor substantive goal acceptance.

# Actual FAA Operations Manager dialogue: twelve new page readings

October 31, 2026. **The actual dialogue supplies more precise notification, uncertainty and military-response leads than the previously read cover and endpoint.** It records airline and FAA information exchanges, qualified aircraft identification, an unresolved visual-verification account, an unidentified unanswered-call complaint and a report of military aircraft airborne. These are attributed statements in a released transcript, not independently verified events or proof of misconduct.

[Official NARA PDF](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=149), SHA-256 `5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179`. The previously acquired entity contains 237 physical pages and 15,505,493 bytes. Its original acquisition is documented in the [packet study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e64f8f3006879bfde9fa76f1db4b9be6b514d5af/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-completed-package-original-2026-10-30/README.md); no redownload occurred.

We newly read physical pages **149–160**, actually printed **2–13 of 26**, in twelve whole-page renders. The cover at 148 and terminal page at 173 were previously read. **Physical 161–172 remains unread.** This is therefore a partial Operations Manager transcript review, not a complete 26-page block or whole-packet examination. Across the checked study lineage, 32 distinct packet images have been read and 205 remain unread. Those counts describe scope, not evidentiary strength.

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0fd213897e5edb3dd123) separates printed statements from inference. [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-34b45bd7165e9debb21e) supplies exact pages, row times, source and raster hashes. [OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ec49d7969bdd80da4ecc) preserves the records needed to test each account. Original source bytes, conflicting labels and prior reports remain unchanged.

## What the body actually says

| Pin and source locator | Newly read observation | Implication and remaining limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OM01: physical 149–153, printed 2–6; 1241:14, 1244:23, 1245:35 | UAL175 heading/certification coexists with actual American 11 dialogue. Page 149 begins with bare 1230–1240 markers before timed row 1240:14. | A case heading does not identify every conversation's aircraft. Bare markers do not prove acoustic silence or missing speech. |
| OM02: 151/153/155; 1244:27/30, 1245:44 and 1246:49–54 | Earlier verification and “two nine zero” wording coexist with later “at thirty one,” “did not verify,” “at twenty nine” and “did not see him” wording. | Preserve both reports and their source expressions. Do not silently convert “thirty one” into a formal flight level. Actual altitude, identity and cause remain unverified. |
| OM03: 152–153; 1245:32–1246:04 | Boston/New York role-coded dialogue attributes information to the airline and reports cockpit microphone broadcasts and threats. | This identifies original radio/telephone counterpart targets. The relay is not a CVR or independent validation of the underlying broadcast or threat. |
| OM04: 154–155; 1246:40–54 | Requests to keep other traffic away accompany the later no-visual-confirmation report. | Requested protective work is contrary to a universal no-action account, but does not establish actual execution of every instruction or a verified military response. |
| OM05: 155–157; 1249:01–25 and 1252:55 | An AEA500-coded inquiry receives a hijack/position report; the ATM role later reports using two phones. | These are concrete role/time/source pointers. Labels do not authenticate voices, call-leg topology, bridge participation or the cause of a later unanswered call. |
| OM06: 158–159; 1252:59–1253:43 | Fire/possible-impact and looked-like-explosion descriptions are relayed with Newark/media attribution. | Preserve appearances and their source chain. This is not independently heard explosive evidence, a chemical finding or a demolition-mechanism identification; it supplies no WTC 7 connection. |
| OM07: 159; 1300:56, 1301:40, 1302:06 | Unanswered-phone complaints coexist with reported military aircraft airborne, F15s and a target point-out concerning a United aircraft. | The unanswered recipient and exact call leg are unidentified in this passage. Do not substitute NORAD/Huntress from a legend, or treat an airborne report as verified interception. |
| OM08: 159–160; 1303:17, 1310:24–29, 1311:09 | Ground-stop/ATC-zero discussion, a Cleveland-role query/answer and a Command Center NOM update request occur in the body. | These supply counterpart/order/log targets. A caller's negative-framed question is not an established opposite status; New York's reply reports ATC zero. No delivery delay or independent clock offset is measured. |
| OM09: 160; 1311:20–33 | New York's role disclaims confirmed aircraft identification, then reports suspicion of American 11 and United 175. | Preserve the qualification. A later packet heading is not contemporaneous certainty or independent proof of occupants or cause. |

All times are **printed labels**. The previously read cover declares 1230–1344 UTC and bears February 4, 2002. That declaration does not calibrate a source clock, authenticate a native version or signature, or show what a later reviewer received. No conversion into measured impact times or elapsed delays was performed.

## Conflicts and uncertainty remain explicit

**Verification and identity:** earlier verification/altitude expressions and the later no-visual-confirmation account remain side by side. Different observers, checks, targets or times, relay error and unsupported assurance are untested possibilities. Original radar/aircraft reports and matched audio would distinguish them. The later explicit uncertainty about identifying the two aircraft is contrary evidence to treating every earlier remark as confirmed knowledge.

**Telephone failure and military activity:** unanswered calls and aircraft reportedly airborne are different claims, not an automatic contradiction. The recipient is unresolved; one role's report is not independent confirmation of an intercept or intentional refusal. The two-phone remark is retained as a separate source statement, not selected as the cause of the later complaint. Obtain source-specific dialing, recipient and counterpart records before attributing the failed contact.

**Incident labels and appearances:** American 11 dialogue in a UAL175-headed transcript demonstrates that headings and individual conversation subjects need not match exclusively. This qualifies an exclusive-flight interpretation; it does **not** resolve the separate AWA937 AAL11/ZBW versus UAL175 work-file inconsistency in the [five-item comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e4e27ee8cd54f00d62917b2b9181d22503d2df9e/investigation/broader-evidence/five-inconsistency-tests-2026-10-31/README.md). Relayed impact, fire and explosion appearances likewise are not competing forensic mechanisms by themselves. No source is selected as controlling.

## Next records and the original investigation

These actual body passages provide more precise acquisition targets than a legend alone: matching Boston, regional-office, Command Center and other-center recordings; call-leg and failed-attempt records; actual ground-stop orders; radar/other-aircraft reports; and their clock/custody chains. A generic NOM recording label does not identify a particular call or channel.

No Huntress-coded timed row was observed in the twelve pages inspected. This is **not** an absence claim for unread 161–172, other channels, the complete line or historical contacts. The remaining held body is available work under the next prospective reading scope.

The actual May 2003 draft/answer/corrected version/receipt, signed administrative consideration/action/no-action, and separate recommendation response/implementation/closure remain missing inputs. This transcript does not establish which later official read which source version or knowingly made an inaccurate statement. Test a concealment interpretation by matching material information to actual received/reviewed records and the exact later statement or decision. Different source scopes, uncertain identifications, accurate qualifications, documented corrections or substantive follow-through could weaken that interpretation. None is adopted to explain away the unresolved record.

All 18 original FAA, telephone, recorder, materials and steel acceptance criteria remain open or partial. Actual source reading is progress; publication and hashes do not close required original, custody or empirical joins. Independent decoding and calibrated clock tests remain assigned to the technical owner. Available unread body pages prevent a premature whole-goal blocked determination.

## Preservation, checks and revision accountability

The held input fingerprint was checked before rendering. Twelve new rasters and their hashes remain in the verified source store. [SOURCE-CHECKS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c906bd8d114e8dd8ef4f) records the actual scope. Reading is separate from rendering and hashing. No new download, OCR, hidden-layer extraction, masked-character recovery, listening, waveform/decoder work, independent clock/radar fit, exhaustive text comparison or scientific/historical authentication occurred. Faint or occluded characters remain unknown.

Before publication, preliminary shorthand expanded the later spoken “thirty one” into 310. That was our interpretation, not a verbatim source field. The preliminary local record and its correction remain preserved; this edition retains the source expressions. No source bytes or conflicting evidence were changed. [EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-aeb9f1c10b7f559a287c) preserves this edition's immediate-predecessor text changes; [TEXT-CHANGES.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-79682352af7ae8d577db) records the actual additions against the new public directory's preceding absent state. PDF/image reading is separate from both text diffs. Earlier failed OCR checks and reading corrections remain unchanged.

[CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a758f817984bd1bdca62) accounts for the requirements. Only eligible original notes, selected safe fields, source pins, qualifications and TODOs form the public derivative. Excluded: raw marked/protected sources, full speech, signatures, identifying/contact/access/financial data, lawsuit material, private correspondence/strategy and credentials. The separate actual AveLyra noreply/account/destination/SSH/ref/all-file receipt establishes publication, not historical truth. No outreach, fee, request, filing, mailbox/private-case access, source overwrite, deletion, private push, main merge or history rewrite occurred.

# FAA Operations Manager transcript: remaining body and notification-source leads

Reviewed October 31, 2026. This is a new source-reading supplement to the [first-half review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/9c1438f5fb3b9098513db5bad9c5a13f6bb127ea/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-omic-body-2026-10-31/README.md), not a replacement for it. No source bytes or earlier findings were edited.

The useful new lead is a traceable information chain about **American 11 allegedly remaining airborne**. One Huntress-labelled exchange cannot confirm it; a later exchange reports it affirmatively and invokes a mission crew commander. The transcript then records New York relaying a separate criticism of Washington Center and Washington questioning that criticism. These are statements in an officially released scan, not independent authentication of the underlying events or proof of deliberate deception.

## Source and actual scope

[NARA catalogue, NAID7598367](https://catalog.archives.gov/id/7598367) · [official FAA AEA1130 PDF](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf)

Source SHA-256: `5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179`. The held 237-page, 15,505,493-byte PDF was acquired October 30, 2026, and rechecked unchanged for this unit. Newly read: **physical pages161–172, printed14–25 of26**, individually viewed as whole-page rasters. The preceding studies read the cover148, body149–160 and end173. Thus this specific 26-page block has now been visually read across those studies. That does not authenticate its source recording, completeness, voices, clock, custody or all other records. Across the checked packet lineage,44distinct pages have been read and193remain outside that scope. Counts measure reading coverage, not proof.

`SOURCES.json` gives each page's first/last timed row. `SOURCE-PINS.json` supplies physical/printed page, row time, source URL/hash, selected literal wording and qualifications for OM10–OM17. Times below retain the printed clock (HHMM:SS); UTC is declared on the transcript cover, not independently calibrated here. No seconds-level cross-record alignment was performed.

## Conflicting or changing reports, preserved side by side

| Source locator | What the source reports | Limit / discriminating record |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OM13, physical164–166 / printed17–19,1326:18–1327:24 | Actual Huntress-labelled rows. New York says it was given a number and, at1326:47, describes **an unconfirmed report** that American11 might remain airborne. At1327:21 Huntress is unsure and cannot confirm either way. | Native audio/call-leg identity and the report's source are unverified. Earlier unanswered-phone passages remain unidentified; this later contact does not identify their recipient. |
| OM14, physical166–168 / printed19–21,1329:28–1330:23 | A later Huntress-labelled opening says the caller was told to call;1329:32 invokes the mission crew commander. Qualifiers accompany the three-aircraft/two-impacts/one-toward-Washington account. At1329:50/53 the caller says American11 was **not the crashed aircraft** and was **still airborne**. At1329:59 current location is unknown and Washington is last-known direction. New York says it had not received this information. | Preserve alongside OM13 and earlier suspected impact identification. Do not assume the same caller, source, correctly identified aircraft or validated route. Retrieve the mission-crew-command input/instruction, plots, counterpart recordings and correction trail. |
| OM15, physical169 / printed22,1330:39–56 | Huntress criticizes Washington, clarifies **Washington Center**, and assents to New York's paraphrase about not taking it seriously. New York promises to let Washington know. | An attributed criticism and a promise, not established Washington conduct or a completed relay. |
| OM16, physical170–171 / printed23–24,1331:46–1332:31 | New York relays the Huntress contact. At1331:52 a ZDC OMIC-labelled row says **“we got the same call”** and mentions an air major/Boston contact. At1332:08 New York qualifies its account as **“this is only relay”**. After New York relays the seriousness criticism, a Washington speaker says they do not know what is meant and, at1332:29, **“i haven't even i haven't even talked to em”**, then promises a call. | Both statements retained. The shared ZDC role label does not prove one individual; holds/introductions intervene. Receiving an American11 report also need not mean receiving the seriousness criticism. The difference is unresolved, not a proved single-person contradiction. |
| OM17, physical172 / printed25,1333:35–1334:00 | ZNY505 reports American77 lost at **flight level three five zero** near the printed **“york kentucky”** wording and requests checks for primary targets. At1333:52 **“went down”** is immediately qualified by **“didn't go down”** and **“they just lost everything”**. | Preserve the wording; do not silently repair geography or convert loss of contact into a crash. The earlier generic Indianapolis/American report on164 is not independently joined to this aircraft. |

The strongest objection to treating this as intentional false information is that these are changing, sometimes explicitly unconfirmed relays during an unfolding event, with incomplete call-leg/speaker/source information. That possibility is not established either. The native messages, validation/correction steps, actual recipients and counterpart audio could distinguish ordinary error or relay confusion from knowingly false reporting. No originator, intent, causal scramble or cover-up is established by this reading.

## Other concrete leads and contrary evidence

- **Military detail remains qualified (OM10).** At1311:53 and1312:01, physical161 / printed14, ZNY reports military aircraft up but does not know count/location/origin. Keep this beside earlier F15/point-out reports on159, rather than assume inconsistency or verified interception.
- **Headquarters telcon: request and plan, not attendance (OM11).** Physical162–164 / printed15–17: AEA500 asks a manager to join at1318:07; an Unknown-labelled speaker refers to getting a number at1320:15; ATM says going to the telcon at1325:04. Actual participation, bridge topology and any military participation remain unverified.
- **Area B staff hold/discussions (OM12).** Physical163–164 / printed16–17,1320:20 and1325:06/17: a hold-before-discussion instruction coexists with breaks/discussions and shaken-staff descriptions. This warrants retrieving the actual instructions, independent statements and tape/custody crosswalk. It does not establish coaching, fabricated accounts or destruction, and is not automatically the separately described recorded-statements tape.
- **Readiness wording (OM15).** Physical168 / printed21,1330:09–20 contains readiness discussion and a claim of a recent increase. It gives no authenticated formal order, level or effective time. A comment about not saying too much is not proof of concealment.

## What changed, what remains open

The prior review's absence of a Huntress-coded row was expressly limited to149–160. The new rows are on164–169. The earlier bounded observation remains accurate; it must not be generalized to the whole block or earliest historical contact. These studies are dependent readings of one released scan, not independent corroborating records.

Only **OM-T08 (remaining page reading)** is completed. OM-T01–07 remain TODO_INPUT; OM-T09–12 identify the new source-input, receiving/callback, staff-discussion and order/radar dependencies. The five-track plan's original18requirements all remain open/partial. This transcript does not establish which later officials read which version, the actual May2003 corrected answer/delivery, a signed2006 action/no-action determination or recommendation disposition.

Missing inputs stay TODOs. No outreach, request, fees, mailbox access, technical decoding or experiment occurred. Original sources and conflicting values remain unchanged. Source integrity checks in `SOURCE-CHECKS.json` are separate from visual reading and do not authenticate historical truth. `CHECKLIST.md` maps the user's requirements; `TEXT-CHANGES.diff` is the actual Git addition diff against absent prior paths. Raw marked source pages, full speech, personal/contact/access/signature fields and private case material are excluded from this public derivative. Publication status is established separately by the remote receipt, not this prepublication text.

Prepublication editorial review corrected the agent draft's physical161 terminal row from1312:15 to1312:13 and replaced paraphrased/typographically changed fragments in literal-quotation fields with the inspected source wording. These were errors in our draft, not discrepancies in the released evidence. The source and v1 draft remain preserved; `EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff` records the exact changes.

# FAA personnel accounts: military contacts, routing and correction instructions

November 1, 2026. Existing **B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04**. This additive supplement completes reading the personnel section reproduced in the held packet. It does not complete the missing FAA correction/disposition investigation.

The most useful new record is the military-operations specialist's two-page account of obtaining a NEADS number and later requesting jets. It supplies a closer comparison for the earlier **1239** notification entry. The records do not yet establish that those references describe the same call, flight, content or time convention. The underlying recordings and routing records are the discriminating evidence.

## Source and completed reading scope

**AEA1130/NARA7598367** was acquired October30,2026 from the [official download](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf). Its unchanged entity is **15,505,493 bytes**, SHA-256 **5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179**. This unit visually read physical **207–234** at1800pixels, and rechecked208/210/211 with fresh3000pixel renders. The detail renders are another display of the same file, not an independent record or handwriting authentication. No OCR, hidden text extraction or redaction reconstruction was performed.

Together with the [preceding191–206 review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6e846c1ea159fde25e1291c1c5046e70e2e4de80/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-notification-personnel-originals-2026-11-01/README.md), this covers the personnel section between divider193 and the weather-section divider232. Page206 is prior context, not counted again as a new page. This unit read the OSIC continuation207, OMIC208–209, military specialist210–211, air traffic manager212–214/216/218, assistant manager220–221, traffic-management accounts222–225, R55226/228 and D55230–231. Blank or largely unused leaves215/217/219/227/229 remain preserved.233–234 were viewed as adjacent weather context; no new meteorological inference or full-weather-section claim is made. This is not a complete reading or authentication of all237pages, all agency statements or every retained version.

Physical page and named field/paragraph references below are precise scan locators, not invented original line numbers. [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-26e25051b641ed4d16c4) includes acquisition dates, source/raster hashes, reading scope and the selected observations. Raw scans, signatures, employee names, full telephone numbers and the printed telcon access details remain outside this public payload.

## 1. Preserve the notification entry and the specialist's account separately

| Record | Displayed entry or reported event | What it does not establish |
|---|---|---|
| Previously read physical192, military-authority row | **1239 /Huntress ID**; the caller-field name corresponds to the writer identified on210 |Native identity/call join, contents, time zone, receipt or flight-specific knowledge.|
| Physical210, opening body |At **1249**, a request to obtain a NEADS telephone number; the writer says a VSCS call obtained a number, which was passed on |That this is the same event as the1239 notification row, an exact clock reading, or an operational scramble request.|
| Physical210–211, continuation |**Maybe10minutes later, maybe less**, a manager requested a call asking for jets because another hijack was possible |An exact second-call time, authenticated request receipt or actual launch.|
| Physical211, phone description |A separate phone beside the VSCS is followed by the circled parenthetical **1429** |That1429 is a time rather than an extension/position/other identifier. Its meaning is unestablished.|
| Physical211, end of account |The writer reports giving a call sign, possible transponder code and brief summary; NEADS reportedly said Boston had also requested jets |Independent confirmation of the words, Boston request, knowledge of every official or operational execution.|
| Physical216,1304Z-labelled paragraph |A manager says the specialist reported the military had scrambled and wanted the current aircraft location |An independently verified scramble order, launch time, aircraft assignment or interception.|

[210](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=210) · [211](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=211) · [216](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=216). The specialist form's signature-date field reads **09/19/01**; it is a released recollection, not a live source recording or authenticated native signed original. The word-level readings1249 and1429 were checked against the3000pixel displays. No elapsed delay or synthesized historical chronology is computed.

The1239/1249 records are an **unresolved comparison requiring an event join**, not proof of a ten-minute delay or false entry. Obtaining a number, notifying, requesting jets, reporting a scramble and actual execution are distinct acts. Different calls, recollection/entry errors, differing time conventions and other explanations remain testable possibilities. None is selected as the cause. The later1326/1329 Huntress transcript exchanges already published remain another set of records; their disagreement is not resolved by either account.

## 2. Telcon instructions are concrete routing leads, not attendance proof

The OMIC account on208 says the writer was asked to join a telcon with **CF2/BOS and military** about AAL11. Its opening time contains **1240** struck through with **1238** beside it. Both visible values remain; no preferred historical time or amendment chronology is adopted. The body also reports monitoring, dispatcher information and other relays; the signature-date field is **9/14/01**. [208](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=208).

The air traffic manager's account on218 says a regional official directed immediate participation in a **national telcon**. That direction alone does not prove the writer joined, who else was present, that this was the telcon described on208, or who received a particular statement. The full telephone/access details are excluded here. [218](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=218).

The212–218 account also describes attempts to reach regional personnel, holds and unavailable recipients, a request to check television reports, and later briefings. These are participants' reports to match to original lines, bridge records, attendance and counterpart logs. Different attempted/completed call legs cannot be collapsed into a single agency-wide knowledge state. Nor do the written accounts alone establish coordinated delay or concealment.

## 3. Stop accepting traffic, clear sectors and declare ATC zero are different outcomes

The previously incomplete OSIC account206–207 is now read through its end. The opening reports stopping incoming AreaB traffic before a named route point; the continuation describes tracking/avoiding UAL175, seeing its target disappear, receiving impact news, relieving upset controllers and consolidating sectors after their aircraft cleared. It states equipment settings were not remembered. This is not evidence of coaching or suppression of accounts. [207](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=207).

The OMIC account on209 records a **1315** statement that facilities could go ATC zero while retaining airspace, and a **1325** oceanic-diversion report. The manager account on216 separately records a **1305Z** direction to declare New York Center ATC zero. The traffic-management account on225 says ATCSCC was notified at approximately **1305Z** that ZNY was ATC zero and entering traffic should stop. The earlier RA42 account on201 describes deciding to close that sector after the target disappeared. [209](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=209) · [216](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=216) · [225](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=225).

Preserve **1305/1315** and their different scope/wording. Do not infer one ten-minute system delay, a stand-down order or a preferred time. Local traffic restrictions, a sector closure, facility declaration, relayed authorization and retained airspace need their own source/receipt/execution joins. The recorded actions are counterevidence to a blanket claim that nobody attempted protective action; they do not establish that every action was timely or effective.

## 4. Local awareness and failed counterpart recognition remain separate observations

The traffic-management account224–225 reports that AreaB notified OMIC/TMU of a deviation around **1250Z**, followed by display observations of **code3321**. It recounts N90 initially not identifying or displaying that target around **1300Z**, then reporting an unidentified9500foot target around **1302Z**, and an apparent crash around **1303Z**. These are remembered, approximately timed relays, not verified original N90 utterances or independently calibrated coverage. [224](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=224) · [225](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=225).

The R55/D55 accounts226/228/230/231 separately describe tracking/point-outs, traffic-avoidance actions and an intruder code3321. The D55 continuation describes asking Approach to hold not-yet-departed traffic and reports its last observed hit at **2200feet**. Preserve their qualifications and settings-memory limitations. This is not measured impact altitude or independent aircraft identity. [228](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=228) · [231](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=231).

These accounts supply specific facilities, roles and candidate source events. Differences in identification/display or relayed certainty could reflect different coverage, times, tasks or records; deliberate misstatement remains an unproved hypothesis. Source recordings, display settings, radar/track data, flight strips and counterpart records are required to discriminate. This unit performs no new recorder decoding or clock alignment and does not duplicate the technical owner's work.

## 5. Compare visible amendments with the held correction instructions

The previously published **AEA1121** correction list, physical9/printed8of8, directs handwritten equipment-configuration statements and conditional removal/initialling of the Attachment label. It separately asks for an OSIC accident-time change to **1303**, asks whether the assistant-manager heading is **1303 or1309**, and requests a D55 location of **New York,NY**. Its marked responses say changes were made. These are source instructions/judgments, not our adoption of their correctness. [Original list](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1121-MEMORANDUM--FORMAL-ACCIDENT-PACKAGE-REF-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-CORRECTIONS.pdf#page=9) · [earlier full review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/a42d0956202dacc3b971d21f479966d56fefcd60/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-package-version-chain-2026-10-30/README.md).

The now-read forms contain corresponding features: handwritten settings-memory statements and struck preprinted/Attachment labels; the206 header has1303 with an earlier time struck;220 has1303 above a struck time field;230 has a New York location alongside struck prior location text. The body's other events are not automatically retimed by an incident-header change. Selected visible call-sign headers on208/210/212/226/230 likewise contain crossed AAL11 and written UAL175 labels, while bodies still discuss AAL11. Both labels and the bodies remain preserved.

This correspondence is an observable version-control lead, **not native authentication that the memo caused every mark**, that all requested edits were properly implemented, or that any change occurred after signing. The memo supplies an explicit ordinary review context for some annotations; it does not explain every call-sign/body/time difference. Original and amended leaves, approvals/initials, submission/receipt and actual change history remain necessary. No record is overwritten or adjudicated here. These2002 package corrections are not the missing May2003 QFR correction or2006 administrative outcome.

## Completion and remaining dependencies

The bounded personnel-section reading dependency is complete **for this reproduced packet**, with blank/repeated/annotated leaves preserved. It is not a claim that every statement is complete, independently corroborative, historically accurate or natively authenticated. One declared indexed search for a1429/ZNY archive locator returned no result; no recording was acquired from that search, and no nonexistence conclusion follows.

All five tracks and eighteen substantive requirements remain open/partial. Actual May2003 drafts/correction/delivery, signed administrative action/no-action/reasons and distinct recommendation outcomes are still missing. Telephone native joins, recorder/custody/clocks, materials specimen/calibration records and steel preservation/attribution remain required. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-219e86ef09f26ba475e4) carries them forward. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-84949969d036a6dd8225), [revision diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-fedc78f2ae4224356202), [verifier](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8a56c2e94dcf74e7dd69) and [verification scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8e8fc13f368ef552fcc2) distinguish byte/reading checks from historical inference, publication and whole-goal acceptance.

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 76abaaa5ebacde0c0d5cdd30da5cb4c815b11c216f2d4eac901276e7b9817920 -->
# FAA position records: four time matches, distinct labels and unresolved clocks

Research date: November 1, 2026. Existing B129 / PD01–PD04 / FAA01–FAA04 record trail. This is one bounded inspection of held public primary records, not a new download, a whole-packet authentication or completion of the original-record work plan.

The located position report supplies all four preselected on/off intervals, and each agrees with the corresponding statement's time fields after removing the report's colon punctuation. This is useful for locating the underlying records. **It is not independent validation:** the statement forms direct writers to make their times correspond with SISO. One position label remains different, `RA42` versus `D42`; the report's timezone and historical clock accuracy are not established. Separate roster and overtime records also prevent treating an `RDO` entry as standalone evidence that a writer was absent.

A separate within-form discrepancy is preserved: two weather descriptions and their adjacent time boxes differ by four hours while both label their values UTC. The values' cause and effect remain untested. These findings do not establish coordinated falsification, foreknowledge, suppression or a cover-up.

## Source and reading scope

Official held source: [AEA1130 / NARA7598367, completed accident package](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf), acquired October 30, 2026, 18:22:50 UTC; 15,505,493 bytes, 237 physical PDF pages. SHA-256: `5bb71c00b716fb59425571d0c714a97a32d84b13f35dcc65d7cc6bd6f83b5179`. Physical PDF page numbers below are one-based; printed sheet pagination differs. The held bytes were rehashed and preserved unchanged. The public derivative contains research text, selected field transcriptions and provenance, without the raw scan, personnel roster, names, signatures or contact/access details.

Actually inspected in this unit: **27 distinct new whole pages — 5, 8, 16 and 24–47**. Page 39 received an additional whole-page 3000-pixel legibility render; that is not an additional source page. Previously inspected statement faces 194, 200, 226 and 230 were rechecked as context, not counted as new acquisition or new pages. On the position printouts the relevant columns/selected rows and boundaries were read; faint, cropped and unrelated totals were not exhaustively transcribed. The other 237-page packet contents have not all been reviewed by this unit.

The frozen four-row selection preceded complete-log reading. Four directly relevant positions were retained regardless of outcome; no failed or inconvenient row was replaced. Visible name/operating-initial cross-references support the candidate row joins, but native identity and custody have not been authenticated. Source faces, render hashes, precise column locators and all selected strings are in [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1a5d52011dda862b424c) and [COMPARISON.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5efab08d95fdca1a9443). No hidden text extraction, new OCR, redaction reconstruction or source-image editing occurred.

## What the physical sequence contains

[Physical 24–36](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=24) includes the Section 5 Personnel Logs divider; Area B personnel, additional-personnel, OSIC/CIC certification, correction and assignment-change forms; and Traffic Management forms. The sign-in/sign-out correction form on physical 32 / printed 7 of 8 has an unused body. That does not prove no corrections occurred elsewhere. Physical 31 has populated certification time fields; physical 33 has assignment changes. Those records have distinct purposes and must not be collapsed into the blank correction form.

The **Section 6 Automated Sign-In/Sign-Out (SISO) Data divider is physical 37**, immediately followed by the **Section 7 Facility Layout Chart divider on physical 38**. A position report containing the selected rows is at **physical 39**, with further printout material at 40–42; facility drawings follow at 43–44. Physical 45 is the Section 8 divider; physical 46 contains two UAL175 flight strips; physical 47 begins Section 9 Transcriptions of Voice Recordings. The misplaced-looking divider sequence is preserved as observed. It does not show why the sequence exists, that a page was deliberately removed, or that SISO data are globally absent. Reading the divider alone would have missed the available report.

Physical 40 carries a representation notice: the document derives from magnetic recordings of internal computer processing and is not an exact representation of the control-position display. That is a scope qualification, not a finding of tampering, a guarantee of raw-data completeness, or proof of what a controller saw. Physical 44 labels an Area B layout position `D42`, separately from `R42`; it does not by itself authenticate the statement's `RA42` terminology, actual staffing or September 11 hardware/display state.

## Four selected position intervals

Statement position fields and on/off fields are on [194](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=194), [200](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=200), [226](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=226) and [230](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=230). The report's POSITION, TIME-ON and TIME-OFF columns are on [39](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=39). Selected row locators below avoid reproducing personnel identities.

| Statement face and literal position label | Statement fields, UTC printed beside fields | Report position and selected row | Report TIME-ON / TIME-OFF | Bounded result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 194, `RADAR Sector42` | `1207`–`1319` | `R42`, second R42 row | `12:07`–`13:19` | Both time strings match after colon removal. `R42` is the research role shorthand; literal labels remain distinct. |
| 200, `RA42` | `1255`–`1319` | `D42`, row immediately below the two R42 rows | `12:55`–`13:19` | Time strings match; `RA42` / `D42` label difference remains preserved. |
| 226, `R55` | `1120`–`1316` | `R55`, sixth R55 row | `11:20`–`13:16` | Both time strings match. |
| 230, `D55` | `1132`–`1316` | `D55`, third D55 row | `11:32`–`13:16` | Both time strings match. |

**What was tested:** punctuation-only comparison of eight selected time fields, with source strings retained. All four on/off pairs agree. This is a transcribed-field consistency test, not a measurement of clock error. No timezone label is visible on the inspected position-report face; its case context and surrounding dated records are not a substitute for an authenticated export/date/timezone definition.

**Strongest limitation on corroboration:** all four statement faces explicitly require statement times and dates to correspond with SISO. Their agreement is compatible with writers consulting the same administrative source. The actual preparation history remains unknown; we have not proved copying in any individual instance. The agreement cannot be multiplied into four independent confirmations of clock accuracy, identity, continuous presence, what was heard, or every statement narrative.

The prior [personnel continuation review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/59828ff9efa5ee2bf073ebf3796a012b35bdd180/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-personnel-continuation-2026-11-01/README.md) retains the January 2002 correction memorandum's account that RA42 and D42 refer to the same sector. That is the memorandum author's characterization, not this investigation's adjudication of equivalent positions or duties. The newly located report preserves `D42` alongside `RA42`; no label is substituted or made controlling. Underlying position definitions and amendments remain TODOs.

## Shift records differ in scope from position records

The Personnel Log's Time on / Time off fields cover shift/attendance-style entries; the position report and statements cover particular positions. No timezone is visible on the selected roster sheets. The research has not converted a shift time into UTC or treated unlike intervals as a contradiction.

| Role used to locate the same statement author's row | Personnel Log fields | Additional context | Consequence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R42 | Physical 26 / printed 1 of 8: `0630`–`1430` | Position interval above is `1207`–`1319` UTC | Different scope and unestablished clock basis; no attendance residual calculated. |
| RA42 | Physical 28 / printed 3 of 8: `0630`–`1430` | Released POS area obscured | Do not reconstruct the hidden POS field or infer its exact label. |
| R55 | Physical 28 / printed 3 of 8: `0657`–`1457` | Statement interval `1120`–`1316` UTC | Both records retained without timezone conversion. |
| D55 | Physical 26: Time on `RDO`, Time off blank | Physical 30, Operational Overtime or Additional Personnel, has same-name/initial entries `0645`–`1445` with `8.0` duty hours, and `0615`–`0645` with `.5` | Preserve all entries; `RDO` alone does not establish absence or false duty. |

`RDO` is left unexpanded because the native legend and roster definitions have not been obtained. The additional-personnel rows and position interval are material contrary evidence to an absence inference. They also do not, without authentication and clock definitions, prove physical attendance or resolve the records' administrative meaning. Overtime labels, signatures or correction marks are not motive evidence.

## Weather fields: two explicit UTC-labelled mismatches

[Physical 5 / FAA Form 8020-6, printed 1 of 4, report date 10/11/01](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=5), field 11:

| Field 11 row | Narrative-body time, as printed | Adjacent DATE/TIME, as printed | Arithmetic on the printed HHMM values |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Report just prior to accident | `0751 UTC` | `09/11/01 1151 UTC` | Adjacent minus body = 240 minutes |
| First report subsequent to accident | `0951 UTC` | `09/11/01 1351 UTC` | Adjacent minus body = 240 minutes |

Both pairs explicitly use UTC labels. The separate conditions-at-accident narrative also says `0851 UTC`, but it has no matching adjacent DATE/TIME box in this comparison; no third pair is manufactured. We preserve all printed values and do not choose a preferred one, relabel a value as local time, or apply a four-hour correction to the accident, SISO, radar or recorder timelines.

**Untested hypotheses:** a time-convention or export/editorial error, or another preparation error, could produce these labels. Their likelihood and specific cause are not established. The original weather product, form-preparation worksheets, native version history and applicable time instructions could discriminate. Technical weather decoding and comparison of the weather products elsewhere in the packet were not performed here. The disagreement warrants a source-quality check; by itself it shows neither a physical four-hour offset nor deliberate falsification.

## A concrete historical custodian lead

[Physical 16, Certified Index dated September 19, 2001](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/dc-metro/rg-237/7419198-9-11-faa/4-UAL-175/e-Other/4-AEA-1130-COMPLETED-ACCID-PCKG--FORMAL-ACCID-PCKG-ZNY-ARTCC-148-UAL175-REDACT-SEALED.pdf#page=16) records the Boston TRACON Air Traffic Manager's certification that listed originals were on file in that office. It lists Form 7230-4; Form 7230-10; Form 8020-6-1; Sign On Log: A90; Personnel Statements; **Original Voice Recording(s)**; and **Certified Cassette Recording(s)**.

The list distinguishes original voice recordings from certified cassette copies and identifies a historical office. It supplies a specific records/custody target. It does not authenticate today's scan signatures, prove the originals survive today, identify all recorded channels, or show that those audio originals are embedded in this PDF. Boston TRACON's contribution also must not be treated as the same facility/report version as the New York ARTCC October 11 form. No holder was contacted and no request was sent.

## Consequences, missing inputs and unchanged limits

This batch advances the operational source map: previously sought SISO-style position rows are now located in the held scan; four predetermined fields can be compared; the direct statement-to-SISO instruction constrains independence; roster/overtime context qualifies an absence inference; and a labelled weather inconsistency and historical original/copy custodian list are pinned.

The native SISO export, date/timezone/code dictionary, generation/audit/correction history, roster/extra-duty definitions, original voice/cassette provenance, and independent clock anchors remain missing. No physical-clock alignment, speech/event validation, contemporaneous knowledge inference, or historical-cause determination has been performed. A shared origin could explain document agreement without proving operational accuracy; the source chain must be tested rather than counted repeatedly.

The actual May 2003 corrected QFR and delivery acknowledgment; signed action/no-action determination; and separate OIG recommendation response, implementation and closure remain unacquired. This September 2001 staffing material does not substitute for those records. All five work-plan tracks and eighteen substantive criteria remain open or partial; publication is not acceptance of them. Technical NIST/UAF, recorder/materials comparisons and independent-method audit retain their assigned owners.

[Open inputs and revisit tests](CITATIONS.md#nov04-4445e152bb3aa6885605) · [Point-by-point checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-92e9f20f80a8380fbe1c) · [Revision diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-bd57214575a41a78b427) · [Verifier](CITATIONS.md#nov04-924657654568f853cf9e). Earlier findings and source bytes remain unchanged. No outreach, request, fee, filing, private-source disclosure, private push or history rewrite is authorized by this report.

# FBI telephone sources: a reported original diskette, field definitions and the Keyton counterpart

October 31, 2026. **TEL01-TEL04 / existing AUD016-A1,TC05-B105, TC06-B110**. Two newly preserved official FBI PDF entities improve the source trail. The most useful new retrieval target is a contemporaneous report that an agent received GTE spreadsheets on paper and on an **Excel 97 diskette**. An official-hosted Keyton form supplies a counterpart to the already reviewed mirror. These are source acquisitions, not independent proof of the calls or a new historical witness.

## Sources and actual reading

The public FBI item pages exposed their download links: [September part01](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-01-of-08/view) and [part02](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/view). Both public GETs succeeded without login or a gate workaround. Part01 has 49 pages, 4,468,234 bytes, SHA-256 **4760de107bef3c75f4d7629f90f7a70d2c861cb3b53dcbe37a3bf0fb768f93cb**; part02 has 78 pages, 5,561,167 bytes, SHA-256 **055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51**. Item sizes/page counts match the preserved entities. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-9d63ef4627b89de554b2) records exact URLs, acquisition times and reused sources.

**Eight distinct page images were read**, part01 physical 20/45/46 and part02 physical 24/56/69/70/71. A higher-resolution crop checked one digit on already selected 46. Ordinary text from 127 pages was used only for limited locators. No whole-release reading/completeness or OCR-based absence claim follows. Raw PDFs, rasters and full extraction remain preserved outside this public payload. [Reading scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a163e4cf81882944ba54).

The earlier October 27 review reported an FBI bundle unacquired after 403. Its exact denied URL has not been established from the inspected history; the initial current census omitted nested publication records. Do not claim the same endpoint was never tried, that current 200 disproves the old 403, or that either access result proves concealment. Current official hosting authenticates the preserved public entity's provenance, not every assertion or complete underlying production.

## 1. A concrete native-spreadsheet and attachment trail

[Part01 physical 45-46](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-01-of-08/at_download/file#page=45) is a September 11, 2001 interview report, internal pages 1-2, file 265A-NY-280350. The subject is redacted; no identity is reconstructed from other releases. Page 45 says notes, a typed event summary and a screen print were handed to the interviewing agent. It attributes the UA93/UA175 spreadsheet preparation to Network Operations using **GTE ACIS billing-system information**, with relevant flights extracted/listed. The source uses cellular-call wording alongside Airphone; that wording does not establish personal-handset use.

Page 46's **last paragraph** says the agent received paper copies and a copy on a **3.5-inch diskette under Microsoft Excel 97**. The diskette, native workbooks, attachment/evidence identifiers and custody are still missing. This is a precise original-record lead: obtain a preserved image/native files and the paper set, then inspect stored versus displayed dates/times, keys, sheets, formulas, flight filters and version history. Do not infer the supplied workbook was raw switch data, that its bytes survive, or that this record is the missing September 20 usage analysis.

## 2. Field explanations advance the dictionary trail, with explicit limits

Page 45 describes **ANSWER_STATUS 0 = no answer, 1 = answered**. Page 46 describes termination type 0 = normal / 1 = abnormal; termination direction **0 = air, 1 = ground**; reason code 0 = normal, 22 = invalid data, 24 = credit-card denied; and SCP duration as call length in seconds. Its unit/terminal/cellsite/handoff explanations give further configuration targets, not authenticated seat/caller identities.

I initially misread the air-side direction digit as 1 and announced a 1/1 conflict. The closer source-image check shows 0/1. **Do not count that preliminary analyst error as an FBI discrepancy.** [Reading correction](CITATIONS.md#nov04-30121ab0e9ee0a7da614) preserves the false preliminary claim and actual validation, without rewriting the source or previous reports.

The CALL_DIAL_TIME explanation on page 45 is qualified: times **“are believed”** to use the originating caller's time zone, described as **“eastern standard time.”** Preserve that wording; no EST/EDT conversion, calibrated offset or individual-row timezone is established here. A proposed loss-of-signal/turn/range explanation for abnormal termination in the report is a provider account of possibilities, not a determination of why any selected call ended.

The previously acquired [May 13, 2004 UA175 briefing, pages 2-3](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00216.pdf#page=2) already supplies field explanations: Connect is setup time in seconds; SCP duration is time connected to the dialed number; operator duration is time on line, with no separate unit in that bullet. It describes names known in United's system and later DOJ additions from interviews. The 2001 report refers to names already in its billing system. These are differently scoped descriptions to preserve and test against native versions, not established incompatible measurements or proof of fabricated names. [Field comparison](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a6c1a7f672565a438ec2).

The 2004 briefing's Eastern/two-UA93-Central-zone account remains beside the 2001 qualified standard-time wording. No common-row identity or clock correction is inferred. An interview-based field explanation is useful evidence, but does not authenticate installed vendor/version rules, timeout/answer supervision, row allocation, billing legs or real conversations. None of these GTE rules is automatically applied to AA77's separate AT&T/Claircom records. Earlier 35-row counts/long durations/uncertain glyphs are not recalculated or resolved here.

## 3. Keyton: official-hosted counterpart, dates and unresolved collect/direct accounts

[Part02 physical 69](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=69) records Lori Lynn Keyton's account. The form prints **transcription 09/14/2001** and **investigation 9/11/01**; its dictated-date field is visibly blank. Those are distinct form fields, not automatically inconsistent event dates. About 9:00 a.m. has no printed timezone. It reports approximately 6-8 automated collect attempts, a live-operator collect call, then a later call described as **direct, not collect**, a few to five minutes later. She had **no caller ID** and did **not know onboard phone versus cellphone**. Olson/Olsen spelling variants remain as printed, without inference about identity or cause.

The [earlier mirror physical 48](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=48) already supplied that account. The gain is official-hosted provenance, not another independent witness. The prior review of [Voss, mirror physical 51](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=51) records her belief both calls were collect. Keep **Voss: believed both collect / Keyton: later direct, not collect** unresolved. Shared-office retellings are not independent network evidence.

The [Commission staff report physical 94-95, notes 266-268](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=94) retains anonymous citation dates September 14 and September 4 and a watch-log lead. The new form's dates do **not** identify an unnamed/redacted witness, repair the September 4 citation, or authenticate the unacquired watch log/9:33 clock. The quoted command center in Keyton's account is not automatically the same entity as the interview-contact route or the missing watch entry. Native office/operator/transfer records remain needed for any source-to-call/exhibit join.

## 4. A separate May-family lead and duplicate-source cautions

[Part02 physical 70-71](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=70) is a separate September 12 Las Vegas-to-Dallas communication referencing **265D-NY-280350 Serial 79**. It reports a family account of flight-attendant Renee May's **6:13 a.m.** call, with no printed timezone, and a reported **six** hijackers. Neither the time/zone nor the actual count is independently established here. Do not join it to a carrier row or another witness's call by a desired conversion.

Page 71 states: **“No further investigation by Las Vegas at this time, this lead should be considered covered.”** That is the scope of this local lead, not the end of the FBI case, all telephone analysis, or an FAA administrative-action decision. Case-title bombing labels are case headings, not forensic explosives findings. Serial 79, AA receiving records, carrier evidence and the actual disposition trail remain TODOs; nothing here establishes concealment or coordination.

Part01 physical 20 is another Jefferson first page; part02 physical 24/56 are similar Woodward first pages with different redaction/footers. Their continuations 21/25/57 were not visually read. Do not count the reproductions as independent corroboration, claim full-document byte/content equivalence, or use one release to fill another's redactions. Three context pages and their similarities are source-dependence cautions, not additional wrongdoing evidence.

## What is still open

The next discriminating records are the reported original diskette/workbooks and paper attachments, installed ACIS field/clock rules, operator notes/logs and source-to-worksheet/exhibit joins. Keyton notes/audio/office records, the actual September 20 analysis, complete request/production chain, zero-duration exhibit provenance and Serial 79 remain TODOs. We have advanced TEL02/TEL03/TEL04 source provenance without closing any of the original 18 requirements.

FAA correction/delivery/signed-action and recommendation-disposition originals remain open. The technical owner retains recorder/materials native-input and empirical tests. Steel preservation/custody/attribution and contractor-original records remain open. Publication and source hash checks are not historical validation, independent audit acceptance or substantive goal completion. [Open inputs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-dee472a04428afe915e9), [source/page/paragraph pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2a8d36fa28b7aa408adc), [request checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-35cb375e1668547b48e9), [actual additive text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-db3ce06bddc177cf615e) and [verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3ca4db9da6cb9a41bbd3).
