# A September 15 follow-up recording and an FBI 1A synopsis pointer

October 31, 2026. **TEL01-TEL04 / existing AUD016-A1, TC05-B105, TC06-B110.** A newly read part of an already preserved FBI release identifies two more specific original-record targets: a **synopsis referenced in the case's 1A section** and a **taped September 15 conversation whose copy was promised to the FBI**. The actual synopsis, recording and receipt are not recovered here. This must not be described as a recording of the September 11 in-flight call.

## Exact source and scope

[FBI September part01, physical pages 47-49](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-01-of-08/at_download/file#page=47), item [page](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-01-of-08/view), is the same official-hosted released entity acquired in the preceding study: **49 pages, 4,468,234 bytes**, SHA-256 **4760de107bef3c75f4d7629f90f7a70d2c861cb3b53dcbe37a3bf0fb768f93cb**. Access was October 31, 2026 at 22:04:22.990490 UTC. It is a public released scan, not a native recording, evidence receipt or independent authentication of the interview statements.

Three previously unread complete source images were inspected, physical 47,48,49, at 140 dpi. The adjacent ACIS report ends on 46; 47-48 are a separate FD-302 and 49 is a release information sheet. This was not a new download, new OCR, full 49-page reading, audio playback or hidden-layer examination. [Scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ba15127cb8c992af9e1e), [source/page/paragraph pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c390a4448b7829ea54ad), [source relationships](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5abb0854d2dfa43e0de8).

## 1. The later recording is a distinct evidence object

Physical47, internal page1, prints **transcription 09/22/2001**, **investigation 9/22/01** and file **265A-NY-280350-NK**. The interview subject, operator and other identifying fields are redacted. The first substantive account says the subject did **not** speak with Todd Beamer during the hijacking. Do not turn this interviewee into another independently observed in-flight recipient or reconstruct redacted names from related releases.

In body paragraph 2, the report describes a UAL contact on or about **9/14/01**, provision of a **synopsis**, and a copy of that synopsis **in 1A**. It then describes a conversation arranged for **9/15/01**, says it was **taped**, and records an agreement to provide the FBI a copy **at a later date**. These are concrete retrieval anchors for the actual synopsis and recording/custody trail.

Keep four objects separate: the September 11 passenger/operator conversation; the written synopsis; the September 15 follow-up conversation and its recording; and the September 22 FBI interview report. A recording of the follow-up could test what was recounted then, including later changes or dependencies. It would not automatically authenticate every statement about September 11, establish what the passenger actually said, or replace the original network/clock/recipient evidence.

**Promise is not receipt.** The inspected pages do not supply the promised recording, an evidence-item/barcode, a native file hash, a signed delivery record, or a confirmed disposition. A 1A reference is a file-location lead, not proof that we possess the attachment or that it survives. Failure to recover it here is not proof it never existed, was withheld or was destroyed.

## 2. Preserve the time and attribution limits

Physical47 relays an operator-conversation time of **8:45 a.m. central standard time**. Physical48 includes an attributed screaming/silence account **about 9:00 a.m.** and staying on the telephone until learning of the crash. Preserve the source wording, qualifications and redactions. The dates of the later contacts and report do not calibrate either time or establish a native call leg. No EST/EDT/Central conversion, fitted clock offset or exact interval is measured here.

The [earlier operator-source study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/29ee78c52ae09a2cadeb33b0856c654c51982a3c/investigation/broader-evidence/fbi-vault-operator-record-2026-10-31/README.md) distinguishes a conversation, silent open connection and later indirect contact. Keep those phases and their estimates beside this later relayed account. Do not use common wording or a similar time to identify the redacted operator, declare source independence, align a carrier row or select a controlling chronology.

The strongest objection to treating a similar narrative as corroboration is that the report itself describes a **synopsis and later operator conversation** in its information chain. Similarities may reflect dependence on the same account. The actual synopsis, follow-up recording, original notes and interview histories are the discriminating records. No finding of coaching, fabrication, concealment, attack participation or motive follows from the existence of a follow-up conversation.

## 3. The release's duplicate-page statement needs its own map

Physical49 is an FBI **FOIPA Deleted Page Information Sheet**. It reports **10** deleted pages and lists page labels **47,48,51,52,53,54,55,56,59,60**, each with reason **Duplicate**. The ten listed entries match the reported count. This is accounting of the printed release sheet, not independent validation of each duplicate determination.

These are the sheet's **release-page labels**. They are not established as physical PDF page numbers: physical 47-48 contain the retained interview just read, and the PDF has 49 physical pages. No original-source pagination/duplicate-to-retained-page crosswalk was acquired. Preserve both numbering systems without assuming their mapping, correcting either, or alleging an inconsistency from their numerical overlap. Neither the total nor the largest label proves the original document's length or which specific record was removed.

The sheet records a stated release-processing reason. It is not a destruction memorandum, custody disposition, evidence of deliberate removal from an archive, or a conclusion about the separate diskette/recording. Obtain the original release/page/duplicate crosswalk before interpreting completeness or treating removed duplicates as additional independent evidence.

## What advances and what remains open

The preceding [ACIS/Keyton report](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/fb0d56eeaa07cfc9348306d1cedbcbea059fc5c8/investigation/broader-evidence/fbi-keyton-acis-originals-2026-10-31/README.md) identified the paper/Excel 97 diskette and operator-attachment targets. The subsequent structural check of both official PDFs found no standard embedded attachments in the inspected indexed dictionaries; that result does not establish the fate or availability of separately held original media. No native workbook or recording was recovered in this reading either.

The new advance is narrower and specific: **the September 15 recording/copy promise, the 1A synopsis reference, the September 22 source report and the release's explicit duplicate-page labels now have inspected page/paragraph locators**. A bounded check of selected earlier telephone reports found no equivalent pointer there; this is not a claim of global historical novelty, an independent witness or an authenticated native-media acquisition.

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-809e43bf14d13bc4ea67) carries the exact synopsis/recording/receipt/release-map targets along with every original five-track requirement. The actual May 2003 correctedQFR/delivery, signed FAA administrative outcome and distinct recommendation follow-up remain missing. Recorder/materials native inputs and tests remain with the technical owner. Steel decision/custody/native inventory/attribution/contractor delivery originals remain open. None of the original 18requirements is closed by this source reading, attachment count or publication.

Raw source PDFs/images/full speech, ancillary personal/contact/financial/access/signature data and private case material are excluded from this public supplement. Earlier sources, conflicting observations, reports, shared registers/workplan and public history remain unchanged. No outreach, fee, request, filing, mailbox access, unredaction, native decoding, experiment, deletion, private push or history rewrite occurred. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-333516c6d8c9e8ceb281), [mechanical verification and limits](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8018575ea1e006a691c2), [actual addition diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e0ce52c0595b43e40939). Public commit/ref/file verification is saved separately; it does not establish historical truth or independent audit acceptance.

# FBI-hosted operator interview: speech, open connections and indirect contacts

October 31, 2026. **An official FBI-hosted release now supplies a primary interview report with GTE operator Lisa Jefferson.** It distinguishes a reported direct passenger conversation, a silent but open connection and a later Verizon-mediated contact she could not hear directly. Those distinctions matter when testing what a call duration, connection endpoint or witness time actually describes.

This is a new agency-hosted source acquisition in this work, not a claim that the historical account was previously unknown or independently confirmed. It is **not** the missing September 20 American Airlines Airphone Usage analysis, Keyton's interview, a raw network export or an authenticated source-to-exhibit worksheet. Those targets remain open.

## Source and actual coverage

The ordinary FBI Vault search for Airphone returned five titles. The [September 2001 interviews, part 08 of 08 item](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/view) exposed [this actual PDF](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file): **21 pages, 1,985,063 bytes**, SHA-256 **`a7d3d872daf33d41c993ade5ccf2e9aa5421ffb1f4cda6164d6bb712372dde41`**. The exact-title follow-up displayed zero items; that bounded index observation does not establish that the usage analysis is absent from FBI holdings or releases. Other returned September parts 01/02 remain unexamined candidate routes.

We extracted ordinary text on 21 pages only for navigation and visually read complete physical pages 3–5. **Pages 4–5 are the selected Jefferson record**; page 3 is another report's continuation and was not attributed to her. The other 18 pages were not visually read. Source identity, rendering and scope are in `SOURCES.json` and `READING-SCOPE.json`; page/paragraph pins are in `OBSERVATION-PINS.json`.

Page 4 gives a September 11, 2001 visible date and identifies her role as **GTE Airphone**; page 5 supplies the continuation name/date/page 2. The visible case field is **265D-NY-280350**. These are this record's fields, not the different M-INT/265A identifier sought for the September 20 analysis. A visible unclassified-information stamp is dated 12-14-2006; that stamp is not treated as the interview or release date. Official hosting supports the retrieved release's provenance; it does not independently verify its historical assertions or supply native interview audio.

## What the report distinguishes

| Phase | Source account and exact location | What remains unknown |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Reported direct conversation | [Page 4](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=4), body paragraphs 2–5: a caller identified himself as Todd Beamer; Jefferson reports speaking with him and hearing changing cabin activity. | Voice/caller identity, actual network event, precise aircraft-event sequence and clock are not independently authenticated here. |
| Silence while connected | [Page 5](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=5), first paragraph: after the last reported exchange, she heard nothing and kept the connection open for another 20 minutes before disconnecting. | An open connection is not automatically continuing passenger speech. Its native switch/session/leg and duration definitions are missing. |
| Later provider-mediated contact | Page 5, second paragraph: a Verizon Quality Control contact relayed information from relatives reportedly speaking with another passenger. Jefferson says she could not hear that passenger and received information second hand. | Exact routing, simultaneous legs, caller identity and which connection ended at the reported endpoint are unverified. No redacted identity or LNU surname is reconstructed. |
| Equipment indication | Page 5, end of first paragraph: tracking equipment reportedly indicated a Pittsburgh transceiver/service area. | Actual equipment logs, field definitions, service-area bounds and clock were not acquired. This is not a pinpoint aircraft location or validated flight path. |

These are statements recorded in one FBI interview report, not raw audio, switching evidence or separate independent witnesses. The firsthand/secondhand distinction is explicit in the source and must survive later summaries. Neither the silence nor the continuing provider conversation alone establishes the aircraft's physical condition or a time of passenger speech.

## Preserve the time fields and conditional arithmetic

The report gives an initial **approximately 8:45 a.m. Central** call, **seven minutes** before a described change, **another eight minutes**, and a last exchange around **9:00 a.m. Central**. It then gives **another 20 minutes** open, a callback **approximately five minutes later**, a separate **9:30 a.m. Central** callback estimate and an **approximately 9:43 a.m.** conversation/connection endpoint. The last label's clock basis is read in its paragraph context, not independently calibrated.

Two nominal calculations can be reproduced from those printed values: **8:45+7+8→9:00**, while **9:00+20+5→9:25**, beside the separately estimated **9:30** callback. `TIMING-ARITHMETIC.json` retains the inputs and conditional five-minute difference. This is not an independently measured gap: the source uses estimates and supplies no uncertainty bounds. We preserve both accounts without selecting a corrected time, assigning a cause or converting them into synchronized aircraft/network timestamps. No timezone conversion, crash-time comparison or offset fit was performed.

The next useful evidence is the operator/session and Verizon transfer record, its clock basis and the interview's underlying notes/audio. Those could show whether the statements concern different legs or different events, an estimated chronology or another explanation. None is established here.

## Effect on the existing telephone investigation

This advances **TEL02/TEL04** only through an agency-hosted primary operator record and a clearer source-specific retrieval target. It does not resolve the AA77 connected/unconnected descriptions, 154/159-second values, eight-call/seven-row presentation or the 35-of-37 reconstruction. GTE terminology cannot define AT&T/Claircom records automatically. The already reviewed GTE tables also contain repeated timestamps and uncertain operator-field readings; a name, similar minute or estimated duration does not create a unique row/leg match. Existing table and decoder checks were not rerun.

The source is relevant contrary evidence to flattening every connection interval into a continuing passenger conversation. Equally, it does not prove that a particular carrier row represents the silent interval or resolve the later endpoint. Native records are needed to distinguish those possibilities. No fabrication, deliberate alteration, suppression or historical cause is established by these interview estimates.

All earlier records and conflicting values remain unchanged. `OPEN-TODOS.json` retains the original 18 requirements and previous dependencies, adding the operator/Verizon/transceiver and actual report/version targets. FAA corrected-QFR/receipt and signed disposition, native recorder/calibration/clock, materials specimen/run/measurement and steel preservation/inventory/attribution/delivery gaps remain open or partial. No original requirement closes from this acquisition.

The original PDF, separate page rasters and raw extraction remain in the verified native store. Public notes exclude raw documents/images, financial/contact/home/work information, signatures, redacted identities and private case material. `VERIFICATION.json` separates source integrity/conditional arithmetic from historical validation; `CHECKLIST.md` records outcomes; `TEXT-CHANGES.diff` preserves actual new-document additions. `EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff` retains the first draft while qualifying the form-date/stamp wording and improving spacing; no source value changes. No outreach, fee, request, filing, mailbox/private correspondence, deletion, source overwrite, private push, merge or public-history rewrite occurred. Publication and goal completion remain separate milestones.

# Five inconsistencies: source-role tests and new transcript endpoint checks

October 31, 2026. **The strongest new result qualifies the inference of missing conversations: two actual FAA transcript terminal pages end their timed dialogue before the latest listed rerecording end labels, then continue with bare minute markers.** The original range differences remain. Exact media/channel/copy joins are still missing.

This review follows the human-authorized five-item request. It checked the [current catalogue](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/60f3d93c90374e2b50147bfb19867aceccb3ba0e/investigation/catalogue/README.md), later completed readings, source/version/failure records and actual publication receipts before new work. It does not update the catalogue concurrently. [RESULTS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-85c4e1e9d87c4cb25797) gives each proposition, previous work, genuinely new work, exact locators, classification, strongest contrary finding, named missing inputs and a falsifiable next test. [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f21c404a6f179464b368) retains source URLs/access dates/hashes and actual reading scope. Earlier reports and conflicting values remain unchanged.

## 1. Flight/file labels: a real mismatch, an unjoined lineage

The held AWA937/NARA7599161 two-page entity was reinspected. Its first page prints AAL11 in subject and certification, ZBW-ARTCC-148 in the addressee, January17,2002, TMU commercialline1078,1255–1316UTC and the first timed row1300:41/AD1. Its second page prints the work-file label `UAL175_R42_memo.doc` and footer date09/27/2001. A pagination/masthead placeholder is also visible. In the AEA1130/NARA7598367 packet, physical186 prints UAL175 and ZNY-ARTCC-148, while the selected date,line,range and first timedrow match. [AWA source study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/0584407dac2190a9f9514ee157124446e60672d5/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-tmu1078-source-2026-10-30/README.md); [packet study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e64f8f3006879bfde9fa76f1db4b9be6b514d5af/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-completed-package-original-2026-10-30/README.md).

The later [complete1078study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/8b787be27fcb665a851a42e318ccc4fd0b6117e9/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-1078-marked-passages-2026-10-30/README.md) already read all five pages186–190 and preserved48timedrows,45distinctsecondlabels and22minutemarkers. We reuse that completed work; the middle pages are **not** currently missing/unread in this source block. The AWA entity visibly contains one timed dialogue row followed by its administrative footer page; it is not the same complete rendered entity as the five-page block. That does not establish why its contents differ, a missing recording, or a common native origin. Selected first-row/header agreement cannot authenticate a full-document join.

**Classification:** confirmed subject/file/work-file contrasts; native lineage unresolved. A ground ATC line may concern several aircraft, and subject-specific transcription/certification can select material. Template reuse, a different subject selection, assembly and version differences remain testable alternatives, not resolved causes. Needed: native Word/enclosure/version identifiers, selection instructions, actual source channel and assembly/distribution records. Neither flight label is preferred.

## 2. Retention: keep the printed destruction date beside the hold

AEA1130physical1 prints **DESTROY: March11,2004**. The [September14,2001instruction](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/45ee3925dc1edd3137cf85182570bfc8440afcac/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-retention-directive-2026-10-30/README.md), AEA2579physical1, says retain and secure administrative/operational data until further notice and retain when uncertain. Its temporal/facility scope and differing OIG-reported interpretations remain preserved. A label is not an executed disposal record, and the OIG investigation of another locally created tape does not establish this package's disposition.

The already-read [2005specialschedule](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/8bc80894a40ac207b2ccce62a9fdd6d22d4c1139/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-911-special-schedule-2026-10-30/README.md) separates permanent original records/audio re-recordings from temporary website-image backups, convenience/generation and additional dissemination/revision copies. Its60-day and180-day periods have different classes and triggers. It **cannot by itself establish authorization in2004 or withdrawal of the2001hold**. Original tape, re-recording, accident package, recordkeeping copy, convenience copy and surviving scan cannot silently be treated as one object.

**Classification:** qualified retention tension; governing applicability and actual disposition unresolved. The surviving scan and permanent-original class are contrary evidence to a universal no-surviving-copy claim, without proving an original tape survived. Named TODOs are the applicable historical authority, copy classification, hold receipt/withdrawal/exception, custody/transfer and actual disposition records. No source, schedule or date is normalized into a finding of lawful or unlawful disposal. No deletion occurred.

## 3. End labels versus actual terminal pages: a new qualification

The [Request2Item16list](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/62a102da31cc82a7216dc41e60bed185a11c8d45/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-response-labels-2026-10-30/README.md) preserves these conditional pairings. Physical15/printed8 points onward to Item20; physical16/printed9 includes the Boston entry, transcript labels and further cross-references. It is not the complete production manifest.

| Conditional role/start pairing | Listed rerecording ranges | Transcript range | Last printed timed dialogue row in held counterpart | Terminal minute label |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| NewYorkOperationsManager |1230–1331;1331–1340|1230–1344|**1338:24**, newly read physical173/printed26of26|1344|
| NewYorkTMU1245start |1245–1314|1245–1316|**1310:37**, newly read physical185/printed9of9|1316|
| NewYorkTMU1255start/1078candidate |1255–1315|1255–1316|**1310:14**, prior completed block physical190, ledgerP190R05|1316|
| BostonMilitaryOperationsSpecialist |1232–1259|1232–1259|Not examined here|Matching declared1259label|

Physical173 has bare markers1339–1344 and EndofTranscript. Physical185 has bare1311–1316markers and EndofTranscript; its top case header is partly occluded, and the visibleUAL175/agency/page fields and prior cover mapping are retained without reconstructing covered characters. The existing1078ledger retains its final row and following markers. The two endpoint candidates were frozen before viewing; their actual printed labels confirmed these selected terminal-page mappings. Other middle pages remain unread. Our checked lineage now covers20distinctAEA1130pageimages, leaving217unread; that is not a full-package or completeOMIC/TMU-DCreading.

**Classification:** confirmed printed-range differences, with a new contrary endpoint observation. Under the conditional same-source/day/clock/role pairing, each latest listed rerecording end is later than the last printed timed dialogue row. Thus the4/2/1minute label differences alone do not establish omitted conversations. They also do not prove acoustic silence, full coverage or faithful transcription. Preserve every label and the matchingBostoncontrol; do not add label differences into missing audio. Native/source/channel/copy/generation identity, completeItem20enclosures, actual media and check worksheets remain TODOs. No audio was acquired, listened to, decoded or aligned here.

## 4. Moussaoui: test the actual information available, not document counts

[636/637and639](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/3f50f2c6904eca6a39efa4c06c15ea838729a65d/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-reply-update-2026-10-30/README.md) preserve possible-link concern, reported field/headquarters disagreement, caution about indications of planned wrongdoing and reported dissemination. Possible association and evidence of a concrete plot are different propositions. The exact earlier communication referenced in639 remains unidentified.

[346](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/d39c2a1f666f298df0cbea3115349075135e82b9/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-french-reply-controls-2026-10-31/README.md) adds an August22dated attributedFrenchaccount; attention fields do not prove receipt.59B/642 contain a later supplement reportedly receivedAugust29 and an August30cover explicitly warning that its attachment is a draft, the translation rough and upload future. The source interview's older date does not backdate U.S. availability of that supplement toAugust22/24. [643/644](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/77fd80202f5d6b01a98eb4a7f59c798690004bf1/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-france-followup-2026-10-31/README.md) add planning, reported checking and translation assertions, with their authorship/header/version limits. The separately published [327review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/591ed5873ce7895f47c092f83f244d98efa18154/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-aug22-dissemination-2026-10-31/README.md) adds an August22field aircraft-attack assessment and explicit requested fieldwork. It is not silently selected as639's unidentified earlier message.

**Classification:** qualified assessment contrast; knowledge, exact inputs and follow-through unjoined. Reported sharing, permission, inquiries and continued planning weigh against a blanket claim that all investigative work stopped. Different inputs/questions/thresholds, tentative or inaccurate source information, later supplementation and substantive returns could weaken a knowing-exclusion inference. Conversely, a matched received-version/review/decision chain showing knowing material exclusion or misstatement would strengthen it. No such chain was newly acquired. DRAFT-T01–T06 and the underlying intelligence/advice/receipt/decision/return targets remain open.

## 5. Printed times and authorship: preserve the fields without inventing a clock

The existing [643/644comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/77fd80202f5d6b01a98eb4a7f59c798690004bf1/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-france-followup-2026-10-31/README.md) is preserved:644index **12:58a** versus PDFp1 **12:58PM**;643quotedAbbottparent **10:31AM** versus642cover **11:32AM**;643index planning description versus its reply's plan-in-works wording;644p2 **ScottB** signoff without a separate visibleFrom/To/Dateheader. The cached index's raw byte-line locators are562/572 for643/644. Index descriptions, quoted parents, displayed covers and assembled pages are different source roles.

**Classification:** source-layer contrasts and attribution gap confirmed in the held record; native chronology unjoined. No preferred time, calibrated61minuteclock offset, common native parent, misassembly or attribution of all644text toSamit follows. Native messageIDs, complete headers/timezones, parent/attachment versions, recipient receipts and court assembly/index history remain TODOs.

The prior suspected opposite custody instruction in59B/642 is **not** another unresolved word conflict: high-resolution inspection found **not** in both clauses. Preserve that failed suspicion and correction as an agent reading error; neither source was changed or adjudicated into agreement. Earlier literalOCRfailures likewise remain recorded, not silently repaired into an all-passed result.

## Verification and publication scope

New work was two previously unread endpoint images, two heldAWAimage reinspections and the five-item cross-source/role/availability analysis. The completed1078ledger and prior full readings were reused, not repeated as independent validation. No new source download, hidden-layer extraction, OCR, masked-field reconstruction, legal determination, native-message authentication, acoustic/clock test, outreach, fee, request, filing, private push, deletion or history rewrite occurred.

[compare_endpoints.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-10ab85ec47f61634e1cb) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d6c21f23d1858783e74b) reproduce only the three conditional comparisons from manually checked endpoint fields and the prior ledger. Hashes/reference checks establish copy identity to their stated scope, not independent scientific or primary-source validation. All18original acceptance criteria remain unchanged and incomplete.

### August 22 dissemination and investigative leads: exhibit 327

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY f9ad884615f0b296663c7093ed05ebb8403d21dbad824996cfca4ab8392781d3 -->
# August 22 dissemination and investigative leads: exhibit 327

Research date: October 31, 2026. **The court copy supplies an eight-page FBI communication with explicit investigative leads and a stated aircraft-attack suspicion before September 11. It does not establish actual recipient reading, completed task returns, a verified directing group or deliberate obstruction.** This is a new acquisition and reading in this investigation, not a claim that the document was previously unknown publicly.

The [official court exhibit](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=1), [source identities](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2e371d2d1b375af6f9de), [page/Bates pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7bfd2f0bbbfc1db3e8cc), [unresolved comparisons](CITATIONS.md#nov04-75ef4a210fc79df83b79) and [carried TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b2b47852d8b2329760f4) preserve the source and its limits. All eight physical pages were visually inspected. The original PDF remains unchanged in the verified source store. This new directory adds notes; it replaces no earlier source or public assessment.

## What the communication actually records

**Distribution and task setting.** Page 1 is marked PRIORITY, dated 08/22/2001, and originates in Minneapolis. It lists Counterterrorism, Oklahoma City and Memphis, references case 199M-MP-60130, Serial 3, and says the information was discussed in August 21 telephone calls. The full-field-investigation field says 08/15/2001. Page 8 sets three leads: headquarters at ITOS/RFU receives an information lead; Oklahoma City receives an identity-coordination task plus investigation into possible terrorist or criminal activity; Memphis receives an identity-coordination task. These are visible destinations and requested work, not dispatch logs, acknowledged receipt, the exact package a legal reviewer examined, or proof of completed work. [Pages 1 and 8](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=8), AD01 and AD07.

**Suspicion is explicit; the directing group is not established.** Page 2 reports Minneapolis's belief that Moussaoui was preparing for international terrorism. Page 7 says Minneapolis opines that Moussaoui and Al-Attas were preparing an attack involving a Boeing 747-400, directed by an as-yet-unknown international group. Those are contemporaneously dated investigator assessments in this court representation. They are not an independently verified operational plan, a demonstrated al-Qaeda link, proof of a specific target/date, or evidence that every recipient knew an attack would occur. Page 7 also alleges that associates deliberately downplayed the threat. Preserve that allegation as the author's interpretation, not a finding independently established here. [Pages 2 and 7](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=7), AD02.

**The supporting accounts are disputed and qualified within the memo.** Pages 4–5 report an interviewee's account of ordinary flight training, peaceful study, denial of violent discussions, and a practical explanation for arranging bail. Pages 6–7 argue that inconsistencies and other information suggest deception and involvement. Page 4 says investigators are trying to establish an identity match; page 8 repeats that task. Part of the comparison is masked. Page 6's criminal-history argument itself includes a probability qualification. The investigator's interpretation, the reported contrary account and the unresolved identity question all remain visible. Neither this reading nor similarity of names resolves them. Underlying interviews, database returns and identity-resolution records would be needed. [Pages 4–8](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=4), AD03–AD04.

**A telephone-record lead has a specific source chain.** Page 3 reports that a detention-unit sergeant supplied a call log: 13 attempts between 1:07 p.m. and 4:14 p.m., with the first 12 refused, busy or unanswered and a final conversation described as heated. The language and tone are reported characterizations; the memo says the officer presumed the language. Pages 6–7 refer back to the same calling pattern. This is not 13 demonstrated completed conversations, a translated recording, an independent carrier return or several independent corroborations. No telephone number, address, birth date or other unnecessary personal identifier is reproduced in these notes. The original log, event definitions, any recording and the underlying interview are concrete TODOs. [Page 3](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=3), AD05.

**Training and custody descriptions need their source records.** Page 2 reports a request for 747-400 instruction sufficient to take off and land. That wording is preserved; no contradictory source is invented or selected to replace it. Original school records, instructor interviews and actual requested lessons would test it. Page 3 reports a conditional estimate of deportation and return of property unless investigative progress was made. An estimate is not an executed deportation, an authorization to search, a completed property return or the later France proposal's disposition. [Pages 2–3](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/327.pdf#page=2), AD06–AD07.

## How this fits the existing trail

The [earlier French-report study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/d39c2a1f666f298df0cbea3115349075135e82b9/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-french-reply-controls-2026-10-31/README.md) preserved attributed intelligence, draft warnings and questions about exact review packages. The [France-option follow-up](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/77fd80202f5d6b01a98eb4a7f59c798690004bf1/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-france-followup-2026-10-31/README.md) preserved proposed alternatives and conflicting time labels. Those prior records and values remain unchanged; they were not newly reauthenticated or completely reread in this batch.

Exhibit 327 adds a different retrieval path: its named field-office tasks, headquarters information lead, Serial 3 reference, August 21 telephone contacts and source-log attribution. **The existence of investigative tasks weighs against a blanket assertion that nobody initiated or requested any investigation.** It does not show that the tasks were sufficient or fulfilled. Conversely, a task without a return in these eight pages is not evidence that no return existed. Nor does an information lead establish that headquarters prohibited action elsewhere.

The memo's August 15 initiation date, August 21 telephone contacts and August 22 face date describe different events. They are retained as printed; their difference alone is not a chronology conflict. The 13 attempts and 12 unsuccessful attempts likewise are related counts, not a demonstrated numerical contradiction. The actual unresolved disputes concern the reported accounts, identity association, supporting evidence, receipt and follow-through.

## Next discriminating records, kept as TODOs

1. Native EC/ACS record, serial history, package/enclosure manifest, dispatch and recipient acknowledgments; matched legal-review copies if any.
2. Oklahoma City and Memphis lead assignments, responses, identity-resolution records and closure/disposition entries. Compare the requested task with each actual return, including contrary or negative results.
3. Serial 3 and original August 21 telephone-contact records; the original detention call log, outcome-code definitions, preserved audio if any, and source interview/translation records.
4. Original training request, school/instructor records and the actual interview statements underlying both the suspicion and the contrary explanation.
5. Any actual custody/property/removal decision and execution record, kept separate from estimates and later proposed alternatives.
6. Exhibit 633: acquired and preserved from its actual court-index link, but its one-page body was **not read in this batch**. Its index label is not a substitute for reading it or a basis for claiming agreement/disagreement.

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b2b47852d8b2329760f4) carries DRAFT-T01–T06 and their prior missing-input wording forward. All 18 original FAA/telephone/recorder/materials/steel requirements remain open or partial; none is completed by this source review. No new outreach, request, fee or filing was made. The failed FAA-catalog route remains a separate local search result, not evidence of suppression and not this public finding.

## Confidence, alternatives and verification limits

High confidence is limited to the readable wording, page order and fields of the acquired court PDF. Confidence in actual delivery, completeness, underlying allegation accuracy and historical action is unresolved. The court host supplies provenance for this released representation; a hash identifies these downloaded bytes, not the unproduced native EC, original interviews or transmission chain.

A deliberate-obstruction interpretation would need a matched record showing material information was received and knowingly misrepresented, withheld or left unacted on for an improper reason. Contemporaneous fieldwork, substantive task returns, a mistaken source/identity inference, different actual review inputs, or documented legitimate decisions could weaken it. These remain possible discriminating outcomes, not explanations adopted to resolve the conflicts. The memo alone establishes neither a conspiracy nor the adequacy of the investigation.

Actual work: two source downloads; eight newly read pages in 327; 633 preserved but unread; passive page/attachment-name checks and source/render hash checks. No OCR, hidden-layer extraction, masked-field recovery, native-email authentication, audio decoding, independent interview verification, full source-to-source text diff, legal-sufficiency determination or counterfactual prevention test was performed. An empty embedded-attachment-name list does not prove there were no historical enclosures. Reading every page does not certify every faint character; masked content remains unknown.

[SOURCE-CHECKS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-50631e471957c8beb4ee) records preservation checks, not historical validation. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1713f6cc6ce3aec81974) accounts for the request. [TEXT-CHANGES.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-6d914d1b88f62023ee2b) is the actual Git addition diff against this directory's preceding absent state; it is not an FBI draft/version diff. Raw PDFs, rasters, full transcriptions, signatures, private case material, personal/contact data and credentials are excluded from publication. Public commit/ref/body verification is recorded separately after execution; publication does not close the substantive investigation.

### August 30 follow-ups: France-option planning, investigative reports and unresolved time labels

# August 30 follow-ups: France-option planning, investigative reports and unresolved time labels

Research date: October 31, 2026. Existing DRAFT-T01–T06 remain open. Following the [earlier French-source review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/d39c2a1f666f298df0cbea3115349075135e82b9/investigation/broader-evidence/moussaoui-french-reply-controls-2026-10-31/README.md), court exhibits **643 and 644** were acquired from the actual links in the [official defense-exhibit index](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense.html). All three new physical pages were visually inspected; held exhibit642 page1 was reinspected for comparison. Originals and previous findings remain unchanged.

The useful addition is a recorded reply describing a plan to meet French interest, plus source-attributed investigative and proposed immigration/banking follow-ups. These distinguish continuing activity from an executed search, removal or final decision. They do not supply the missing native emails, original intelligence, translations, orders or results.

## FP01 — A reply describes a France-related plan, not its execution

[643, physical page1](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/643.pdf#page=1), marked **M-MP1-88000424**, displays Harry Samit to W.Jay Abbott, August30 at **11:55AM**. Samit thanks Abbott, describes a plan in the works to meet French interest, and promises an update. The quoted parent describes French interest in further exploring deportation/transport to France, permission to share information with the British, and a draft/rough-translation/upload qualification. This is a source-level reply/acknowledgment representation; it does not establish that the complete attached EC was received, finalized, legally approved or acted upon.

The earlier59B/642 conditional escort/evidence proposal and custody qualification remain preserved in the prior review. The reply adds reported planning, not a completed France option. A native parent/reply/attachment association, actual plan, recommendation/response and disposition would discriminate between continued planning, revision, abandonment or execution. No outcome is selected here.

## FP02 — Two time-label contrasts remain unresolved

| Records compared | Values preserved | Scope and limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [644 index description](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense.html) versus [644 page1 header](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/644.pdf#page=1) | Index **12:58a**; displayed email header **12:58PM**, August30 | Navigation metadata and a rendered email header are different source layers. Their cause and the actual send time are not established |
| [643 page1 quoted Abbott header](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/643.pdf#page=1) versus [642 page1 displayed cover header](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/642.pdf#page=1) | **08/30/01 10:31AM** versus **Thu, Aug30,2001 11:32AM** | The readable cover bodies are similar; native Message-ID/version/forwarding identity and timezone are unverified. This is not an authenticated elapsed interval or calibrated clock offset |

Both accounts are retained without choosing a controlling value. Editorial description, differing message/version or rendering/clock conventions are untested possibilities. The evidence needed is the native parent/reply and forwarding chain, headers/timezones, transport/recipient records and original-to-court assembly history. The index/body contrast alone does not establish altered evidence or what any investigator received.

A smaller wording contrast also remains: the index describes643 using an additional planning step, whereas the visible reply says a plan is already in the works. That is a description-versus-body observation; neither wording establishes an implemented plan. [CONFLICTS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-67252472887620f429bc) preserves the short source strings and byte-line locators from the cached index.

## FP03 — Allegations, reported inquiry and proposed action are different propositions

[644, physical page1](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/644.pdf#page=1), marked **M-MP1-88000425**, displays Samit to Scott Billings. Samit recounts a roughly translated Al-Attas telephone conversation and will, reports additional checking, draws strong conclusions about intent, and advocates prompt immigration action if a basis arises. These are the author's reports and interpretations. The original audio, original-language will, translator notes/method and the claimed checking are not attached or independently validated here. Religious wording, anxiety or a forceful investigative characterization does not itself complete the inferred-plan test; the underlying context and competing readings remain necessary.

[644, physical page2](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/defense/644.pdf#page=2), marked **M-MP1-88000426**, ends with a **Scott B.** signoff but has no separate visible From/To/Date header. It mentions possible bank-record NSLs and reports a university visit with INS to check enrollment, followed by a conditional future status check. This is a source-reported inquiry, not the underlying school/bank/INS records or a completed NSL, status determination, removal or search. Relative yesterday/Friday wording is not silently converted into fixed dates while the native message association is missing. Specific financial/contact/personal details are omitted from this derivative.

The page2 signoff also prevents treating every sentence of this two-page exhibit as independently authenticated Samit-authored text. An included earlier message is a plausible reading, but the native thread and page-assembly record are unacquired. No misassembly, identity reconstruction or historical falsification is established.

## Evidentiary strength and remaining originals

These records support the narrower source-level account that field planning and inquiry continued. They weigh against a blanket claim that no activity followed the French information. They do not independently establish the truth of the reported allegations, the adequacy of the inquiry, inclusion in a warrant-review package or an executed alternative. A documented completed or declined action, contrary translation, unsupported attribution or different reviewed package could materially change the assessment; none is selected as the explanation.

The repeated Abbott passage is not a second independent intelligence source. Court hosting, stamps, production marks and hashes identify these representations, not native email transport, historical truth or approval. Similar prose is not proof of a byte-identical attachment or that later information was available earlier.

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a39ded924d70d22de4a3) carries all six prior DRAFT IDs and their earlier missing-input wording forward, alongside the new plan/attribution/time tests. The original eighteen five-track input requirements remain open or partial. This broader operational-record step neither closes them nor duplicates the technical owner's recorder/materials work.

## Sources, reading and revision accountability

643 is **61,911bytes /1page**, SHA-256 **f69602cf016f31a6060c90c174108a5ea34e1ebced8f2f90df307337ff3dccc8**. 644 is **136,111bytes /2pages**, SHA-256 **9c0c63a7f8cb81f370d2b2a4ba5d32ca8c3824e18300a672d23bb27c1447e541**. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-215ba761f83439bf92f0) records actual access UTC/NewYork time, URLs, roles and the reused index identity. The held642 PDF hash remains **6968155013d3d53aa6c26c14338310fc6cb603552b9cf3836822bf70a7d96b0f**; its acquisition is not new.

All three new pages were viewed at1800pixels on the longest dimension; one held642 page was reinspected. Faint/redacted material remains qualified. No OCR, hidden-layer extraction, masked-identity reconstruction, exhaustive word/pixel comparison, native-message/clock authentication, legal sufficiency adjudication or physical test was performed. Passive PDF inspection exposed no embedded attachment names; that does not establish the absence of historical attachments.

[SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c057c40672106541e11b) gives physical pages, visible headers/paragraphs/production marks, cached-index byte-line positions and this report's line references; no raster source-line numbers are invented. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3c1d12af9469c9c7b822), the editorial [EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-82e9c563f1596127740d), and [TEXT-CHANGES.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b518b5be926ef97278dd) record the actual new-text addition against the immediately preceding absent state. Prior reports, source order, masks, annotations, conflicting values and TODOs remain unchanged; source/layout reading is separate from the text diff.

The public payload contains original research notes and locators only. Raw PDFs/renders, signatures, specific financial/contact/personal data, private case correspondence/strategy and credentials are excluded. No outreach, fee, new request/filing, mailbox access, deletion, source overwrite, private push, merge or history rewrite occurred. AveLyra publication/ref/all-file verification follows the frozen candidate separately; publication does not complete the underlying historical questions.

Editorial review relabeled inherited642 acquisition/reading fields as prior-unit metadata and explicitly records only page1 reinspected here. This corrects our scope description, leaves source bytes and conflicting time labels unchanged, and is preserved in the version diff.

# Candidate Alderson transcript: a limited version comparison

October 31, 2026. A newly located [public preview](https://www.scribd.com/document/14142047/NYC-Box-3-Neads-conr-norad-Fdr-Transcript-Neads-Channel-2-Mcc-Upside-006) has a cover attributing its transcription to Alderson Reporting. It provides a concrete version-tracing lead after the [interview notes](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048-doc8.pdf#page=17) warned of missing calls and timing that needed verification. **The native PDF, exact version pair and completed timing verification remain missing.**

## What the preview adds

The cover labels the recording **DRM1/DAT2, Channel2, MCC Upside**. This remains beside the **DRM2/DAT2 Channel2** heading in the [earlier reproduction](https://www.scribd.com/document/14142009/NYC-Box-2-NEADS-Transcript-Rome-NY-Fdr-Transcript-Pgs-1-82-1237-1417), printed50/NCT0000098. The official [MFR page3](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00771.pdf#page=3) names a DRM1/DAT2/Channel2 MCCOp review with an Alderson transcript. None of these labels is selected as controlling. A matching label does not authenticate this as the copy staff reviewed. Exact fields and locations are retained in `COMPARISON-PINS.json`, AV01–02.

A limited common passage is visible in new viewer26, internal sections0061–0063. The earlier copy's printed62–63/NCT0000110–111 contains the same recognizable sequence: AA11 reported still airborne, a conditional pursuit, a spoken direction correction, a Langley scramble instruction and adjacent N334AA discussion. Speaker categories and pagination differ. This is **documentary overlap**, not a complete version diff, a unique source-audio match, or independent proof that the reported aircraft was airborne. AV03 records exactly which portions were compared.

This overlap is relevant contrary evidence to an expansive claim that Alderson removed the whole AA11/Langley sequence. It does **not** determine whether the different, specifically alleged ZNY/positioning call was omitted. That call was not tested in this unit. Two reproductions may share the same underlying tape and must not be counted as independent witnesses.

## Conflicting labels and time fields remain uncorrected

| Observation | Exact source location | Meaning still unverified |
| --- | --- | --- |
| New cover: DRM1/DAT2; earlier heading: DRM2/DAT2 | New viewer1/internal0001–0002; prior printed50/NCT0000098 | Actual recording, copy and channel relationship. The held MP3's DRM1 filename is another label, not authentication. |
| Header `Ch2MCCUpsideDRM1DAT220031219[1]` | New viewer1 running header | The date-like suffix does not establish creation, export, review or revision date. |
| `(12 hours 30 minutes 35 seconds)`; `00:00:13 (Elapsed time) 03:02:00`; marginal `00:00:04` | New viewer1, internal0002 lines2–3 and left margin | Multiple time-like fields are present. No duration, clock origin, timezone or alignment is assigned. |
| Earlier common exchange: `13:22:05`, `13:22:27`, `13:22:47`; adjacent `13:23:10` | Prior printed62–63/TR04–05 | These remain source labels. No equivalent absolute-time label was adopted from the new common-passage viewport. No offset is calculated. |
| Notes require a post-about9:10 timing redo; the MFR reports a transcript/audio review | Notes17; MFR3 | A review statement and readable preview are not the actual redo, its results or evidence of correction delivery. |

The [preceding MFR comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6af556052ee0745e95151faa13294e864decdd89/investigation/broader-evidence/neads-mfr-version-crosswalk-2026-10-31/README.md) preserves the notes' specific warning beside its absence from the fully read six-page summary. This unit does not establish why those records differ or whether corrections were completed. Editorial differences, different copies, reconstruction and improper omission are untested possibilities; none is adopted as an explanation.

## Actual reading and preservation

The platform advertises93 pages. We inspected cover/first-section viewports on viewer1, a partial top view on viewer2 and **one limited common-passage viewport on viewer26**. Viewer26 showed0061 lines15–25,0062 and0063 through aboutline10; its lower part was outside the viewport or obscured by an advertisement. We did not read all93 pages or all of page26. The earlier copy's printed50/62–63 findings reuse the previously recorded partial reading, with retained selected web text reread; they are not claimed as newly acquired native pages. The previously fully read18-page notes and six-page MFR were reused.

Two unchanged browser JPEGs were preserved separately in the verified native media store:

| Representation | Bytes | SHA-256 |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Cover viewport | 97,504 | `4ab7824d516cc54610ddb65901ad3448bae18160d8729256224ef1e52ed895d6` |
| Common-passage viewport26 | 126,575 | `d33e2697691520903a98885e8c13a932620119936702dcefdf2e1ddd23d3945d` |

These hashes identify **our screenshots**, not a native transcript PDF or an authenticated historical master. `SOURCES.json` distinguishes browser representations from the two official PDFs actually acquired in prior work. The platform's AI title, upload information and displayed licence are not provenance. A handwritten ownership-like mark is not authenticated or adopted. Native downloads led to signup and were not used; the inspected public reading viewports displayed no reading gate. No login, trial, hidden endpoint or access workaround was used.

## Next discriminating records

MC-T01 and MFR-T02 advance only to **public candidate preview located**. Obtain the actual custodian transcript versions, copy/channel manifests and the exact allegedly missing call. Then compare their coverage and seek the post9:10 redo worksheet, validated transcript and delivery history. A completed, propagated correction could weaken an integrity concern; established known uncorrected errors in the actual relied-on version could strengthen a narrower one. This preview establishes neither result.

`OPEN-TODOS.json` preserves all prior MC/MFR/OM dependencies and the original18requirements. The corrected May2003 FAA QFR and receipt, signed action/no-action decision, recommendation-disposition chain and other telephone/recorder/materials/steel inputs remain open/partial. No native audio decoding, clock fit, experiment or technical-owner work was duplicated.

`CHECKLIST.md` accounts for the user requirements; `READING-SCOPE.json` separates viewed regions from unreviewed text; `SOURCE-CHECKS.json` reports actual representation checks; `TEXT-CHANGES.diff` records these additive text changes. Prior sources, conflicting values, reports and public history remain unchanged. Raw screenshots, marked pages/full speech, ancillary personnel/contact/access details and private case material are outside this public payload. No outreach, request, fee, filing, deletion, private push, merge or history rewrite occurred. Publication is a separate milestone, not substantive goal completion.

# NEADS command interview notes: transcript omissions, timing verification and information sources

Reviewed October 31, 2026. This supplement follows the [newly read FAA Operations Manager exchanges](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/dcdc9f647130139c60128ae3c131d40955536b34/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-omic-body-completion-2026-10-31/README.md). A newly acquired official release gives a concrete source-version warning and a more specific reported path for the American 11 still-airborne information. It does **not** identify the rumor's ultimate origin, establish deliberate deception or authenticate the underlying call clocks.

## The most useful new record

On [physical/printed page 17](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048-doc8.pdf#page=17), typed interview notes state that a ZNY/fighter-positioning call and some following conversations appear in an **original transcript** but not the **Alderson transcript**, although the communications are clear. A separate bold note says that, from around **9:10 forward**, the Alderson **MCC OP Ch. 2** times **“must be redone and verified.”**

This is direct documentary support for retrieving and comparing the two versions and the actual verification work. It is not our completed measurement of an omission or clock error. We have not established which versions those labels denote, whether the warning was later acted on, or whether either is the reproduced NCT transcript or held MP3. A corrected final version may exist; this note cannot prove that it does not.

The same page attributes the American 11 still-airborne/toward-Washington information **to the identification section as the reported source to the mission crew commander**. It separately describes Battle Cab's role in a Langley decision. These are distinct source/decision steps. They narrow the next retrieval to ID-section inputs, verification/correction messages and matched calls; they do not establish the originating source or a direct join to the FAA's printed 1326/1329 exchanges.

## Source, form and reading scope

- [Official NARA ISCAP 2011-048 index](https://www.archives.gov/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048.html), row *Interview of Kevin Nasypany*, dated **2004/01/22**; release label **9/29/2014**.
- [Released document 8](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048-doc8.pdf): **18 pages**, **13,154,124 bytes**; SHA-256 `598711f6f1236a0ad30f1dbe7f9c1a2cd43507b4c12fdb18b3283e3472d1ea4e`. Ordinary public acquisition returned 200; original and readback fingerprints match. Exact acquisition times are in `SOURCES.json`.
- All **18 whole-page images** were actually viewed. Pages 1–14 are handwritten excerpt/question/interview/continuation material; pages 15–18 are typed bullet interview notes. Page 18 is sparse, with one substantive bullet and two empty markers, rather than blank. Unclear handwriting, masks and initials were not reconstructed. This is not a certified verbatim transcription of every mark.
- NARA separately indexes **NAID 2610370**, *Nasypany, Kevin J.*, **1/22–23/2004**, **six pages**. That is a separate MFR locator. Its body and page/byte relationship to this 18-page mixed packet were not acquired or established here. We do not silently equate the two.

Official hosting establishes the provenance of this released scan. It does not by itself establish the author/date of every note, assembly completeness, accurate recollection or the truth and timing of the source events. The catalogue date, internal interview labels, recording/excerpt labels and 2014 release date are different kinds of evidence.

## Other findings and qualifications

| Pin / exact page anchor | Preserved record | What it means and what remains unknown |
| --- | --- | --- |
| MC03; handwritten 9–10 and typed 15, communications/bridge bullets | Notes distinguish FAA communications via ID/WD; possible WOC/Herndon bridge access; an attributed personal statement of not joining; possible Battle Cab access; and no personal Air Threat participation/monitoring. Page 9 reports no NORAD/CMOC conversation; page 15 says he could not communicate with CMOC. | **Capability, individual conduct and organizational participation differ.** Neither possible access nor one individual's denial proves whether another position joined. Native bridge/call-leg/recipient records are still needed. |
| MC04; typed 15, chat-log bullets | Multiple logs and different read/write rights are described. Real-world events reportedly entered an **exercise chat log**; some data, including RADES material, **may** have been entered after 9/11. | A log's title is not sufficient to classify all its entries or prove contemporaneous entry. Later reconstruction is not automatically fabrication. Retrieve originals, schema, entry/event timestamps, source data and version history. |
| MC05; typed 15–16, awareness/ID-feed and Z-point/primary-return bullets | Favorable situation-awareness and extremely fast internal information-flow assessments coexist with not hearing the initial call, an interviewer challenging the FAA-provided Z-point, and qualification that a believed AA11 primary observation may not have been that aircraft. | Preserve both the praise and limitations. These concern different scopes; they do not independently measure latency or prove one global contradiction. A staff comment is not an independent radar test. |
| MC06; handwritten 9/14 and typed 16, press-release bullet | January labels **1/22/04** and **1/23** remain beside the typed literal **9/18/04** NORAD press-release reference and **0843 FAA notifies NEADS** wording, which the notes attribute Nasypany as challenging. | The literal date is **not repaired to 2001**. Note creation/assembly date and the actual referenced release/version remain unverified. The attributed challenge is not our independently established timing result. |
| MC07; handwritten 8 versus typed 16 | Page 8 places **925** beside Otis CAP over NYC and an approximate arrival/second-tower-hit line. Typed 16 reports UAL175 notification coinciding with notification of the second hit. | Preserve the awkward juxtaposition; the handwritten referent/time basis is uncertain. No preferred value, inferred offset or calibrated event interval is selected. |
| MC08; page 9 header and packet form | A handwritten header has **Moynahan** crossed out and **Kevin Nasypany** above. Earlier pages contain questions, excerpt references and other attributed accounts; typed 15 explicitly heads the Nasypany interview. | Do not attribute every earlier quotation or question to the interview subject. Reused notes, draft correction and composite assembly are possible explanations, not established causes or proof of substitution. |
| MC09; typed 15–16, record-gathering/AAR/memorialization bullets | Recalled gathering of information for Battle Cab coexists with descriptions of group discussion, no after-action review and no remembered memorialization attempt. | Collection, discussion, formal review and memorialization are distinct. No inference that notes never existed or that this is the separately destroyed FAA cassette. |
| MC10; handwritten 3–7/12–14 and typed 17 | Other material flags readiness/order/routing questions, a visible shoot-down/intercept wording change, VP-directive/ROE terminology, and qualified expectations about Norfolk/Giant Killer/AFIO and control. | These notes are not authenticated readiness/weapons orders or measured execution. A changed note wording is not proof an operational order changed. Neither advance knowledge, a real shoot-down nor intentional obstruction is established. |

## What could weaken a suspicious reading

The strongest relevant objection is that the interview/review process was explicitly identifying uncertain information and requesting transcript correction. If the versions and worksheets show the corrections were performed and accurately propagated, that would weaken a concealment interpretation while leaving the earlier error documented. Conversely, a source-supported unexplained omission, known uncorrected error or incompatible final account could strengthen a specific integrity concern. These tests have not been performed here.

The typed notes describe review of chat logs and recordings, including tape playback. That is a recorded interview-review activity, not merely an unprompted recollection; the exact reviewed media and versions remain unacquired here, and we did not independently replay or validate them.

Mixed real/exercise entries or later RADES additions could be ordinary logging/reconstruction practices; they could also impair historical reconstruction if mislabeled or undocumented. We need actual entries, definitions and edit history. A label alone establishes neither an innocent explanation nor fabrication. These notes and later narratives may depend on the same recordings/logs and are not independent corroboration simply because they appear in different documents.

## Next records and unchanged limits

`SOURCE-PINS.json` records MC01–MC10 with exact page URLs, visible anchors, selected literal wording, source hash and limits. `OPEN-TODOS.json` retains the original 18 requirements and earlier OM dependencies, adding actual transcript versions/verification work, ID-source messages/call joins, log provenance, bridge participation, the literal press-release reference, the six-page MFR crosswalk and underlying target/order records. No unchanged failed clock or catalogue query was rerun.

The existing May 2003 native drafting/corrected-QFR/delivery and signed 2006 FAA action/no-action/recommendation-disposition records remain missing. This packet does not prove what a later official knew, when a correction was delivered or whether personnel action occurred. Telephone, recorder, materials and steel criteria and technical ownership remain unchanged. **No substantive original-input requirement is closed by this acquisition, reading or publication.**

`PAGE-READING.json` states actual visual scope; `VERIFICATION.json` separates source/raster integrity from historical authentication. `CHECKLIST.md` maps the requirements and `TEXT-CHANGES.diff` is the actual Git addition diff. `EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff` preserves the final wording changes from the retained draft; no source values were changed. The unchanged original PDF and rasters remain in the native source store; this redundant public derivative contains reviewed notes and safe source references, not raw marked pages/full speech, ancillary personnel/contact/access data, private case correspondence or credentials. No outreach, fee, new request, filing, audio/clock decoding, experiment, private push, deletion or history rewrite occurred. Publication is separately verified by the remote commit/ref/file receipt.
<!-- END REPORT BODY 987ea25ff1ee40ed2152162189a8aff18e40ba6c234a28514a123ce08d76a5e7 -->

# Six-page NEADS MFR compared with the retained interview notes

October 31, 2026. The separately indexed **six-page Nasypany MFR is now acquired and fully read**. It is a different released file from the retained **18-page interview/review notes**. Both originals remain unchanged. This advances MC-T07 without completing the authoring/assembly/version relationship or any of the original 18 substantive criteria.

## Main result

On [MFR page 3](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00771.pdf#page=3), Commission staff explicitly say they reviewed **“DRM 1 DAT 2 Channel 2 MCC Op”** audio with the **Alderson Reporting transcript**. The retained [notes page 17](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048-doc8.pdf#page=17) says some clear calls occur in an **original transcript** but not Alderson, and that Alderson MCC OP Ch. 2 times from around **9:10 onward** must be redone and verified.

Those specific missing-call and timing-redo warnings were **not observed in the complete six-page MFR**. That is a bounded comparison of what these copies contain. It does not establish why the coverage differs, whether the warning was later addressed, or whether anyone intentionally concealed it. The MFR's review statement is not a completed timing worksheet.

The explicit recording label is useful for tracing the review. It remains beside the previously observed **DRM2/DAT2 Channel2** label in a selected transcript reproduction and the held MP3's **DRM1_DAT2_Channel_2_MCC_Op.mp3** filename. We do not choose one as controlling or authenticate a recording from its name. The actual versions, source/copy/channel manifest and completed verification work are still required.

## Source and actual reading

| Record | Newly acquired six-page MFR | Previously held notes packet |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official locator | [NAID 2610370](https://catalog.archives.gov/id/2610370), [actual PDF](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00771.pdf) | [ISCAP 2011-048, doc8](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2011-048-doc8.pdf) |
| Extent / bytes | **6 pages / 724,933 bytes** | **18 pages / 13,154,124 bytes** |
| SHA-256 | `9ffb79352ad865b540181d91e5479996747aa4b645227556e20f68b2ff011942` | `598711f6f1236a0ad30f1dbe7f9c1a2cd43507b4c12fdb18b3283e3472d1ea4e` |
| Form / fields | Self-titled MFR, interview dates **January 22 and 23, 2004**, **Prepared by Geoffrey Brown**, Team8 | Mixed handwritten excerpt/question/interview/continuation notes plus typed interview bullets |
| This unit's reading | All six whole-page images, visually read | Reused the preceding unit's actual 18-page reading/pins; no newly claimed re-viewing of those images |

The catalogue loaded through the ordinary public browser and exposed the current PDF download. Its six-page extent matched the acquired PDF. That does not explain the different 18-page note packet or establish a complete draft-to-MFR lineage. Acquisition times and source response fields are in `SOURCES.json`; actual image scope is in `PAGE-READING.json`.

The file was served without a Content-Type header. Our first header-only guard stopped before saving the body; its script/failure remain preserved. One corrective acquisition retained the missing header as null and required the actual PDF signature, successful parse, size cap and readback hash. This was our acquisition-assumption correction, not a suspicious finding about the historical record. No login, challenge or alternate-host bypass occurred. Raw catalogue/contact text and marked source pages are excluded from this public derivative.

## Side-by-side findings

| Pin / exact source scope | MFR | Retained notes / unresolved comparison |
| --- | --- | --- |
| MFR01, page1 heading and crossed-through line | States interview subject/dates/preparer. A visible line referring the reader to the recorded interview for further details is struck through. | Do not identify who crossed it out, why, when, or whether a recording exists or was destroyed. The actual interview/source/version history remains a retrieval target. |
| MFR02, page3 review statement; all six pages for the bounded warning check | Names DRM1/DAT2/Ch2 and Alderson review; no specific missing-call or post9:10 redo warning observed. | Notes17 contains those warnings. Summarization, scope/version differences, completed correction and improper omission remain possible explanations; none is selected. |
| MFR03, page2 *Chat logs* | Reports real-world information in the exercise log and says Nasypany **did not know why** time/day entries were disjointed. | Notes15 also says some data, including RADES, **may** have been entered after9/11. That explicit qualification was not observed in the MFR. Native entries/schema/creation-event-edit history needed; no fabricated-log conclusion. |
| MFR04, page2 *FAA communication* and phone paragraphs | Describes communication through ID, possible use of the FAA representative in BattleCab and personal nonmonitoring of FAA conference calls; secure/CONR versus CheyenneMountain connections are distinguished. | Notes9–10/15 give different detail on possible WOC/Herndon bridge access, personal nonparticipation and BattleCab/AirThreat roles. Capability, speaking, monitoring and organization-wide participation are not equivalent. |
| MFR05, pages3–4 *Otis/UAL175/Langley* | Gives qualified target/recollection accounts, Langley battle stations around9:09AM and barrier-cap intent. It does not expressly repeat the notes' American11-still-airborne **IDs→MCC** source passage. | Notes16–17 preserve the specific source path and second-hit/notification wording. The underlying rumor, validation/correction and exact FAA1326/1329 call legs remain unjoined. Distinct battle-stations, scramble, positioning and receipt events are not conflated. |
| MFR06, pages4–5 *Langley/AFIO* | Retains **010** versus **090 for60** headings, says other Langley/GiantKiller sources are needed, and records uncertainty whether Nasypany recommended BattleCab AFIO authority or acted himself. A higher-authority requirement is specifically described as post9/11. | Notes17 give qualified expectations/control beliefs. Actual agreements, commands, handoffs, receivers and execution remain needed. Later policy does not automatically establish the applicable9/11 rule. |
| MFR07, pages5–6 *ROE/DEFCON* | Records source-attributed approximate10:10/10:14AM, **1415GMT**, **1431(GMT)**, **1433**, and approximately**11:03AM** statements. The MFR distinguishes clearance/ROE information and DEFCON3 relay. | Keep these labels and qualifications beside the handwritten readiness/order notes. No independent clock fit or recovered signed order. The earlier FAA1330 “we just went up” passage did not identify a formal level; do not automatically treat it as the same event. |
| MFR08, all six pages versus notes8/9/14/16 | No repetition observed of the literal **9/18/04 / 0843** press-release challenge, handwritten **925** CAP/second-hit line or mixed/crossed-out subject header. | Earlier source values remain preserved, unexplained and unrepaired. Absence from this summary does not authenticate a corrected value or erase the note record. |
| MFR09, pages1–3/6 | Retains increased exercise staffing, initial exercise expectation, rapid information-feed account, operational traffic constraints and overwhelmed radio coverage. | Preserve capability and strain together. Neither establishes zero exercise effect, deliberate obstruction or independent measured response performance. |

`COMPARISON-PINS.json` supplies each exact source hash, page URL, visible heading/paragraph anchor, literal fields and inference limits. These are image/page references, not invented native text-line numbers. The two records may share the same interview, recordings and staff review; they are not independent corroboration merely because they are separate files.

## What could change the assessment

An actual comparison of the original/Alderson versions could establish which calls and times differed. The redo worksheet or corrected transcript could show accurate correction and propagation, weakening a concealment interpretation while preserving the earlier defect. Conversely, source-supported known uncorrected errors or unexplained incompatible delivered versions could strengthen a specific integrity concern. The present MFR does not settle either possibility.

Native logs and edit history could distinguish ordinary later reconstruction, mixed logging conventions or actual alteration. Orders, routing/receipt records and source clocks could test the attributed headings/readiness/authority accounts. None of those tests has been performed from this six-page acquisition, and a staff summary's assertion is not an independent measurement.

## Status and preservation

MC-T07 is now **partial: MFR acquired/read and selected content compared**. The complete note-to-MFR author/date/assembly/approval crosswalk remains missing. All earlier MC/OM input gaps and the original18requirements across FAA, telephone, recorder, materials and steel remain open/partial. In particular this is not the actual May2003 corrected QFR/receipt, signed2006 consideration/action/no-action decision or recommendation-disposition chain. Earlier completed technical tests remain performed; they were not duplicated here.

Original PDFs and separate rasters remain in the native Intel source store. New public notes preserve the competing wording; prior reports, source bytes and public history remain unchanged. `SOURCE-CHECKS.json` separates format/fingerprint/render checks from visual reading and historical authentication. `CHECKLIST.md` records requirement outcomes; `TEXT-CHANGES.diff` is the actual new-document Git addition diff. `EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff` preserves the final clarification of one paraphrase against the retained draft: remembered information before staff heard of a second hijack, rather than before the independently timed event. No source wording or value was changed. Public payload excludes raw marked pages/full speech, ancillary personnel/contact/access details, private correspondence, strategy and credentials. No outreach, fee, request, filing, source repair/deletion, audio decoding, clock fitting, experiment, private push or history rewrite occurred. Publication is a separate verified milestone, not acceptance of the substantive goal.
