### AA77 carrier printouts: event stages, zero fields and the limits of call counts

**Source unit:** `telephone-aa77-event-census-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-aa5baa42594bfa5cb926).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY fb46aa22712dbc1c08af01528c5e599c444b86a0c862f13bd24f5bf5807fbd21 -->
# AA77 carrier printouts: event stages, zero fields and the limits of call counts

November2,2026, America/New_York. TEL01/TEL03/TEL04; AUD016-A1 / TC05-B105 / TC06-B110. **New comparison of an already-held reproduced record span; no new download, native carrier database, authenticated conversation or complete release.**

The available Flight77 section contains **23 source-annotated records with eight different printed Start Time labels**. Of these,18print zero in the Duration field and5print positive numbers. In **four accepted CSC-identifier groups**, a routed-to-operator record has zero while a later disconnect record with the same printed identifier has a positive Duration. One group includes handoff-started/completed records as well. This makes it unsafe to count each zero record as a separate failed conversation, or each printed record as a separate call. It does **not** establish the real number of conversations, their callers, their recipients or what happened in the disputed named zero-duration entry.

## Source, actual reading and new-versus-reused work

The [public Request14 reader](https://www.scribd.com/document/13499791/T7-B13-Flight-11-Calls-Fdr-Response-From-DOJ-to-Doc-Req-14-Calls-From-AA-11-and-77-and-UA-175-and-93-ATT-Wireless-UA-And-GTE-Airphone-Call-Record) supplied28hosted JPEG entities on October27,2026. This unit reads **reader11–24**, Bates **M-WFA-00000632–645**, fax labels034–047, as reproduced images. All14preserved entity lengths and SHA-256 values match their acquisition pins. [Every image's exact URL/access date/hash](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8a1eb996a9b2bc683a28).

The [earlier enclosure review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/ad7be60185f5d3a49f0e0b7564f4da3610afaf92/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-carrier-enclosures-2026-10-27/README.md) had read selected fields and all page-accounting labels. Its C05 already documented158-versus146anchor arithmetic. The [earlier selected-event comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/cd950cd141582ab562ad74e17be6231077a2cc4c/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-event-joins-2026-10-27/README.md) tested fiveblocks on15/16/20/21 and exact CSC/GS pairs for two groups. Those are reused findings, not new discoveries here. The new work is the **whole available Flight77 span's record/continuation accounting, accepted CSC-only grouping, broader phase patterns and explicit annotation map**. The completed UA93/UA175 table work and court-label analysis are not repeated as new evidence.

All14whole page images were inspected, plus local nearest-neighbor key/field detail sheets. Enlargement adds no source detail; no OCR or new automated transcription was run. The upper fragment on11 precedes the Flight77 heading and is **excluded**, not silently assigned to that flight. Several records continue across pages; the final fragment on24 continues record23, not an extra event. Source circled1–23labels are useful locators, **not authenticated native unique record IDs**. Their author and insertion date remain unknown. [23record operational readings](CITATIONS.md#nov04-59499de32ad524a351a6) · [Computed results](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c272361f807e0925b080) · [Method and reading limits](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3c0e8858702dbf8b64a3).

## 1. Quantities in the held span

| Quantity | Result | Meaning and limit |
|---|---:|---|
|Source-annotated record headers under the Flight77 heading |23 |Not23people, calls or conversations; page continuations excluded from extra counting. |
|Distinct printed Start Time strings |8 |Not independent clock calibration or eight authenticated calls. Source order includes07:14:57 before07:12:18. |
|Accepted complete CSC readings / distinct CSC values |22records /7values |One first-record repeated-digit/length reading remains unaccepted. No claim of eight accepted full identifier groups. |
|Full GS strings independently accepted for this census |0 |GS fields were inspected but uncertain separators/final glyphs were not filled; no new full CSC+GS equality claim. Earlier selected pair tests remain separate. |
|Zero / positive Duration fields |18 /5 |Field values, not failure/success findings about conversations. |
|Event-type codes represented |6 |Menu selection25:9; routing29:4; air disconnect4:7; ground disconnect3:1; handoff started1:1; handoff completed2:1. |

Raw CSC values, GS reading details, contact/card/phone fields and images are excluded from publication. Public K01–K07 labels are **our derived aliases**, not carrier IDs or caller identities. The first unaccepted CSC record is retained separately; it is not matched to another row using its clock. Equal printed identifiers strengthen a printout association but do not prove installed identifier allocation/reuse rules, actual physical call identity, a unique end-to-end leg or a complete export.

## 2. Four groups contain zero routing records and positive disconnect records

|Our alias / printed start |Source-annotated records and reader pages |Printed event-code sequence |Duration fields in that source order |
|---|---|---|---|
|K03 /07:15:34 |4–7; reader13–15, Bates634–636 |menu25,menu25,routed29,air disconnect4 |0,0,0,102 |
|K05 /07:20:15 |10–15; reader16–19, Bates637–640 |menu25,menu25,routed29,handoff1,handoff2,air disconnect4 |0,0,0,0,0,274 |
|K06 /07:25:48 |16–19; reader19–22, Bates640–643 |menu25,menu25,routed29,air disconnect4 |0,0,0,159 |
|K07 /07:30:56 |20–23; reader22–24, Bates643–645 |menu25,menu25,routed29,air disconnect4 |0,0,0,260 |

The code groups these records by **individually entered accepted CSC strings**, not by desired start/time matches, circled labels or a group key copied into all rows. Full GS-string equality is not asserted. The K06 selected zero/159pair was already documented; the other three full phase groups and the broader accounting extend that observation. These are related stages in the **same reproduced source**, not four independent witnesses or corroborations of a historical conversation.

For K05, the [handoff-started record13 on reader18](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/18-f7e10d4c8d.jpg) prints07:24:01 and its [completed continuation14 on reader19](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/19-1f570a8a28.jpg) prints07:24:06. Both have zero Duration; the later disconnect record15 prints274. Those labels do not establish aircraft position, a measured five-second transfer, the effective radio configuration or what any person heard. They are a concrete target for the actual event/schema/clock and station-mapping records. No timing cause or preferred account is selected.

The07:18:58group K04 remains different: its two held records8–9, [reader15–16](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/15-d3c94df2c0.jpg), print zero. The early connected-to-office narrative and later court/briefing unconnected label remain alongside it. **Positive fields in other groups do not resolve K04**, identify Barbara Olson or prove which operator/office event the early unnamed narrative describes. The first two source-annotated records are also zero and are not transformed into conversations from their start-to-event spans.

## 3. The printout itself contains scoped explanatory annotations

[Reader13, Bates634, record4](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/13-ec2683a0f2.jpg) marks the1969billing-start and answer-supervision fields with **“Time is not tracked because OSPS bills”**. [Reader14, Bates635, record6](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/14-92e4203dc6.jpg) marks the routed-event duration **“Duration is not tracked by AWS”**. The record4annotation also describes an operator-selection path; that remains the annotator's description, not an inspected operator ticket or proof of an actual completed transfer.

These are source-image annotations. They are more specific than a generic possible explanation, but **the annotator, insertion date, installed version and governing authority are unverified**. A fax header naming AT&T Wireless does not authenticate who added every note. Do not promote the annotations into a universal carrier data dictionary, or apply GTE ACIS definitions to this different AT&T/Claircom evidence.

All18zero rows in this span also show the printed1969billing/answer dates. Those literal values remain; we do not replace them with0, an epoch conversion, a true call time or a demonstrated explanation for their presence. The annotations specifically address selected fields/records; they do not establish that every zero, including the07:18:58disconnect, is merely an untracked value.

## 4. Broader arithmetic controls, without reannouncing old discrepancies

The positive Duration values are158,102,274,159and260. In all five, the number equals the difference between the supplied printed event and billing-start clocks. In four it also equals the answer-supervision-to-event difference. The [record3 continuation on reader13](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/13-ec2683a0f2.jpg) is the existing exception:07:13:11billing,07:13:23answer,07:15:49event, Duration158; the answer interval is146. **The12-second anchor distinction was already published as C05**, not newly discovered. The new all-positive accounting supplies controls around it; it does not establish universal duration units, conversation onset or calibrated elapsed time.

The [routed record18 on reader21](https://html.scribdassets.com/5bafzdomdce6sz3/images/21-1a330ff42d.jpg) retains the earlier **07:26:18 /07:26:28** reading uncertainty; no single event time is accepted for that row. It is not converted into a measured negative/positive timing anomaly or silently chosen to make the sequence orderly. Higher-quality source media and effective system definitions remain discriminators.

## Conflicts, contrary evidence and what remains missing

- **159/154seconds:**the reproduced159field remains beside the Commission final-report note57's154; the new grouping does not explain or correct either account. [Final-report notes, printed455](https://www.9-11commission.gov/report/911Report_Notes.pdf#page=7).
- **Connected/unconnected:**the September20,2001report reader43–44/internal1–2 describes an unnamed caller connected to the office; the May2004briefing and court classify the09:18:58entry as unconnected. A unique underlying event/leg join is still missing. [Early report](https://www.scribd.com/document/24392516/T7-B19-Key-302s-Fdr-Entire-Contents-FBI-302s) · [May briefing](https://www.scribd.com/document/18886083/T7-B12-Flight-93-Calls-General-Fdr-52004-DOJ-Briefing-on-Cell-and-Phone-Calls-From-AA-77-408).
- **Eight/seven, direct/collect and May6:05Pacific/6:13nozone:**earlier count, recipient and time accounts remain source-specific and unresolved. Eight different start strings here do not adjudicate another report's counting conventions. Keyton's released form, Sept20reader rendering and the May-family released response are held components, not wholly missing categories.
- **Counterevidence to simplistic interpretations:**multiple printed phase records share accepted CSC values; zero routing/handoff fields coexist with positive disconnect fields. That weakens counting every zero as a separate failed call. Conversely, a positive duration and a named display label do not independently authenticate an actual speaker/conversation. The [staff report physical94note265](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=94) expressly leaves conversation-to-unknown-record matching uncertain.

Routine event-stage reporting, transcription/compilation error, version differences, different legs or incorrect attribution are competing possibilities to test; deliberate manipulation is untested. Neither explanation nor motive is inferred from this census. The source printouts, their summaries and exhibit may share inputs and are not independent corroboration.

The full Request14delivery/release/version chain and missing Bates609–631/fax011–033remain open. No contents are inferred for that hosted gap. Native carrier/switch/operator/office records, key-allocation and field/clock rules, annotator/transformation records and source-to-exhibit/caller/recipient joins remain needed. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-55886fe0a96a634fc753) retain all five tracks and18full criteria; this unit closes none by publication or arithmetic.

## Reproduction and preservation

[compare_events.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d38d2f23f75ccc0aa5b8) computes counts and groupings from supplied manual readings. Native execution used the local raw-CSC transcription; the public derivative retains only aliases. Controls include an unequal-key pair, a changed key with the same start, reversed input membership and two missing keys that must not be joined. They validate comparison behavior, **not our ability to read each glyph or a historical event**. No blinded second analyst, native export or independent image decoder was used.

`python3 -B verify.py` checks the bundle and safe derived-data reproduction. Optional `--capture-dir` checks original JPEG hashes; optional `--transcriptions` repeats the accepted raw-CSC comparison from locally supplied readings. An independent reviewer should transcribe the original public images first, rather than copying our groups as a second source. [Actual verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-268403673fbdc4d62fb8).

Original images/old reports/public history remain unchanged; [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a30c0f437519ba4c0b67) is actual zero-context Git output against the immediately preceding preserved combined review. Initial source-path guesses, premature image views while a producer was still live, and mispositioned reading crops were our process errors, not historical anomalies. The same live process was awaited to terminal success; no failed output was counted as source absence. No raw images/keys/contacts/card data, signatures, private case material, outreach, new request, fee, deletion, overwrite, main merge or history rewrite is part of this public addition.
<!-- END REPORT BODY fb46aa22712dbc1c08af01528c5e599c444b86a0c862f13bd24f5bf5807fbd21 -->

</details>

<a id="supplement-ae2f2071277cb6b4"></a>
### An answering-system collection report supplies a concrete property-record target

**Source unit:** `telephone-answering-system-property-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-77c093578f2968a6cf77).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY ae2f2071277cb6b489afb0ad3fa69e6b8bd3858620515bb28fc9aa03f90b3877 -->
# An answering-system collection report supplies a concrete property-record target

November2,2026, America/New_York; acquisition/reading November3UTC. **TEL01–TEL04 / AUD016-A1, TC05-B105 and TC06-B110.** A newly inspected official FBI interview describes an unintelligible message possibly associated with Philip Rosenzweig. Following that exact lead located a public Commission-packet mirror containing a report of collecting a telephone with an answering system and referring to **FD-192 / bulky evidence**. This advances the property/custody inquiry. The original property form, device, audio, analysis and disposition remain unacquired.

## First source: a possible caller, not an identified voice

[Official FBI part02, physical35, footer000000425](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=35), final body paragraph, says an answering machine recorded an **unintelligible phone call between8:44a.m.and9:00a.m.** on September11. It records the interviewee's belief that Rosenzweig **might have attempted** to call. That is a qualified attribution, not intelligible identification, a verified completed passenger conversation or proof the call originated on an aircraft.

The same paragraph lists his cellular carrier as **either Sprint or Cellular One**. It does not establish that the reported message used that handset or either service. Do not infer a cellular transmission from possession of a cell phone, choose a carrier, or add this unconfirmed contact to an authenticated-call total. No caller voice, original recording, destination/service event, aircraft position or calibrated clock was tested.

The visible report gives **investigation09/12/2001**, **transcription09/15/2001**, **dictated09/15/2001**, **case265D-NY-280350**, **REQ.#35-13**, and footer **000000425**. These fields have different explicit roles. They are not competing dates for the September11 event. The recipient's identity is masked in this release and is not reconstructed from another version. Unrelated personal/medical/employment/travel details, contacts and agent names are excluded from this derivative.

## Second source: reported collection and a reference to FD-192

The [public Packet6 reader](https://www.scribd.com/document/19131547/T7-B13-DOJ-Doc-Req-35-13-Packet-6-Fdr-Entire-Contents-FBI-Reports-730), **reader84 / printedfooter000000476**, shows a report dated **9/14/01**. Its opening says items were provided to FBI agents and a Massachusetts state trooper on **9/14/2001 at11:45AM**. Item1 describes **one white Sony telephone with a self-contained answering system** and says:

> This item was placed on a FD-192 to be placed in bulky evidence.

That sentence is a specific route to pursue. It is a statement in a report about listing/routing the telephone, **not the FD-192 itself**, an inspected bulky-evidence entry, a barcode, a complete custody log or proof of present survival. No original telephone, answering-machine recording, forensic extraction, examination result or disposal record was acquired here. The hardware serial and private telephone/address fields are retained only in the preserved raw image and excluded from publication; their omission from this derivative does not mean the source lacks them.

The final body paragraph describes **approximately8:45AM** on September11 and a telephone call that was **static**, whose nature the recipient could not determine. Preserve this beside the first report's **8:44–9:00 / unintelligible / might have attempted** account. A common unmasked passenger-name reference, request label or approximate time does not authenticate a common household, message, device or call. The masked interviewee's identity is not filled from this mirror's different redactions. No controlling timestamp, service or caller is selected, and no discrepancy is resolved.

The collection report also displays **case265D-NY-280350**, **REQ.#35-13**, and footer **000000476**. Its bottom handwritten302-like control is not adopted as an exact identifier. Its separately listed personal items and biological-identification information are outside this telephone study and not republished. They do not become audio evidence by appearing on the same page.

## Source relationship and authentication limits

The FBI source is an official delivered PDF entity, unchanged at **5,561,167bytes /78pages**, SHA-256 **055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51**, acquired **2026-10-31T22:04:24.920527+00:00**. This unit inspected only physical35. Official hosting authenticates the provenance of that released entity; it does not verify the historical truth of every interview statement.

The collection report is a **third-party public reader image of an apparent FBI/Commission record**, not an authenticated agency-native production. The visible cover, reader1, says **Commission Copy / Requests#35-13 / Packet#6**. That identifies the displayed packet's claimed relationship; it does not prove complete production, native delivery, an official source-to-item join, or that every record in the100-page reader was inspected. The reader's AI-enhanced title/description is not relied on as evidence.

Only reader1 and84 were downloaded through the supported browser's displayed-image function and read. Both entities are **WebP,904×1242**, despite displayed asset names ending.jpg. That is delivery-format metadata, not evidence of historical alteration. The original browser downloads and redundant Intel copies remain unchanged. The image entities identify the actual viewed bytes:

| Entity | Bytes | SHA-256 | Preservation UTC |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| Reader84 collection report |48,980|62df96a149ce5f4d6399aee8bcf1c4e5c975a01eb3222349fc68a2a9e460af8e|2026-11-03T03:00:22.998104+00:00|
| Reader1 cover |74,054|5c3c272ca7b38d276178bddcc64047684f8812ba8f9f2ff2060c79391283b75e|2026-11-03T03:00:54.992220+00:00|

These are preservation times; the exact preceding browser-download times were not separately captured. Ephemeral asset-query credentials are excluded from provenance/public payload; no token-removal fetch, login, gated whole-document download or denied official route was used. A first local copy attempt failed because the resolved output directory had not yet been created; the directory was then created and the same downloaded bytes copied exclusively and verified. No remote re-download, original overwrite or historical anomaly is inferred from that execution error.

The ordinary-text index for held official part02 had no Sony/FD-192/bulky hit. That bounded index result is **not** a source-absence, official-omission or concealment finding. This review did not read all78pages, all eight FBI parts or all100 mirror pages. A newly located indexed NARA finding-aid reference to Team7/Box13/Packet6 is a navigation lead, not an acquired official counterpart of footer476. No claim of a complete official release crosswalk is made.

## What the records can test next

**RH01:** Obtain the agency-held counterpart of footer476 and any native attachments/metadata. Compare to the mirror without overwriting either. Source authenticity and full document context remain open.

**RH02:** Obtain the actual **FD-192**, bulky-evidence registration, item/barcode/location fields and all receipts/transfers for the telephone described in the September14 collection report. A report's past-tense listing statement and future placement language do not certify every handling stage.

**RH03:** Obtain preserved answering-system memory/audio, source/copy/extraction logs, examinations and findings, including negative or inconclusive outcomes. Determine whether any record actually joins the collection to the earlier possible message. Ordinary noise, an unrelated caller, a misunderstood connection, an aircraft call, or another contact remain untested possibilities. No voice or signal analysis was performed here.

**RH04:** Obtain device settings/time checks, native carrier/operator/destination returns and source-to-recipient worksheets. Retain both time descriptions and both carrier candidates; an uncalibrated reported interval is not a flight-clock anchor. Do not identify callers from matching time strings or infer tampering from absent verification.

**RH05:** Obtain retention/disposition/return records and surviving derivative-copy references. The report establishes a concrete retrieval target; it establishes neither destruction nor continued retention. These requests are TODOs, not sent communications; no new FOIA, fee, outreach or filing occurred.

The strongest limitation is the unverified link between a qualified message attribution and a telephone collection described in a separate, mirrored report. An authenticated counterpart, item-level custody/analysis or contrary source could change this assessment. A collection record alone cannot establish who called, what they said, advance knowledge or conspiracy.

## Scope, revision and continuing work

Actual reading: **one official FBI face**, plus **two newly acquired mirror faces**. No adjacent official page was needed because physical35 ends a self-contained report. The Ong transcript candidate pages48/50 repeated completed work and were excluded. No new OCR, ASR, sound decoding, clock fitting, physical measurement, independent transcription or complete archive search was performed. The unchanged technical-owner recorder/materials results and independent audit retain their own scope; this unit does not claim their unfinished tests.

All five original work-plan tracks and all eighteen substantive criteria remain open/partial. FAA correction/delivery, signed administrative disposition, telephone original joins, recorder/materials native tests and steel preservation/attribution originals remain explicit dependencies. This is progress on a concrete telephone property-record chain, not completion of the goal.

[SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0799143605bf320ebd1a), [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ac24fe7595f3558d12d6), [READING-SCOPE.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-173d3a0c2385674891fe), [OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1502b4c5961d1f0cd5a3), [LOCATORS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3cdd3c169e2feb424309), [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d932a867d58cc433900c) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d2cea0cfed6fe793625b) separate source representations, inference limits, actual preservation checks and missing originals. [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e9ec303ebf257b8565c9) preserves the exact additive text comparison against the immediately preceding combined review. Raw source images/PDF, private fields, message content, correspondence and local coordination are excluded from the public derivative. Remote Git verification is separate from source reading, and publication does not authenticate historical claims.

### Britton telephone evidence: underlying interview, reference dates and search coverage

<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 9ca9357fc59c4fd77aa39bb00a521c74db26ebef344561eeefd34c53df7430bb -->
# Britton telephone evidence: underlying interview, reference dates and search coverage

Review: October 31, 2026 local / November 1, 2026 UTC. This is a documentary source comparison, not a call/device experiment or a finding of fabrication or concealment.

The held official FBI release contains a two-page recipient interview that we had not previously read in this work. It qualifies the cellphone claim as the recipient's assumption, gives an approximate alternative call time, and supplies a specific report locator. A separate staff working sheet cites that locator but still calls for phone-record confirmation. The Commission staff-report citation gives a different interview date. These records now narrow the required carrier, source-version and citation checks; those checks are not complete.

## Sources and actual reading scope

1. **Official FBI part 02 PDF, physical pages 9–10**, release footers **000000226–000000227**, REQ. #35-13, file **265A-NY-280350-302**; first-page handwritten locator **302 25306**. [Official PDF, page9](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=9). The previously acquired 78-page PDF is 5,561,167 bytes, SHA-256 **055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51**. Newly rendered and visually read both selected pages in full. The released PDF is held; original interview notes/audio, native authoring and carrier events are not thereby authenticated.
2. **Official PG1602 control record, part 06 viewer pages 10–12**, footers **000000251–000000253**, case **265A-PG-69744 (PENDING)**, REQ. #35-13. [Official viewer](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-06-of-08/view). Read those three pages, preserving six unchanged browser views. The source PDF was not downloaded in this unit; the earlier stopped 403 route was not retried. A historical PENDING label is not a current case-status determination.
3. **Commission staff report, physical page 99, note 371.** [Official NARA PDF](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=99). Previously held 121-page PDF, 6,760,035 bytes, SHA-256 **9671b7afe9a45928dcbe053e567b7f830876bc31e32a1d221b39fa59493d5bbb**. Newly rendered page 99 and read the citation in the page image. The source is the official archived staff report, not merely an extracted-date assertion.
4. **Staff working-sheet reproduction, selected blocks on viewer pages 1–2 of 8**: TO: File; FM: John Raidt; RE:Phone calls from aircraft hijacked on 9/11 as per Concordance. [Public reproduction](https://www.scribd.com/document/13499803/T7-B13-Flights-Phone-Calls-Fdr-Search-of-FBI-302-Files-for-175-77-and-93). Read the header/query discussion and Britton entry 3, including fields B/D/F/G/H; preserve three selected viewports. No native memo/PDF/query database or authenticated archival delivery was acquired. AI-enhanced title/description, suggested documents and uploader labels are not agency evidence. No claim that every page or entry was read.

[SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b836497165710d6e543f) provides exact fields, dates, source URLs, acquisition/capture times, byte/hash and scope records. Images and source PDFs remain preserved locally, outside the public payload. Screenshot/render hashes identify those derivatives, not historical call data.

## 1. What the underlying interview actually qualifies

The official part 02 report's first page records an interview on **09/17/2001**, with **Date of transcription 09/20/2001**; its continuation repeats **09/17/2001**. The interview subject remains masked here. No identity from a less-redacted mirror is used to fill that mask or identify a masked PG1602 actor.

The call paragraph begins **“At approximately 9:30am or 9:45am on 09/11/01”** (the **or**, two values and approximate qualification are retained). It reports a hijacking conversation, a number supplied for a callback, screaming and the line going dead. The next paragraph says the attempted callback got a message that the number was not in service. It then expressly says the recipient **assumed** Britton had borrowed a cellphone, given the number she supplied. This is an inference reported in an interview, not direct observation of the onboard equipment or an independently verified service classification.

The report also limits what the recipient said was conveyed: no identifying details about the hijackers, their number or armament, who was flying, the intended destination, or other passengers' activities. The continuation says Britton did not explain how she knew about the two World Trade Center impacts. Those are limits on this account, not proof the events did not occur. A callback failure is not proof that the original conversation was impossible; original routing, dialed-number and service records are needed. No personal/contact/profile data from the continuation is reproduced publicly.

## 2. Comparison without silently converting assumptions into measurements

| Source and exact field | Preserved wording/value | Limit or unresolved join |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official part 02 p. 9, callback paragraph | Recipient **assumed** a borrowed cellphone from the supplied number | No native carrier/device verification; subject masked |
| Working sheet p. 2, entry 3B | **Borrowed Cell Phone**; contact digits excluded from this public derivative | Summarized category; entryG cites record 25306, not a carrier row |
| Working sheet p. 2, entry 3H | **CONFIRM TIME AND DURATION OF CALL WITH CELL PHONE RECORD.** | Explicitly an outstanding task in the sheet; no later completion supplied by that instruction |
| Official part 02 p. 9, call paragraph | Approximately **9:30am or 9:45am** | Two approximate alternatives; timezone/clock not established |
| Working sheet p. 2, entry 3D | **Time: Between 9:30-9:45** | Different time formulation; no conversion of alternatives into a verified interval |
| Working sheet p. 2, entry 3F | **No tape mentioned in 302** | Not a finding that no recording existed anywhere |
| PG1602 pp. 10–11, Event narrative | Relayed **cellular telephone** description and **“the plane will crash in 20” / “SECONDS.”** | Report of a relay, not an independently timed 20-second interval; actors remain masked |
| Official part 02 p. 9, crash wording | Turning and going to crash; line went dead | This selected two-page report does not supply the PG1602 narrative's 20-second measurement or independently authenticate a crash time |

The working sheet's explicit **265A-NY-280350-302-25306** reference and the official scan's **302 25306** annotation provide a qualified record-reference association. They are not a native byte/version match or permission to reconstruct masked identities. The sheet is a derivative account of its cited report, not a second independent witness or carrier confirmation. We have not proved which document PG1602's reply referenced.

## 3. Dates and the prior-interview claim remain separate

**PG1602 p. 10** is a communication dated **09/22/2001**, Pittsburgh to Pittsburgh. The information-received field says **09/20/2001, 11:18 PM**, source label **SOMERSET CRASH DP** (unexpanded). Its continuation on **p. 11** reports contact and a reply that the person had been interviewed by FBI New York. Some wording near the end of the first line is obscured by markings; the legible phrase **“INTERVIEWED BY FBI NEW YORK”** is retained without inventing missing text. The form's **Event Date 09/11/2001; Time 12:00 PM** is an administrative field whose relation to the reported call is unverified.

**PG1602 p. 12** records assignment on **09/21/2001 at 1:23 AM** and conditions setting an interview lead on a determination that the person **“HAS NOT ALREADY BEEN INTERVIEWED.”** The prior-interview reply, “Lead Required?: YES” and conditional assignment are all preserved. Neither the presence of an assignment nor a reply about an interview establishes the actual interview's record ID, performance, clearance or later closure. No missing return is treated as proof of inaction.

**Staff-report p. 99, note 371** cites: **“FBI report of investigation, recipient of call from Marion Britton, Sept. 14, 2001.”** The held official candidate instead has an interview date of **September 17** and transcription date of **September 20**. Both candidate dates and the **September 14 citation** remain. Note371 lacks the specific 25306 identifier in the printed citation. Thus we cannot yet establish that it cites this very report, that the citation is erroneous, or that the dates were changed. A different interview, a citation mistake or a version difference are untested possibilities. Obtain the actual cited September 14 record or a source crosswalk before adjudicating them.

## 4. A specific search-coverage question, not a reproduced defect

The working sheet says its data came from a set of FBI 302 files derived from searches using **phone call AND flight AND 175/77/93**, **phone AND call AND flight AND 175/77/93**, **cell phone AND flight AND 175/77/93**, and **cell AND call AND flight AND 175/77/93**, with the flight numbers checked independently. Its claim of a “complete set” is scoped to those searches; it does not establish complete FBI holdings, a full carrier database or a complete independent call census.

The writer adds: **“For some odd reason inputting 11 in the flight number field would search and return 175 instead of 11. Need to check with FBI on this.”** We read that statement in the reproduction; we did not rerun the query or acquire its native execution log, syntax, field definitions, database version, original/corrected results or follow-up. Treat it as a reported retrieval problem and a concrete TODO. It does not establish deliberate exclusion, actual loss of Flight 11 evidence or a live defect today. The relevant test is reconstruction from the original database/query records, preserving both successful and contrary results.

## Assessment, missing records and preservation

Confidence is high about the displayed qualifications and dates in the held official PDFs. Confidence is limited about call service, exact time, native-record authenticity beyond the released PDF, whether the candidate is the note 371 source or PG1602 interview, and what happened to the reported search issue. A plausible ordinary reading is early interview inference followed by provisional staff categorization and a still-pending verification task. That interpretation is not established merely because it is plausible. These sources do not establish a manufactured call, suppression or intent.

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a1f6f2e18e739c6a3a08) retains the actual September 14 source/citation crosswalk; native interview material; PG1602 contact/assignment/return/version records; carrier/operator/routing/service and clock joins; the exact 25306-to-call/exhibit mapping; and native working-sheet/query/follow-up records. No inquiry/request was sent. The held official two-page interview is now read and should not be described as wholly unacquired; its underlying notes/audio and operational joins remain missing. All 18 original FAA/telephone/recorder/materials/steel requirements remain open/partial; no native call, calibrated clock, voice, decoder or laboratory test was completed here.

Earlier records and conflicting values are unchanged. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d1d5843a507a252d330d), [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-6329515b0029b6e61362) and [TEXT-CHANGES.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-4da340b4f44199e8692d) distinguish actual reading, source/hash/diff checks, public additions and unperformed tests. The addition diff is against no prior file at this new path, not a replacement of previous reports. [EDITORIAL-REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-84fbad2b4e5834103db1) and [FINAL-REVIEW-REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7a6d02ee3662d0a837ba) preserve successive prepublication text changes; earlier candidates remain unchanged locally. Raw PDFs/screenshots, personal/contact/profile details, masked identities, private correspondence, strategy and credentials are excluded from the public payload.
<!-- END REPORT BODY 9ca9357fc59c4fd77aa39bb00a521c74db26ebef344561eeefd34c53df7430bb -->

</details>

<a id="supplement-db35489ff539b5bd"></a>
### First operator’s 2002 interview: screen-print lead, handoffs and conflicting labels

**Source unit:** `telephone-first-operator-followup-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-cfe366a595c641e54a3c).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY db35489ff539b5bd71547e4979f7beb074a470d0e9bae6c9e1477d6d5cbdd802 -->
# First operator’s 2002 interview: screen-print lead, handoffs and conflicting labels

The interview records an Airfone operator’s account of a caller who identified himself as **Todd Beamer**. That is the report’s attribution, not an independent voice or caller identification.

**The newly read interview identifies a specific original artifact:** a computer-screen print reportedly made when the call disconnected, intended for engineering to determine where the call originated on the aircraft. It also gives a file/serial pointer to the supervisor’s interview. Neither the print, transfer receipt nor engineering result is attached to these two pages.

The record also prints **flight 99**, whereas the earlier supervisor account prints **Flight 93**. It shifts between **JOHNSON** and **JACKSON** within one paragraph. These values remain preserved with their qualifications. They do not establish a different aircraft, fabricated call, deliberate alteration or concealment.

## Source and actual scope

The [held public mirror, physical 23–24](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=23) is a third-party reproduction of FBI-form records. The 73-page PDF has **2,997,662 bytes**, SHA-256 **`e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`**, and was acquired October 27, 2026. No new source download or public search was performed. The original bytes are unchanged; a hash does not independently authenticate the source as an agency master.

Both interview pages were newly read in full on November 2. They are internal **1–2**, production footers **000000370–371**, file **265A-NY-280350-302**. The investigation date is **06/05/2002**; transcription and dictation fields are **06/11/2002**. Those are distinct field roles, not an adopted date discrepancy. The account concerns September 11, 2001 and is a later recorded recollection.

For comparison, previously read [official FBI part 08, physical 4–5](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=4) was viewed again. Its PDF SHA-256 is **`a7d3d872daf33d41c993ade5ccf2e9aa5421ffb1f4cda6164d6bb712372dde41`**. The earlier source carries **265D-NY-280350**, distinct from the 2002 report’s **265A** labels. No case alias or serialized-copy relationship is established by adjacency or similar names. Official hosting identifies that released entity, not the truth of every assertion or full custody.

Five held contact sheets were used only for navigation. They are not 60 newly read source pages. Two new and two previously read full-page faces were examined; no complete 73-page/21-page review, hidden-text recovery, new OCR, audio/ASR, native clock or carrier replication is claimed. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ebfbd49e7910ee201910) supplies access dates, representation roles and source/raster hashes. Raw scans, images, full interviews, contact/financial/profile fields and signatures are excluded from this derivative.

## 1. Screen print: reported creation versus intended transfer

[Physical 24, final paragraph](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=24) says a print of the computer screen containing administrative call information was made when the call disconnected. It says the information **was to be passed** to engineering to determine where the call was made on the aircraft.

That identifies an artifact and intended work. It does **not** establish that engineering received it, performed the analysis, identified a seat/handset or produced a retained result. The actual print and its fields, session identifiers, dispatch/receipt, engineering work order and analytical disposition are concrete retrieval targets. An earlier provider report separately describes notes, a typed summary and a print handed to an agent; generic print-screen wording does not show these are the same artifact.

The [earlier May 11, 2004 memorandum review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/b6923d4d9c874ab71fe88112f401d5279caaeb95/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-operator-notes-mfr-2026-10-31/README.md) records uncertainty about whether the first operator made notes. A computer print is not automatically handwritten notes. The new interview neither resolves nor contradicts that uncertainty. It does not report a recovered call recording.

## 2. A specific interview pointer

[Physical 24, first paragraph](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=24) references the supervisor’s interview under **265A-NY-280350-OUT, serial 3255**. Preserve that string as printed. Locate the actual serial, its cover, continuations, enclosures and interview/source-version association before equating it with a previously acquired supervisor account. The pointer is not a receipt or proof the referenced material is in this compilation.

## 3. Times and holds describe different reported phases

| Source | Preserved account | Limitation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| First operator, physical 23, first substantive paragraph | Answered at **approximately 8:40 a.m. Central** | Later recollection; no native clock/event match. |
| First operator, physical 24, second paragraph | **Less than ten minutes** speaking or conferring with the supervisor | Her involvement, not total session, caller speech or billed duration. |
| Same paragraph | **Three holds:** once **less than two minutes**, twice **less than thirty seconds** | Estimated holds; no exact summed interval or event rows recovered. |
| Supervisor, official part 08 physical 4 | Received the call at **approximately 8:45 a.m. Central** | Different participant/possible phase; source clocks and event identity unjoined. |

The supervisor handoff is described, but these reports do not supply the actual transfer timestamp or a calibrated five-minute interval. Do not adjust either time or count held time as continuous passenger speech. Native agent/session/conference/hold records, clock/field definitions and original notes are needed. Existing silence, later-callback and terminal-time disagreements remain unchanged.

## 4. Flight label and surname variations

[Physical 23, final paragraph](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=23) reports the caller’s number as **United flight 99**, expressly qualified as what the operator recalls. Its earlier paragraph says only that her screen indicated a **United Airlines** flight; it does not say the screen displayed 99. The [supervisor report, official physical 4](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=4), reports **United Airlines Flight 93**.

Preserve **99 / 93** and their source roles. Caller name, airline and destination make a proposed documentary comparison useful, but they are not a native session or voice-identification join. Original notes, media, screen fields and report versions could distinguish recollection, transcription, different events or other possibilities. None is selected as the cause or the actual spoken number.

The second substantive paragraph on physical 23 repeatedly uses **JOHNSON**, but two consultation sentences use **JACKSON**. The report does not explain the variation. Both remain recorded. An editorial error, alias, different person or another cause has not been tested; no preferred surname, reconstructed identity or intentional alteration is inferred.

## 5. Preserve what was heard, inferred and not recalled

The first substantive paragraph reports two people with knives and someone entering the cockpit, then explicitly attributes to the operator the conclusion that the latter was additional. That inference is not an independent direct count of three attackers. The second paragraph’s calm/quiet description is reported perception, not an acoustic measurement or test of authenticity.

Physical 24 says she did not recall clothing or ethnicity descriptions. That is a limit on recall, not proof no such words were spoken. These distinctions matter when comparing accounts; they do not independently establish or disprove the caller’s identity or any historical mechanism.

## Status, limits and next records

The useful advance is a closer creation/engineering-work pointer, an exact interview serial and a bounded source comparison. [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-62302f250938af0ccc0b) contains **seven related analyst entries**, not seven independent records or corroborations. No native print, transfer, completed engineering result, original notes/audio, calibrated clock or authenticated caller/session mapping has been recovered by reading these pages.

The strongest limitation is dependence on reproduced reports and recollection: multiple forms can summarize the same event, and the June 2002 and September 2001 records do not authenticate each other. Quiet speech, naming/number differences and missing underlying artifacts do not establish a fake call or cover-up. The notes/print/engineering and source-version records could strengthen or weaken later interpretations.

All original five-track/18 requirements stay open or partial. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-886d30aa896cb023c29b) retains the missing inputs; prior actual acquisitions/tests remain performed. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-248983a7b3ae35136da6), [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d953b48ec06da1120d1b) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a9cb610a6d7da597213e) separate reading, preservation and publication from historical validation. Earlier source bytes, printed conflicts and report versions remain unchanged. No new search, outreach, request, fee, filing, private-source use, source overwrite or history rewrite occurred. Publication is not main-branch adoption or substantive goal completion.

# HQ1492: an explicit request to obtain the reported Flight 77 message

A newly read FBI-form unit, **HQ1492**, directs the Washington Field Office to **obtain a recording** of a message attributed in the intake text to **“TODD REUBEN,”** described as a **Flight 77** passenger. This is a more specific acquisition target than the previously read **WF334** direction to contact someone about message contents.

**The document records a request and assignment—not completed collection, recovered audio, confirmed cellular use, caller identity or a final result.** Its relationship to WF334 remains unverified. The two spellings, dates, control labels and differently worded instructions remain separately preserved.

## Exact new source unit

[Public reproduction, physical 57–59](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=57): production footers **000000753–755**. These three full-size page faces were newly read on **November 2, 2026**. The held source is a **73-page, 2,997,662-byte** third-party reproduction of FBI-form records, acquired **2026-10-27T13:40:38.310907+00:00**, SHA-256 `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`.

This is not an independently authenticated FBI master, native intake database or complete production. No new PDF was downloaded. Source images/full tip/contact/network-address/personnel/signature fields remain outside the public derivative. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-84beedb1d82f89b46a08) gives source/image hashes, exact scopes and reused records.

## What the pages actually say

[Physical 57, Event, footer 000000753](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=57), reports a **“CELL PHONE CALL”** from **“TODD REUBEN, PASSENGER ON FLIGHT 77”**, a **saved message**, and possible useful background noise. Those are reported allegations. There is no recording, transcript, authenticated speaker/flight or carrier determination in this unit. “Cell phone” in a tip is not an engineering finding distinguishing cellular from an installed aircraft telephone. The background-noise description is a possibility, not an acoustic result.

[Physical 58, footer 000000754](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=58), marks **Lead Required? YES**. [Physical 59, Set Lead 1, footer 000000755](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=59), specifies **HQ1492**, **WFO / Washington, DC**, assignment **09/15/2001 at 6:10 PM**, and an instruction to contact a masked party **“AND OBTAIN RECORDING OF MESSAGE LEFT BY PASSENGER OF FLIGHT #77. MAY HAVE BACKGROUND NOISE.”**

This creates precise targets: the actual lead response/contact interview and disposition; any submitted or collected media, evidence inventory/receipt/barcode, copy/transcription/examination and analytical finding. The directive itself does not prove that an agent contacted anyone, a recording existed or was acquired, or any analysis succeeded. No completed result appears within these three faces; that is not proof the FBI failed to follow up or that no result exists elsewhere.

## Preserve the fields and comparisons

| Field / source | HQ1492, newly read | WF334, prior reading |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Reported name | **TODD REUBEN**, p57 Event | **TODD RUBEN**, p68 Event |
| Reported flight | **77** | **#77** |
| Information Received | **09/11/2001, 7:49 AM**, p57 | **09/11/2001, 7:49 PM**, p68 |
| Header date | **09/16/2001**, p57 | **12/15/2001**, p68 |
| Event Date / Time | **Both blank**, p57 | **09/11/2001 / time blank**, p69 |
| Assignment | **09/15/2001, 6:10 PM**, p59 | **09/12/2001, 7:20 AM**, p70 |
| Task | **Obtain recording**, p59 | Contact regarding contents of answering-machine message, p70 |
| Typed control | **HQ1492**, p57/p59 | **WF334**, p68/p70 |
| Handwritten bottom notation | Read as **HQ3060**, p57 | Previously read as **WF8406**, p68 |

The [earlier WF334 report](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6e9ce8fa287fa4410241bc8fba303cefd6476467/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf334-recording-lead-2026-11-02/README.md) supplies its exact source/field pins. The [court animation context](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2f78618d88ebcbd3e91811e9ba17f6457a6027ef/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf334-static-context-2026-11-02/README.md) contains the spelling **Reuben, Todd** as presentation text; independent underlying origin of the accounts is not established.

These are **different, unjoined records**. The matching minute and different AM/PM markers are preserved, but neither intake-receipt field is authenticated as the time a message was left. No AM-to-PM correction, common-clock chronology, processing-delay calculation, post-crash call conclusion or same-message assumption is made. Header, receipt, event and assignment fields retain distinct roles. A native creation/edit/import/routing history and original submission would test their relationship.

Similarly, handwritten notations do not establish aliases or replacement controls. Their writers, dates and functions remain unknown. Neither two typed controls nor four typed/handwritten appearances establish two or four calls. Similar allegations, source copies and handling stages are not independent corroboration of wrongdoing.

## Intake and routing context

Physical 57 records **To:WFO**, **From:COUNTERTERRORISM**, case **265D-HQ-1348101 (PENDING)**, **Method of Contact:Written**, source label **INTERNET TAR - 618**. These labels identify source-record/routing targets, not authentication of an internet submission or caller. **PENDING** is printed source text, not a verified present case status. Masked parties and omitted network/contact identifiers are not reconstructed or republished.

The name and alleged-flight/message similarities make HQ1492 a candidate to cross-reference with WF334 and the earlier family-canvass attribution account. They do not prove identity equivalence, a distinct verified recipient, an additional real call, a specific carrier row, or a refutation of that canvass. Misidentification, duplicated or inaccurate tips, earlier/misattributed messages, inconclusive follow-up and a genuine relevant message remain untested possibilities. Original submissions, actual dispositions, message/custody and carrier/session/canvass joins are the discriminating records—including negative and inconclusive findings.

## Actual work and limits

Two new exact-name official-domain queries and a bounded held-index check yielded no usable original follow-up locator. Generic/unrelated results were not opened as evidence. One index-schema assumption was wrong and was corrected in a preserved local addendum; no text miss was promoted to source-wide absence. The steel reuse check confirmed already completed routes and known photos, so those searches were not repeated or published as new.

The subsequent **16-page contact sheet** was navigation only. It exposed this record missed by the earlier narrow text/report checks. Only **three full faces**, 57–59, were newly read in detail. This is not a full 73-page census or independent transcription of every other tip. It demonstrates why a text-index miss must not be treated as proof of absent source content. Other contact-sheet material remains navigation-only, not adopted historical findings.

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e793ee8e1ddb859cbdb5) contains five related analyst entries about this one unit. [verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2af115e33cdb5818afb9) checks declared source/image/derivative bytes, manually entered locators, exclusions and preserved predecessor; it does not independently verify the pixel reading, tip, voice, caller, aircraft, cellular technology, clock, custody, disposition or historical cause. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3d24b76bbea6fd95abc1) states exactly what ran.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8d563dd2b3b26de75dfc) retains all five tracks and 18 requirements, the original HQ1492/source-submission/recording/disposition targets and previous WF334/serial3255/print/engineering targets. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-02ab8d888e9d68d50db1) and the actual additive [Git text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2131801a83345a14e9e5) preserve revision accountability. No original-plan criterion or whole investigation is completed by this reading or publication. Prior conflicting records and source bytes remain unchanged.

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 19e41c750f30f8e98f12e6a4992a2d652f9eb12bf59181eab2b8d649335e5e7e -->
# May-family relays: the interview-request chain and preserved source differences

The newly read records locate a specific **Las Vegas family-interview and response trail**. A September 11 Dallas memorandum directs Las Vegas to interview Ron May and report information **positive or negative**. A separate **DL99** intake directs Dallas to prepare an interview request. Neither is the completed interview, a returned result or a native carrier record.

Both accounts relay information through **Jane Allen, American Airlines' Vice President for Flight Services**. **They are not independent interviews or independent confirmation of a call.** Both say approximately **6:05 Pacific time**. No 6:45 or 40-minute discrepancy survives full-page reading; the preliminary thumbnail guess was ours and is rejected, not preserved as historical evidence.

The memorandum also describes Flight 77 as having **“crashed into the World Trade Towers.”** The held official staff report describes its impact at the **Pentagon**. Both descriptions remain separately sourced and unchanged. Their difference does not establish why the memorandum used that wording, who knew what when, or that the family call was fabricated.

## Exact new source units

[Physical 53–54](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=53), production footers **000000741–742**, are the Dallas memorandum dated **09/11/2001** and its lead page. [Physical 60–62](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=60), footers **000000756–758**, are **DL99**, header **09/18/2001**, with event/lead continuations. All **five full faces** were newly read on **November 2, 2026**. Earlier contact-sheet views were navigation only.

The unchanged public mirror is **73 pages**, **2,997,662 bytes**, SHA-256 `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`, acquired **2026-10-27T13:40:38.310907+00:00**. It is a **third-party reproduction of FBI-form records**, not an authenticated FBI master/native intake database, complete production, original family interview or carrier event file. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f86f92c0c1e7f1b4a9a9) gives source/image pins and exclusions. No new PDF download, public query, OCR or audio analysis occurred.

## The relay chain and actual requested work

[Physical 53, Details](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=53), says Dallas received information from Allen, who said Ron May had called her. The memorandum says May **“apparently”** told Allen he had received a call from his daughter **Renee May**, a Flight 77 attendant. [Physical 60–61, Event](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=60), attributes information to Allen and describes Ron May and his wife receiving their daughter's call. Both relay six reported hijackers, movement toward the rear, attempted cockpit access and a line going dead. These remain **reported call descriptions**, not independently identified people, authenticated disconnection causes or verified carrier/device/service facts.

[Physical 54, Set Lead1](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=54), directs **Las Vegas** to contact and interview Ron May and send **“information, positive or negative”** to Counterterrorism, New York, Washington Field and Dallas. Other addressees receive **“Read and clear”** instructions. Those office-specific tasks are not interchangeable: read-and-clear does not cancel the Las Vegas interview direction, prove case closure or supply a completed result.

[Physical 61](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=61) marks **Lead Required? YES** and says Las Vegas covers the matter. [Physical 62, DL99 assignment/task](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=62), records **09/11/2001 at 7:30 PM** and directs **“PREPARE EC REQUESTING INTERVIEW OF RON MAY FATHER OF AA FLT 77 FLIGHT ATTENDANT RENEE MAY.”** An instruction to prepare a request is distinct from preparing/sending/receiving it, conducting the interview and returning its findings. No completed EC/receipt/interview/disposition is supplied by these five faces.

Exact underlying targets are the original airline/parent contacts and notes, Dallas EC/request and dispatch/receipt, Las Vegas interview/report/attachments and response—including negative or inconclusive information—and the native control/routing/disposition history. No contact, new request or fee was authorized or made here.

## Preserve times without forcing a clock or call match

| Source field | Printed value / wording |
| --- | --- |
| Memorandum header, p53 | **09/11/2001** |
| Memorandum Details, p53 | **about 6:05 a.m., pacific time, 9/11/01** |
| DL99 header, p60 | **09/18/2001** |
| DL99 Information Received, p60 | **09/11/2001, 3:25 PM** |
| DL99 Event narrative, p60 | **AROUND 6:05 PACIFIC TIME** |
| DL99 Event Date/Time, p61 | **09/11/2001 / 6:05 AM** |
| DL99 assignment, p62 | **09/11/2001 at 7:30 PM** |
| Reused staff-report p31 | Attempt **9:11 A.M.**; connection **9:12 A.M.**, citing note 260 |

The approximate forwarded Pacific-time statements, unlabelled event/receipt/assignment clocks and later call entries have different source roles. No timezone is supplied here for unlabelled fields, and no silent conversion, controlling value, calibrated delay, exact event order or native-row identity is accepted. Original timing bases, carrier dictionary/clock, intake history and family interview are still needed. The later staff entry is not the underlying log.

DL99 is the typed control. Its case is **265D-NY-280350-DL (PENDING)**; a bottom notation is read as **DL666**. The memorandum lists **265D-NY-280350**, **265D-WF-222811**, **265D-HQ-1348101**. A faint handwritten serial-like notation on physical 53 is not confidently transcribed; no value is adopted. Handwriting functions/aliases and PENDING's current relevance are unverified. No contact numbers, masked staff identities or private profiles are reproduced or reconstructed.

## Preserve the impact-site conflict and contrary context

[Memorandum physical 53, opening Details](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=53), describes the attendant aboard American Airlines Flight 77 **“just before it crashed into the World Trade Towers.”** The [official archived staff report, printed/physical34](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=34), says American Airlines Flight 77 crashed into the **Pentagon**. The memorandum's words are not replaced with the later description.

The [same staff report, p31](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=31), also describes earlier airline/FAA uncertainty about which aircraft struck the towers. That is **later reported context**, not independent proof of why this specific memorandum used its phrase. Early confusion, misreporting, copying, a different underlying account or another explanation remain untested; no one is selected. Original upstream contacts and memo drafting/transmission history would discriminate. This mismatch does not invalidate every relayed call detail or establish deliberate deception, a changed aircraft history or a concealed recording.

Staff p31 also retains uncertainty about whether rearward movement meant passengers or crew. Its account of the mother and the relays' references to the father/parents are not silently collapsed into a single verified recipient/conversation. Original interviews, carrier/session records and canvass inclusion rules are needed to test which reports describe which events.

## Reuse the existing interview citation honestly

Previously held [staff-report physical94, notes260–262](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=94), cites **FBI “American Airlines Airphone Usage,” September 20, 2001** for the call entries and **“FBI report of investigation, interview of Ronald and Nancy May, Sept 12, 2001”** in note261. Note262 separately cites an airline-employee interview and January 20, 2004 QFR/email response. These are **existing citations reused as locators**, not newly acquired original interviews, usage logs, emails or source authentication.

The staff PDF has **121 pages**, **6,760,035 bytes**, SHA-256 `9671b7afe9a45928dcbe053e567b7f830876bc31e32a1d221b39fa59493d5bbb`, captured **2026-10-28T00:16:15.117345+00:00** from the official National Archives URL. Previously read pages **31/34/94** were reused; they are not new reading/discovery counts. No fresh aircraft-impact or recorder test was performed, and the technical owner's work remains separate.

## Verification and unresolved requirements

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2d0727b3c27ded805e29) preserves five related analyst entries, not five confirmations or a count of calls/hijackers. [verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-feeaa4837444fd058e6c) checks declared source/raster/body identities, manual source locators/values, exact predecessor preservation and all 18 TODOs; it does not independently transcribe pixels, authenticate a caller, family interview, aircraft, service, clock, carrier event, custody or response. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5e860a61821e8587167b) records actual modes.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8a19c22763b054f432a7) carries the specific original interview/request/response/clock/impact-wording targets and all five tracks and 18 requirements forward. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5a6c42281dccb9a3efde) and the actual additive [text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7a9b75483590f7d92584) preserve accountability. Original records, agreements, source conflicts and earlier versions remain unchanged. No original-plan criterion or whole investigation is completed by this source comparison or publication.
<!-- END REPORT BODY 19e41c750f30f8e98f12e6a4992a2d652f9eb12bf59181eab2b8d649335e5e7e -->

</details>

# May interview: first page located in an already-held official release

Research date: November 2, 2026, America/New_York. Verification receipts may carry November 3 UTC. This is a telephone operational-record follow-up, not a new witness interview or a finding about conspiracy.

## What changed

The first page of the June 2002 Nancy and Ronald May interview is **already held in the official FBI PDF**, physical page **73**, production footer **000000769**, printed page **1**. Its investigation field reads **06/05/2002**; its transcription field reads **06/06/2002**. It says an assistant U.S. attorney from the **Eastern District of Virginia** was present. These are statements and fields in the released report, not independent authentication of the interview, participants or dates. [Official first page](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=73)

The earlier 73-page reproduction remains unchanged: its physical page 70/footer **000000768** is followed by physical page 71/footer **000000770**, printed continuation page **2**. Locating 769 in a different entity does not insert it into that reproduction, explain the selection/redaction history, or prove that an agency removed a page. The [preceding report](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/56e7780d85b9947de12f36dfe614ac1794d3ff78/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-pickup-hq1492-disposition-2026-11-02/README.md) described a gap in that particular held copy. It must no longer be read as though the first-page component is unlocated across all our holdings. [Mirror continuation](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=71)

## New source comparison

| Record / source | Physical PDF page; footer; printed page | What is actually observed | Limit |
|---|---|---|---|
| September 12, 2001 Las Vegas response, third page; official | 72; 000000747; 3 | Four receiving-office entries: Dallas, New York, Washington Field and Counterterrorism, each instructed to **Read and Clear**. Header reference is **265D-NY-280350**. | A routing instruction, not proof of dispatch, receipt, execution, global case closure, or a recording's disposition. |
| June 2002 May interview, first page; official | 73; 000000769; 1 | Investigation **06/05/2002**, transcription **06/06/2002**, file **265A-NY-280350**; EDVA assistant U.S. attorney reported present. | Interview and transcription are different date roles. These dates are not a contradiction merely because they differ. Keep **265A** distinct from the response's **265D**; no reason for the designations is inferred. |
| June 2002 May continuation; official | 74; 000000770; 2 | Continuation date **06/05/2002**. Reports Nancy took notes and wrote down supplied airline telephone numbers during the call. Also reports one-minute-or-less duration and a background male voice she believed was another crew member. | Recollections in an interview report, not recovered notes, recorded audio, measured duration or independent speaker identification. |
| Same-numbered continuation; mirror | 71; 000000770; 2 | The selected note-taking, duration and background-voice passages agree in their reported substance with official page 74. | Same-family / related-document content is not a new independent witness or a second independently authenticated call. No whole-page byte-equivalence claim. |
| Next May continuation; official | 75; 000000771; 3 | Continuation date **06/05/2002**; reports attempted further contact without a conversation. | Identifying portions remain masked. This unit does not join the attempt to a particular phone event, the workplace report, or any recovered recording. |
| Next official page / stop boundary | 76; 000000827 | Different record and footer, not the mirror's workplace footer 772. | Unrelated content excluded; page 77 not inspected. This is not a finding that 772 is absent from every release. |

Sources: [official 72](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=72), [73](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=73), [74](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=74), [75](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=75), and [mirror 71](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=71). Source byte hashes, acquisition times and reading scope are in [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c7ea8d12c0264f27c5a2). Page numbers here are one-based physical PDF pages, not production-footer numbers.

### Different redactions and coverage remain separate

Official page 74 and mirror page 71 share footer **000000770**, but they are not interchangeable page images. The official copy masks airline-employee identifying fields that are visible in the reproduction; excluded personal portions also differ in coverage/redaction. We do not fill the official masks from the mirror or reproduce those personal portions. Shared report wording and footer do not establish the complete copying, review or release history. A release/version manifest or authoritative page-accounting record is still needed to test why the forms differ.

### What the notes reference does—and does not—supply

Both selected continuations report that Nancy took notes during the call and wrote down the airline numbers. **Neither selected passage says those notes were given to the FBI.** No actual note set, evidence item, receipt, transfer, preservation decision or examination result was recovered in this unit. The first-page EDVA participation statement supplies an additional office to check for existing interview-related records; it does not establish that that office received the notes, created a particular record, or retains it now. No contact or request was made.

## Conflicting records and remaining joins

1. **Mirror workplace report:** the preceding reading of mirror page 73/footer **000000772** prints investigation **06/10/2001**, transcription **06/28/2002**, and a **09/11/2001** narrative. These values remain unchanged and unresolved. This unit did not locate or adopt an official counterpart to that report; the June 5/6 family-interview fields are from a different record and cannot correct or authenticate it. Its reported unanswered call / no answering machine or voicemail remains a report, not a recovered message or native caller-ID log. [Prior assessment](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/56e7780d85b9947de12f36dfe614ac1794d3ff78/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-pickup-hq1492-disposition-2026-11-02/README.md); [mirror 73](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=73).
2. **Earlier response timing:** the previously examined Dallas relay's **6:05 a.m. Pacific** and official September 12 response's **6:13 a.m., no printed zone**, remain source-specific observations. This new first page does not independently clock the call or choose between those accounts. [Preserved holdings and limits](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/56e7780d85b9947de12f36dfe614ac1794d3ff78/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-pickup-hq1492-disposition-2026-11-02/HOLDINGS-CHAIN.md).
3. **Pickup / HQ1492 / WF334 / HQ366:** no new collector identity, callback result, media item, receipt, return response or completed disposition was located. The first page and routing page do not establish a common recording, alias, caller, recipient or evidence item across these controls. Earlier differing names, AM/PM entries, dates, assignments and NO flag remain in the [unchanged prior record](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/56e7780d85b9947de12f36dfe614ac1794d3ff78/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-pickup-hq1492-disposition-2026-11-02/OBSERVATIONS.json).

## A limitation in our own search method

The existing ordinary-text indexes for official September parts 01, 02 and 08 were searched for exact date/footer patterns and the already public May names. Only the May-name query returned the useful part-02 page-73 locator. The exact **769** footer and **06/05/2002** date patterns did not match those indexes, even though both are visible on page 73. An exact-text miss therefore cannot establish that a page or record is missing. This is a demonstrated limit of our navigation method, not evidence that the agency hid the image or altered the underlying record. [Search metadata](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d1830aba1d5a6d4e2b93).

## Reading, preservation and verification limits

This unit inspected **five selected official page faces (72–76)** and **one reused mirror face (71)**. It did not read every body in the 78- or 73-page entities, recover hidden text, reconstruct masked identities, download another copy, repeat the completed pickup/provider searches, or inspect original notes/audio/native phone data. The already-held September response pages 70–71 and mirror workplace/continuation readings outside these six faces are reused prior assessments, not newly tested here.

Original PDFs, earlier publications and conflicting observations remain unchanged. The new report is an additive derivative with a [revision diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-157bec17de527546c66d), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5134c5e81bd405b3e488), [method](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d338b99286fb8d50a6cd), and [open inputs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-589a70bde0d08e0a3845). Verification checks source/bundle bytes and reproduces the declared index matches; manual image readings remain explicitly manual. It does not authenticate the call or interview, identify a collector, establish misconduct, or complete the five-track original-records goal. The most useful next evidence is the actual note set and its provenance, an authoritative interview/version inventory, and any collection/receipt/return/disposition documents that explicitly join the recording controls.
