# May-family response status: a released September 12 record is already held

This is an **additive status qualification** to [the relay/request review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6d775a5c70c1f7793dde9a2200d5c456e2779883/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-may-family-relays-2026-11-02/README.md). **We already hold an official released September 12, 2001 Las Vegas-to-Dallas communication reporting a meeting with Ronald and Nancy May and a local covered disposition.** It was acquired, read and [published in the October 31 review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/fb0d56eeaa07cfc9348306d1cedbcbea059fc5c8/investigation/broader-evidence/fbi-keyton-acis-originals-2026-10-31/README.md). It must not remain classified as a wholly missing May-family interview/response category.

The parent review correctly says its five selected relay/request faces do not themselves supply the completed result. Broader TODO wording about obtaining the family report and Las Vegas response needs this holdings qualification. **The response document is held; underlying notes/audio, exact file-version/serial joins, carrier data and independent authentication remain separate gaps.** This does not rewrite prior sources or claim a new download.

## Exact held record and actual source content

[Official FBI September part02, physical 70–71](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=70), production footers **000000745–746**, is dated **09/12/2001**, **To: Dallas / International Terrorism**, **From: Las Vegas / Squad 8**. It expressly references **265D-NY-280350 Serial 79** and lists the same NY/WF/HQ case families as the new relay memorandum.

[Physical 70, Details](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=70), says agents met **RONALD and NANCY MAY** on **September 12, 2001**. It records Nancy's account of a call from her daughter Renee at **“6:13 a.m.”** on **September 11, 2001**, reporting six hijackers and movement toward the rear, after which Nancy was asked to contact the airline. **No timezone is printed for 6:13.** The named interviewees appear openly in this released body; masked agent names are not reconstructed or filled from another source.

[Physical 71](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=71), says Ronald then contacted American Airlines with the information Nancy received, that neither had additional information to report, and **“No further investigation by Las Vegas at this time, this lead should be considered covered.”** Preserve **by Las Vegas**, **at this time** and **this lead**: this is not entire-case closure, a finding that no other office acted, or independent verification of the call's carrier/device/clock/voice.

These passages are a released **interview/response account**, not merely a request to interview. They report a completed meeting and local handling status; we have not independently established the meeting from native notes, nor identified raw audio, a signed separate closure order or all later activity. Do not invent a requirement that only an unredacted native file counts as a held record, and do not promote a released narrative to empirical carrier validation.

## Preserve the clocks and the source chain

| Record | Printed time / source role |
| --- | --- |
| Parent memorandum physical 53 | **about 6:05 a.m., pacific time**; airline-to-FBI relay |
| DL99 physical60/61 | **AROUND 6:05 PACIFIC TIME**; event field **6:05 AM** |
| Official response physical 70 | **6:13 a.m.**, no printed timezone; reported family interview account |

All values remain unchanged. No controlling time, unprinted timezone, clock conversion, exact elapsed difference or native-row association is accepted. A reported family account and relays based on that family account are not independent confirmations or extra calls. The response distinguishes **Nancy receiving** the call and **Ronald forwarding** information to the airline; preserve those roles rather than flattening all versions into one recipient statement. Native timing bases, original notes, carrier/session/clock and canvass records would test the joins.

The response's typed **Serial 79** is a concrete retrieval key. The earlier memorandum has matching subject/routing/case context, but its faint handwritten serial-like notation remains **unadopted/uncertain**. We do not force that glyph from the expected answer or declare a unique request-to-response/version join. Native serial/index/dispatch/receipt records would establish that mapping.

The previously held staff report's **note 261** cites a September12 Ronald/Nancy May FBI interview. This held communication is consistent with that date/participants and supplies an interview account; exact identification of the edition/file the staff used remains unverified. The underlying interview record category is partially satisfied by the official released report; raw notes/media/carrier and source-version requirements remain open. The parent report's separate WorldTradeTowers/Pentagon description conflict and agreement of its two 6:05 relay bodies are unchanged.

## Source provenance, revision and acceptance

The official PDF has **78 pages**, **5,561,167 bytes**, SHA-256 `055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51`, acquired **2026-10-31T22:04:24.920527+00:00**. Prior two source rasters were reused and re-viewed; no new PDF/image download, public query, OCR, audio/ASR or technical-owner experiment occurred. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ab00cb8a06d8c5374c3e) records exact page/footer/image/earlier-publication pins and limits. Official hosting supports release provenance, not independent historical authentication of every assertion.

Initial raster-generation receipts said pending when created. Later final reading/publication records supersede that initial work-status cue; pending on an old receipt is not proof the source is still unread. Our qualification corrects a holdings/status omission, not historical evidence or an old witness statement.

The earlier public relay report and source bytes remain unchanged. This uniquely named candidate adds a source-status qualification and actual predecessor [text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a78d43b619146d506bf8); it does not overwrite the old report. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ef6a97905e88965a9e7c), [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3d90cba0b9f0a13b914e), [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3680a4069f27536e05dc) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d052f1b9a74d5bca0f90) distinguish held documents, source claims and unperformed tests. Four related entries are not independent corroboration. Checks cover bytes/manual locators/preservation, not independent source transcription or call authentication.

A held released interview/response component is now explicitly recognized. **No whole TEL01–TEL04 requirement or original five-track goal is completed**: native carrier/operator/office/clock/canvass/media/custody and other required originals/tests remain unresolved. The qualification is proposed on a separate non-overwriting review branch/PR against the parent relay branch, without merge or history rewrite.

# Telephone originals: two note sheets and an operational lead-assignment trail

October 31, 2026 Eastern / November 1 UTC. The [official FBI September interviews, part 06 of 08](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-06-of-08/view) supplies specific original-note and lead-completion targets. The evidence here consists of rendered agency records, not the underlying notes, carrier exports, original audio or native lead database. All eighteen original five-track requirements remain open/partial.

## 1. A report says two contemporaneous note sheets were given to agents

**Viewer pages 1–2**, internal pages 1–2, are an FD-302-style recipient interview with transcription date **09/11/2001**, file reference **265D-NY-280350**, and **REQ. #35-13** footers **000000598–000000599**. It describes two calls involving Peter Hanson.

**Page 2, note-handoff paragraph**, says the recipient took **two sheets of notes during the two phone calls** and **provided those notes to the interviewing agents**. This is a concrete claim of an original-note handoff. The note sheets themselves, an evidence-item identifier, storage location, signed receipt and subsequent custody history were not acquired here. Do not assume a 1A location merely because other telephone records mention 1A material. These are described as the recipient's notes, distinct from an agent's interview notes and the typed FD-302.

Other source distinctions matter:

- Page 1 places the first call **sometime before 9:00 a.m.**, followed by a second **within a matter of minutes**. Those are recollections; no explicit zone or calibrated start/end pair is supplied in the adopted passages.
- The recipient **believed** the caller was using a cellular telephone. The report records a supplied number but the public copy masks it. This is not verified service or a carrier-row join. The page-2 concern about a callback making the phone ring is another statement from the same account, not independent confirmation of the device.
- Page 1 reports an immediate notification to **Easton Police Department**, including a named captain. The original police call, intake/incident log, any recording and any contemporaneous police notes are additional retrieval targets. No such notification record was obtained in this pass.
- Page 2 explicitly leaves the abrupt ending uncertain: the recipient did not know whether the caller hung up or the phone malfunctioned. Neither explanation, nor a physical cause, is established here.

The most useful next comparison is the actual two note sheets against the typed report, original interview material, police notification records and call-specific carrier data. The sheets could preserve earlier wording or timing, but their contents must not be invented from the report. Two sheets and two calls do not establish two independent witnesses or a complete network-attempt count. The masked recipient identity remains masked; unrelated personal/profile fields are excluded.

## 2. PG1581 separates a newspaper-derived claim from an interview assignment

**Viewer pages 13–15**, footers **000000259–000000261**, contain a Pittsburgh-to-Pittsburgh FBI communication dated **09/22/2001**. Its case ID is **265A-PG-69744 (PENDING)**; its synopsis and embedded **Information Control Form** identify **PG1581**. The printed title is **UNITED FLIGHT 93**, followed by **PFLP**. That second label is retained literally and unexpanded: its administrative meaning is not established here, and it does not by itself identify attackers, affiliation or responsibility. “Pending” is a historical printed status, not a verified current case status.

| Source field/location | Printed value or description | Limit |
|---|---|---|
| Page 13, communication date | **09/22/2001** | Not the date of the in-flight call or proof of dispatch/receipt. |
| Page 13, method and information-received fields | **Written; 09/16/2001; 12:00 AM** | Native field meanings, entry history, clock precision and defaults remain unknown. |
| Page 13, Event narrative | A **September 16 Pittsburgh Post-Gazette article** is said to describe a **13-minute conversation** involving Todd Beamer and a GTE supervisor. | An attributed newspaper statement, not an independent FBI duration measurement. The actual article was not acquired or read here. |
| Page 14, event fields | **09/11/2001; 12:00 AM** | Retain the printed time. Do not treat it as an independently measured midnight call, silently correct it or declare it a default. |
| Pages 13–14, priority/lead fields | **IMMEDIATE; Lead Required: YES** | Priority and an instruction are not proof of notification, interview execution or completion. |
| Page 15, assignment | **PG1581; 09/21/2001 at 1:40 AM**, Pittsburgh | An assignment field, not a calibrated call clock or completed-interview return. |
| Same page, instruction | **“IF NOT ALREADY DONE”**, locate/interview the masked GTE supervisor regarding the Beamer call. | The qualifier does not establish that no earlier interview had occurred. Do not infer a ten-day failure or ignored witness from the dates alone. |

These different date/time roles remain side by side. No preferred event time, correction, source default or explanation for the document/assignment ordering has been selected. The two midnight labels, assignment, priority and newspaper-derived narrative are not independent corroborations of a historical call. This form also does not resolve the various duration and holding-period descriptions preserved in earlier operator-record reviews; those require recording, phase, clock and source joins.

### Exact cross-references to pursue

The page-13 Event paragraph cites **GTE Airfone Serial 348, case 265A-NY-280350**. Its continuation on page 14 separately refers to **Serial #8**, without reprinting an explicit case namespace alongside that reference. Preserve the two references rather than assigning both automatically to one file or assuming their contents agree.

Next targets: the actual referenced serials and their enclosure/custody records; PG1581's native intake/assignment/edit history; its completion, clearance, deconfliction or return record; the actual newspaper article; and case/control-title records needed to understand the printed context. A bounded check of one already held 73-page public mirror's existing text index did not locate PG1581 or Serial 348. That is an index-locator result, not proof the records are missing, withheld or destroyed. No cross-reference body was recovered or validated by that check.

Do not identify the masked supervisor or agent by borrowing a name from another release. Similar subject matter alone does not establish that a previously inspected interview is the completion of PG1581. The source communication, embedded form, continuation and lead page belong to one documentary chain; they are not four independent witnesses.

## Preservation, reading scope and open tests

The official item advertises **516,162 PDF bytes** and exposes **15 viewer pages**. Five distinct source pages were inspected: **1, 2, 13, 14 and 15**; pages **3–12** remain unread in this pass. Eight unchanged browser screenshots were retained locally. Two separate local diagnostic enlargements repeat visible page-13 pixels only, using nearest-neighbour scaling, to read the Event references and case caption. They add no detail and are not OCR, generative enhancement, hidden-layer recovery or independent evidence. The original captured bytes remain unchanged.

[SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f2625e8ef5d115c2e37e) records actual page/paragraph/footer locators, captured-view hashes, preservation times and diagnostic methods. The hashes identify browser images, **not a native FBI PDF, original note, audio master or authenticated case database**. No PDF was requested or obtained; no audio, note sheet, native carrier table or lead-return record was acquired. No waveform, device, row-level call-count, voice or independent-clock test was performed.

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-240df0a5dea4e4269d90) retains the original-note, police, carrier and PG1581 dependencies with the full FAA/telephone/recorder/materials/steel plan. Raw images, contact/DOB/profile details, signatures, masked identities, private case material and correspondence are outside this public payload. No outreach, new request, fee, filing, mailbox access, experiment, source alteration, private push or history rewrite occurred. Earlier source records and published assessments remain unchanged; this is record-acquisition progress, not a completed substantive investigation or a conspiracy finding.

# Official telephone-release lead: recording labels and separate reporting channels

Research date: October 31, 2026, Eastern; public-source views and preservation extend into November 1 UTC. Scope: TEL01–TEL04. All original five-track acceptance requirements remain open.

The [FBI Vault September interviews, part 03 of 08](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-03-of-08/view) supplies two useful record-identification leads. Its Ong transcript header and the opening recorded conversation need separate descriptions. A separate Flight 93 report identifies a handwritten statement and an Airphone information relay. Neither is an acquired native recording, activity table or authenticated handwritten original.

## 1. Preserve the transcript header beside its actual opening

**Observed, viewer page 2:** an FD-302-style header prints **DL-336**, case **265D-NY-280350**, and transcription date **09/12/2001**. Its introductory paragraph describes a taped telephone conversation between Betty Ong and the American Airlines Southeast Reservation Center, identifying Winston and Vanessa and Flight 11. The paragraph states September 11, 2001 as today's date.

**Observed, viewer page 3, internal page 2:** the continuation is headed Betty Ong and dated **09/11/2001**. The visible opening speaker states **12:28 p.m. Central Time**, identifies a corporate-security role, and introduces a conversation with Nydia Gonzalez about what occurred that morning. Gonzalez describes her operations-specialist role and an incoming flight-attendant call at approximately **8:20**; the visible passage does not assign that recollection a time zone.

**Interpretation and limits:** the header's broad description and the opening's retrospective interview context are preserved side by side. Do not relabel the entire record, choose a controlling description, treat the 12:28 recording time as the in-flight call time, silently supply a zone to 8:20, or call this an authenticated native recording of Ong. Pages 4–10 were not read in this pass. They could contain further material relevant to the record's scope. Mapping DL-336 to the actual source recording, transcript segments and custody entries remains a TODO. The apparent difference in descriptions does not establish substitution, falsification or an error in the underlying audio.

The [previous SERO inventory review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/d5b279a04f2fe18b78548222aea75451bfa7f60a/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-sero-evidence-inventory-2026-10-31/README.md) preserves a separate September 13 memorandum's original-media inventory, roughly 8:20 call-start descriptions and reported four-minute recording limit. No byte, track, recording-session or calibrated-clock join between that inventory and DL-336 has been established. Similar names or subject matter do not make them the same recording or independent corroboration.

## 2. Flight 93: a statement and a different Airphone relay

**Observed, viewer page 11:** a report dated **9/11/01** bears **Reference Lead Control Number SF:62**, footer **REQ. #35-13**, and **000000156**. It describes interviewing a United employee at San Francisco International Airport. The narrative says a system aircraft-maintenance controller took a Flight 93 flight-attendant call at approximately **6:40 a.m.** The report describes **two male hijackers with knives**. It then says a handwritten statement was supplied and reproduces a statement beginning **0640 PST** at the **STARFIX Desk**. That is a typed representation of a statement, not the inspected handwritten original.

**Separate reported channel, same page and continuation:** the lower page-11 paragraph describes information from Airphone that a passenger had called; it then introduces information relayed by that passenger. Viewer page 12, internal page 2, reports **three males**, **bombs and knives**, no injuries at that time, and a reported Pennsylvania location. Its header shows **265D-NY-280350** and **9/11/01**. It expressly says the interviewee had no further identifiable information on the weapons or explosives.

**Preserve, do not reconcile:** retain the two-men/knives account beside the three-men/bombs-and-knives relay. These are differently attributed communications within a report; they are not established as simultaneous descriptions of one exchange. Neither headcount is selected as controlling. No weapon, explosive, location, caller identity or exact event clock has been independently verified here. The narrative's unqualified 6:40 and the reproduced statement's 0640 PST remain separate clock labels; no PST/PDT conversion or alignment to other flight records was performed. The report, its typed quotation and continuation are not three independent witnesses.

**Concrete next records:** the original handwritten statement, its attachment/intake record, STARFIX/controller call records and recordings, and the underlying Airphone passenger/operator record and relay documentation. Those could test the timing, attribution, completeness and differences; they have not been acquired or tested in this pass.

## Reading, preservation and access limits

The item advertises **2,119,910 bytes** and its viewer exposes **12 pages**. One request to its actually exposed PDF download link returned **HTTP 403**. No PDF binary, original response payload or PDF SHA-256 was obtained. The normal public Document/Pages viewer remained readable; no alternate binary route, header spoof, hidden asset, credential or gate workaround was used.

Five distinct source-page views were inspected: cover 1; page 2; the visible upper portion of 3; page 11, including a fitted view; and page 12. The poor cover was not used to establish an evidence-custody receipt. Initial loading frames were not interpreted as blank or missing original pages. Page 11's fitted view exposed its lower relay paragraph and prevented treating its two accounts as necessarily one event. Small or cropped fields outside the adopted observations remain unverified.

Four unaltered browser screenshots were retained locally with hashes in [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-712c9806c9a98fcdc6ea). **Their hashes identify captured viewer images, not a native FBI PDF or audio recording.** Raw screenshots, telephone/contact details, redacted identities, signatures and private case material are excluded from this public payload. Source-page paragraphs provide the locators; no artificial line numbers are attributed to scanned pages.

The finding is a traceable record-and-recording identification lead. It does not establish a conspiracy, foreknowledge, fabricated calls, explosives or a complete production. The original activity reports, carrier records, native recordings, clock definitions and custody joins remain [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b32f2fad556b0d84ec95). No agency contact, new request, fee, experiment, private push or source alteration occurred. Prior source records and published assessments remain unchanged.

### Silent-input control failed; court speech was not transcribed

**Source unit:** `telephone-offline-reading-control-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-14c8d9b88406b154490e).

<details>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 7bb21f69292085bdf685f6b2eca09f48e0f8880faefbef3fbe2a2ffe11a8d3ba -->
# Silent-input control failed; court speech was not transcribed

**This is a test of our reading method, not a finding about 9/11.** The installed recognizer produced **“You” from ten seconds of all-zero audio samples**. That failed the predeclared control, so the planned court-audio passes were stopped. No words from the court audio were newly transcribed or authenticated.

## What was actually tested

On November2,2026, a WAV containing160000mono samples at16000Hz/16bits was generated directly from320000zero bytes. A separate file inspection of that saved WAV confirmed every sample byte was zero, the header fields and the10second duration. WAV SHA-256: `ee7bea4232762775f8fce9b3e27e4d3948c8ac6a45a87ca769f703d6eed0b448`.

One local **whisper.cpp1.9.2 /base.en** pass used CPUonly, English,4threads/1processor, temperature0 and no temperature fallback. No initial prompt, desired words, grammar, VAD or translation was supplied. The OS process profile denied network; the permission probe returnedEPERM. Existing installed tools/weights were reused, with no download or source upload. [CONTROL-RESULT.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-671bc6e31936ad99bdb4) pins the exact engine/model/input/output hashes, command template, timing, options and observed outcome.

Inference exited0, but its text file contained `You` followed by a newline. **Successful execution and a passed control are different outcomes.** The frozen criterion required empty stripped text. The observed nonempty output therefore failed that criterion; it was not erased, excluded, retuned or rerun to obtain a pass.

## What stopped, and what remains unknown

The source court archive was checked before and after the job. The intended Ong event/stream payload extraction and both speech passes were **never reached**. The only new derived audio was the synthetic silent WAV; the only inference directory was the silent-control run. No Flash/ActionScript was executed, no actual call was played or submitted to the recognizer, and no court-word timing, voice or source identity was measured.

The [preceding printed-passage comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/a8b410fc96d29f50f9c134f87acb0822eedc8961/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-playback-text-comparison-2026-11-02/README.md) preserves the **I'm number five /Our number5**, speaker-label, cockpit-question and **Somebody/nobody** differences, with their agreements and alignment limits. This failed synthetic control supplies **no new reading of any of those passages**, selects neither printed account and changes no historical conclusion.

One false word on synthetic silence does not estimate the recognizer's speech-error rate or show that every transcript it generates is wrong. Conversely, repeating a plausible word is not independent authentication. A different engine/platform/configuration could behave differently; that was not tested. We did not alter this probe's settings, switch models or run additional controls after its failure. The result is a reason to keep machine text provisional, not a cause assigned to the historical source differences.

## Reproduction and scope

[reproduce_control.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-540b00837ee607e36a80) provides the control-only command with exclusive output creation, pinned engine/model checks, an explicit network-denial probe and no source-media input. Supply the matching existing engine and model paths; no automatic downloads or installation. The original full pipeline executed the documented call. This standalone wrapper was syntax-checked and compared to its options, **not rerun** after the frozen stop condition. Its WAV generator was separately checked in memory against the actual input bytes.

The observed engine binary SHA-256 is `40bca494d49af736058eb3f33cbcebaa020eacf6d0087b623f334946e1ab2128`; model SHA-256 `a03779c86df3323075f5e796cb2ce5029f00ec8869eee3fdfb897afe36c6d002`/147964211bytes. These identify the tested assets; they do not promise identical output on other installations. Public files include selected synthetic results and authored reproducibility material, excluding raw logs, machine transcript files, court audio, model weights and private case data.

The native session/CD/tape/copy/custody/segment records and independent source listening needed to test the printed differences remain missing. Existing FAA correction/disposition, telephone native/release/operator/recipient, recorder configuration/paired-output/clocks, materials specimen/calibration and steel preservation/attribution/review-delivery requirements remain open under their original criteria. ### A later operator interview identifies original notes and qualifies the reported counts

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY a0c82e0e8d10d467602650c077ae7530ed993fe0736d8e00c0003524ad40c501 -->
# A later operator interview identifies original notes and qualifies the reported counts

October 31, 2026, America/New_York; capture November 1 UTC. **TEL01–TEL04 / AUD016-A1, TC05-B105 and TC06-B110.** A newly inspected three-page public reproduction of a May 11, 2004 Commission interview memorandum identifies a concrete missing-record target: Jefferson's brief Post-it notes, reportedly held by the FBI. It also supplies an explicit qualification about reported passenger counts and another silent-line estimate. These are statements in a later interview record, not authenticated original notes or independently measured call events.

## Source, provenance and reading scope

The [public reader](https://www.scribd.com/document/18886438/T7-B12-Flight-93-Calls-Todd-Beamer-Fdr-5-11-04-MFR-Lisa-Jefferson-416) labels the upload as a call transcript, but its actual body is headed **MEMORANDUM FOR THE RECORD**, dated **May 11, 2004**, prepared by **John Raidt**, Team **7**, and describes a briefing by Lisa Jefferson. The uploader is **9/11 Document Archive**, a third-party mirror. Neither the platform's AI-enhanced title/description nor its catalogue ranking establishes document type, truth or agency custodianship. The reproduced historical Commission Sensitive / Unclassified markings are retained as provenance; no classification or release-authority conclusion is inferred from them.

All three displayed pages were read in the normally accessible reader text and visible images. Five adopted viewport captures cover the three pages; a sixth captured page 3 while loading and is retained only as a diagnostic. The subsequent loaded page 3 is readable. A transient blank frame is not a blank original, deletion or missing-page finding. No login, free trial, download gate, hidden API or guessed asset route was used.

The browser DOM/text capture is **54,260 UTF-8 bytes**, SHA-256 **90ecfacf04cb7fdf9cd2a1fc339262efe43ff94aaeb32c3e1358fb1f6183264a**. That fingerprints a captured reader representation, including its fragmentation and interface material; it is not a native PDF or agency-master hash. Raw source text/images and incidental personal/interface details remain outside this public payload. [Source fingerprints](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8797a70f97e7f0ccbf8d), [exact printed-page/paragraph and captured-line pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-21cbbde05d74a6d7f587), [scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a62dff73105eda3baa3d).

## Original notes: a specified form and reported holder

**S1 printed page 3, Additional clarifications, second paragraph** says Jefferson made only a few basic notes on a small Post-it pad and that the FBI held them. The same passage reports her view that they would add little information. Preserve both parts; this is neither a recovered note nor an independent appraisal of its evidentiary value. The interview occurred in 2004 and does not prove present retention, completeness, original acquisition date or a particular evidence barcode. P02.

The already held [official FBI interview report, physical page 45](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-01-of-08/at_download/file#page=45), says notes, a typed event summary and a screen print were handed to the interviewing agent. That separately specifies attachment and receipt targets. It does not, without an item/copy/custody join, identify those notes as the same physical Post-it material described in 2004. A typed synopsis, a screen print, original notes and later recorded recollection remain distinct artifacts. No redacted identity is reconstructed. P03.

**S1 page 3, first clarification** records uncertainty about whether the first operator, Johnson, took notes. Preserve that uncertainty. It does not establish either that she made none or that no such record survived. Obtain the individual originals, not a collective assumption about both operators. P04.

## Counts: the memorandum adds a provenance qualification

**S1 page 2, third paragraph** reports **10 in front / 27 in back / 5 flight attendants**, through an account attributed to the flight attendant. Its parenthetical note says the description reflects the flight manifest rather than contemporaneous distribution within the cabin. That is the memorandum's qualification; the actual manifest, initial notes and interviewer drafting basis are not attached or independently checked here. It cannot be treated as a verified explanation of every earlier count. P05.

Keep the [earlier transcript reproduction](https://www.scribd.com/document/18886478/T7-B12-Flight-93-Calls-Todd-Beamer-Fdr-9-15-01-FBI-302-Transcript-UAL-SAC-Nick-Leonard-Re-Jefferson-Beamer-Call-419) beside it: viewer **5 / Bates M-NKA-00012173** reports **27 front / 10 back**, while viewer **10 / Bates M-NKA-00012178** reports **10 front / 27 back**. Neither allocation is selected, repaired or converted into measured seating positions. The shared total **37** is only arithmetic on reported numbers, not an authenticated manifest or independent headcount. P06.

## Timing and information paths remain unjoined

**S1 page 3, opening paragraph** reports a **15-minute** silent/open-line interval, followed by news of a crash and termination. The [earlier official operator account, physical pages 4–5](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=4) reports **20 minutes** of silent-open time and a separate **approximately 5-minute** later-contact estimate. The September follow-up reproduction, viewer **19–20 / Bates 12187–12188**, reports **about 10 more minutes** holding and news **about 10 minutes later**. These are separate estimates and phase descriptions. No sum, controlling endpoint, clock conversion or identification with billed duration is imposed. P07.

**S1 page 1, notification/handoff paragraphs** describes the **Airplane Operations Surveillance Center (AOSC)** notifying authorities and an FBI connection remaining with AOSC while the operators spoke with the caller. This specifies a relay/bridge record target. It does not demonstrate an FBI recording, prove direct monitoring of every word or authenticate a particular communications leg. Preserve the prior reported lack of local recording capability alongside this reported relay path; different paths have not been joined or tested. P08.

**S1 page 3, third clarification** distinguishes earlier events being recounted to the operator from events occurring during the call. The final clarification is explicitly tentative about another operator listening to a different passenger's call. Neither passage authenticates a clock, carrier assignment or recording of that separate call. They caution against treating all reported detail as simultaneously and independently overheard. P09.

## Next originals and limits

The immediate targets are the individual original Post-it/other operator notes, attachment inventories and transfer receipts; original September 14 letter/synopsis and enclosures; interview notes/recording and draft/version records behind the 2004 memorandum; the actual manifest and source of its parenthetical qualification; and AOSC/FBI relay, bridge, watch-log and any recording/disposition records. Native carrier/operator identifiers, field definitions, clock rules, actual media and complete production/exhibit joins remain open. No new contact or request was sent.

The strongest limitation is source dependence: a 2004 staff memorandum recording one operator's recollection, a 2001 interview summary and a later transcript reproduction are different records, not necessarily independent measurements. Memory, source copying, wording, transcription, scope or other causes could matter; none is selected as a resolution. These observations do not establish fabricated calls, concealment, foreknowledge or attack participation. They identify records that can test those interpretations in either direction.

The separate September 14 letter was **not** recovered in this search. The new substantive advance is the actually inspected interview record's specified note form/holder, count qualification and phase/relay claims. Earlier originals, conflicting values, public history and shared registers remain unchanged. All original five tracks and 18 requirements remain open or partial; publication is not empirical completion or independent audit acceptance.

[Comparison and discriminating records](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a4bad4f52acd24c7c4f7), [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-06cb9997232f9f0f9550), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e01859965e3c38b69386), [verification limits](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a0eb58e62c28fc578be9) and [additive text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-fc1adcc9f68bd57088a0) accompany these redundant public research notes. No raw privacy-marked source, full speech, contact/financial/access/signature data, private correspondence, litigation strategy or credentials are published. Prior [recording-index review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6e75761061f0bfa9148eb98a213aa4a24f1ad123/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-recording-index-2026-10-31/README.md) remains unchanged.

</details>

# FBI-hosted counterpart preserves the Flight 99 reading and engineering-record lead

The already-held FBI release supplies a counterpart for the previously examined mirror: **physical 18–19 of part 02** visibly retain the **flight 99** recollection, screen-print/engineering passage and **serial 3255** citation. This strengthens the provenance of those selected passages. It is a second copy of a related report, **not independent corroboration that a call, print transfer or engineering analysis occurred**.

The referenced interview itself remains unacquired. The exact serial lookup returned this same held PDF, not the separate cited record. No original print, engineering receipt/result, recording or native session was recovered.

## Sources and actual scope

The [FBI-hosted release](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=18) was acquired **October 31, 2026, 22:04:24 UTC**. It contains **78 pages**, **5,561,167 bytes**, SHA-256 **`055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51`**. This unit newly read complete page faces **18–19**, internal **1–2**, production footers **000000370–371**. It did not download the source again or establish its current HTTP availability.

The [earlier mirror, physical 23–24](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=23), has SHA-256 **`e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`**. Its previously inspected fields are retained in the [preceding review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6d9d2c9684c29602cf5bb570cbf38c46e09fd50d/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-first-operator-followup-2026-11-02/README.md). Shared case/date/footer and selected wording support a documentary-counterpart comparison; they do not authenticate a complete release/assembly chain or make the entities byte-identical.

Only nonpersonal independently visible fields are compared. Masked official names and contacts were **not filled from the more-visible mirror**. No whole-file read, new OCR, hidden-content extraction, audio/ASR, voice identification, native clock or carrier replication occurred. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5fb44ccd9a709f3e30f9) distinguishes new readings from reused context and source-entity hashes from historical authentication.

## What the official page visibly supports

| Field or passage | Official part 02 | Earlier mirror | Limit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Case/date/footer | **265A-NY-280350-302**; investigation **06/05/2002**; transcription/dictation **06/11/2002**; footers **370–371** | Same selected labels | Documentary association, not original authorship/custody. |
| Initial call receipt | **Approximately 8:40 a.m. Central**, physical 18, first substantive paragraph | Same reported estimate | Not calibrated call start or independent clock evidence. |
| Recalled flight number | **United flight 99**, physical 18, final substantive paragraph | **United flight 99** | The recall qualification remains. Neither page says the screen displayed 99. |
| Referenced interview | **265A-NY-280350-OUT, serial 3255**, physical 19, first paragraph | Same pointer | The citing document is not the cited serial. |
| Administrative screen print | Reportedly made when the call disconnected; information **was to be passed** to engineering, physical 19, final paragraph | Same reported creation/intended action | Actual print, dispatch/receipt, analysis and result still missing. |

The **99** reading is visible in the agency-hosted page as well as the mirror; it is not merely a value introduced by our new summary or an adopted OCR line. The earlier [supervisor account, part 08 physical 4](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-08-of-08/at_download/file#page=4), reports **United Airlines Flight 93**. Both values remain with their source roles. Repetition in copies of a report does not independently establish what was spoken, which aircraft or native session was involved, or why the values differ.

The screen-print passage remains evidence of what the report says was made and intended. It is not the original print or completed engineering work. The visible serial reference provides a precise retrieval target but does not identify the held supervisor copy as serial 3255. One exact public query for the case/serial returned the same held part 02 entity; it supplied no separate original and was not followed by a redundant download.

## What cannot be transferred across copies

The official personnel fields are masked, including positions involved in the earlier mirror’s surname-variation observation. **That observation remains mirror-specific.** This review neither independently confirms those names at the masked positions nor assigns an identity to an official blank. Source redaction differences are preserved without reconstructing what was withheld or asserting that a particular omission was improper.

The official first page also visibly carries an unclassified-information stamp dated **01-26-2007**. The interview/document fields say **June 2002**, while the collection filename contains **2001-09**. Preserve these distinct field/object roles. The stamp is not substituted for an interview, original creation or public-release date; the filename does not establish when every included document was created. No classification-law finding, later alteration, deletion or selected explanation follows from these labels.

## Remaining records and evidentiary weight

The useful change is a stronger source-location/provenance anchor for selected nonpersonal wording, coupled with a clearer limit: it does not authenticate every unmasked field in the mirror or supply the cited interview. Multiple copies can share the same underlying statement and mistakes. They are not independent witnesses or measurements.

Next discriminators remain the actual **serial 3255** with cover/continuations/enclosures and source-version association; the administrative print with field/session definitions; engineering dispatch/receipt/work order/result/disposition; original notes/media, caller/session and clock/custody joins. Existing **99/93**, timing/hold and mirror-only surname observations stay preserved and unresolved. No fabrication, deliberate alteration, concealment or historical cause is established.

All five tracks and 18 original criteria remain open or partial; prior actual analyses remain performed. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ea55fb172ab5df448a45) retains them. [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d788794976ef816535e2) contains four related source/provenance entries, not four independent confirmations. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-79cdd01e5c54b3a0a10d), [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a0e5580b9c4425937eb0) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d5ec93e3507434a80264) distinguish source reading, preservation and publication from unperformed native tests. Raw PDFs/images/full interviews, contacts, signatures, masked identities and private material are excluded. Publication is not main-branch adoption or substantive goal completion.

### PG1012: a reported telephone story and an instruction to disregard it

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY e9a3b30e010cabc2de9c5e1f5cc11604d61fbca06c65c61c5b1dd3b948416698 -->
# PG1012: a reported telephone story and an instruction to disregard it

Review date: October 31, 2026, America/New_York; source captures November 1, 2026, 03:22–03:23 UTC.

The useful new record is an FBI information-control form that preserves both a relayed story about a Flight 93 passenger and a later instruction to disregard it. It also identifies a fax/control number and says an EC was drafted. These are concrete retrieval targets. The released form alone establishes neither the story's truth nor whether the instruction was justified. It is not evidence, by itself, of deliberate suppression.

## Exact source and reading scope

[Official FBI Vault, Interviews September 2001, part 06 of 08](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-06-of-08/view): viewer pages **7–9 of 15**, printed release footers **000000245–000000247**, request label **REQ. #35-13**, control **PG1012**. Page 7's communication is dated **09/20/2001**, Pittsburgh to Pittsburgh, case **265A-PG-69744 (PENDING)**, precedence **IMMEDIATE**. “PENDING” is the historical printed label, not a current case-status determination. Read all three selected pages; no claim to read the whole 15-page item in this unit.

Five original browser screenshots were preserved unchanged and hashed, with two separate visible-pixel enlargement diagnostics for reading. Capture hashes, exact capture times and page/field locators appear in [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-12727a60cfc18873d0ea). These are hashes of screenshots, not of the FBI PDF, original email, fax or native database. A separate ordinary request for the part06 root PDF endpoint returned HTTP 403; no PDF body or PDF hash was acquired, and that route was stopped. Successful viewer access and a failed direct request are both preserved without inferring removal or motive.

## The two accounts, preserved side by side

**Page 7, Event paragraph, footer 000000245:** the masked source “PROVIDED AN E-MAIL,” described as received from a radio-club member. The paragraph says the email related information from a person on United 93. Its exact relevant wording includes:

> PASSENGERS EVENTUALLY (?) HAD THE HIJACKERS. PASSENGER'S NAME - JEROME GLICK. HIS WIFE TELEPHONED CALL TO 911.

The question mark and wording are retained, not repaired into a clearer story. The paragraph then says **“FAXED COPY OF CONTROL NUMBER HQ1409 RECEIVED FROM SIOC.”** This is an account of an email and fax, not either underlying item, a direct passenger interview, a verified 911 call or a carrier record. The source and preparer remain masked. Their identities are not inferred from other releases.

**Page 8, opening continuation, footer 000000246:** after a masked field, the record says:

> FAXED TO PITTSBURGH 9/16 AT 3:45 A.M. INSTRUCTED TO DISREGARD AS WIFE OF JEROME GLICK IS IN SAN FRANCISCO. EC DRAFTED.

The location assertion and instruction are preserved as the record's claims. This review did not establish who issued the instruction, what particular part of the tip was to be disregarded, how the location was checked, or what the drafted EC said. It also did not establish whether “EC DRAFTED” identifies the displayed September 20 communication itself or a separate record. No separate missing memo is presumed to exist merely from that phrase.

**Pages 8–9, review and lead fields:** “Lead Required?: YES” remains on page 8. Page 9 records assignment of **PG1012** on **09/15/2001 at 7:55 PM**, with the instruction **“CONDUCT VICTIM FAMILY INTERVIEW OF”** followed by a masked field. This is an assignment entry, not proof an interview occurred or failed to occur. The coexistence of the interview instruction and disregard note is retained; their operative scope, edit order and later disposition have not been recovered.

## Dates are different administrative fields

| Printed field and locator | Exact value | What remains unknown |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Information Received, p.7 | 09/13/2001; 10:40 AM | Source clock, timezone, receipt mechanism and underlying email/fax timestamps |
| Event Date/Time, p.8 | 09/13/2001; 10:40 AM | Whether this marks intake or another event; it is not independently established as a September 11 telephone-call time |
| Assigned To, p.9 | 09/15/2001 at 7:55 PM | Native assignment/history and performance/return |
| Fax narrative, p.8 | 9/16 at 3:45 A.M. | Actual fax header, transmission/receipt, timezone and relation to the instruction |
| Communication header, p.7 | 09/20/2001 | Creation/edit/version/approval sequence and relation to “EC DRAFTED” |

The values have not been combined into a calibrated timeline. Their order is not itself a contradiction: receipt, assignment, forwarding and preparation can be different events. Conversely, an ordinary administrative explanation is not established merely because it is possible. Native records are needed to test the relationships.

There is also a representation difference: the rendered title's second line reads **PFLP**, while the previously returned web-reader text gives **PELP**. Both are logged as representations in SOURCE-PINS.json. Neither is expanded into an affiliation claim. Native byte equivalence and the cause of the rendering/extraction difference were not tested; no record alteration is inferred.

## Significance, strongest objection and next discriminating records

This source narrows a question about how a particular tip was screened and corrected. A plausible competing interpretation is ordinary triage of a garbled, multiply relayed email. The form's own disregard note is contrary evidence against treating the initial story as an established independent confirmation. It does not independently prove the reason for disregarding it, and a city name alone does not identify which assertion was considered mistaken. Missing underlying records do not establish concealment, destruction or that the tip was true.

The best next records are **the original email and relay headers/attachments; SIOC control HQ1409 and the fax with transmission/receipt; the instruction's source and supporting check; the EC or a documented link identifying the September 20 wrapper as that EC; and PG1012's actual interview/return and disposition history**. Any claimed 911 call also needs its own independently matched log/audio/custody/clock record. The masked interview subject is not silently equated with a named person in the narrative. All remain open in [OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-052801c8eeeae4926664); no outreach or new request was sent.

Two exact public searches, recorded in SOURCE-PINS.json, returned this same official record plus an unrelated keyword collision. They did not supply the underlying email, fax or EC. A locator check of the already held 73-page mirror's text index found no exact hits for PG1012, HQ1409, EC DRAFTED or Jerome Glick. This is a limited index result, not proof those materials are absent from every release or archive.

The three pages, their five screenshots, two reading crops and returned text are dependent representations of one control-form record, not independent witnesses or multiple corroborations. No original notes/audio/carrier rows/911 record/native lead database were acquired; no call, voice, waveform, decoder, laboratory or calibrated-clock test was performed. All 18 requirements across the existing FAA, telephone, recorder, materials and steel work plan remain open or partial. This bounded source review does not complete that work.

## Preservation and revision accountability

Earlier reports and conflicting accounts are unchanged. This is an additive study, not a replacement for the prior telephone reviews or a new reconstruction of masked identities. The previously covered office-witness paragraph was not republished as a new discovery. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-92774f6ad6c4c651cc58) tracks the user's requirements; [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-797487c6dc9098b47a74) records actual checks and unperformed work; [TEXT-CHANGES.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-73dd17f9d2773362d355) is the exact addition diff for the five substantive files against no preceding file at this new path. Screenshot/legibility verification is separate from that text diff. Raw images, source PDFs, personal/contact fields, signatures, correspondence, strategy and credentials are outside the public payload.

# Tape-pickup and HQ1492 follow-up: collection outcomes still open, further May records located

November2,2026, America/New_York. TEL01–04; AUD016-A1/TC05-B105/TC06-B110. **No collector-identification callback, evidence receipt, actual tape or completed HQ1492collection/disposition was located in this bounded review.** That is not evidence those records never existed, that the FBI failed to act, or that anything was suppressed. The requested pickup, HQ1492, WF334 and HQ366chains remain unjoined.

The new useful source findings are a later May-family account identifying contemporaneous handwritten notes; a separate report of an **unanswered workplace call with no answering machine/voicemail on the reported telephone**; and a source-specific **first-page/production-footer gap** that limits the held later interview. The workplace report prints **investigation06/10/2001** and **transcription06/28/2002** while describing09/11/2001. Those strings remain unresolved; no year is corrected and no pre-event knowledge inferred.

## Sources and actual scope

The [held public mirror](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf) has73pages/2,997,662bytes, SHA-256 `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`, acquired2026-10-27T13:40:38.310907UTC. It is a third-party reproduction, not an authenticated native FBI master or complete production. Its bytes remain unchanged.

This unit inspected18whole mirror faces: **1–3,51–52,54–59,67–73**. Most are reused context checks; the substantive tail71–73was newly assessed for this question. It also inspected footer-only clips from **all73pages**, plus the already-held official FBI response at **part02physical70–71**. The two official faces are reused, not newly acquired witnesses or an independent authentication of calls. No whole73page body review, new OCR, listening, waveform/voice identification, private-record use or redaction recovery occurred. [Exact entities, access dates and image hashes](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b021f850d4fbe212a107) · [Scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-896f3c889fb725b71280) · [Findings/locators](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1f2962020059e389a0e5).

The [official part02PDF](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file) has78pages/5,561,167bytes, SHA-256 `055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51`, acquired2026-10-31T22:04:24.920527UTC. Official hosting identifies the released representation, not native notes, all source facts or a complete evidence chain.

## 1. What is and is not a collection outcome

| Chain | Exact held evidence | What remains unproved |
|---|---|---|
|Reported pickup |[Mirror52/footer000000740](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=52), paragraph2, reports a female FBI agent picked up a tape early in the day; her card was at an office and an identification message had been left. |Collector identity/callback result, actual pickup date/time, original/copy/item identity, receipt, transfer/examination and disposition. This is a reported pickup, not a signed collection certification. |

NO does not prove no work elsewhere or suppression. |

The pickup's **BA264** and handwritten **IN3221** remain unexpanded leads, not authenticated flight/office/alias identifiers. The source's calm/pre-hijacking belief remains a recipient's interpretation, not a measured call time. No masked person or agent identity is reconstructed.

HQ1492prints **TODD REUBEN**, receipt09/11/2001at7:49AM, header09/16/2001 and handwrittenHQ3060. WF334prints **TODD RUBEN**, receipt7:49PM, header12/15/2001 and handwrittenWF8406. Preserve these roles/values, the different task wording and written-versus-telephone/radio intake descriptions. Neither receipt is authenticated as a message timestamp. Typed/handwritten controls are not established aliases or additional calls. [Prior HQ1492findings](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2f363700f6b7010a7927a4794ba9972f09385dbe/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-hq1492-recording-request-2026-11-02/README.md) · [Prior pickup findings](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/96de8d9745391a966e3967195cda70ff77a764f9/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-pickup-preincident-2026-11-02/README.md).

## 2. An official held response lies within a gap in the mirror's numbering

The mirror goes from **physical56/footer744 to57/footer753**. The already-held official May response carries **745–746**, numerically inside that range. Both sources also display the request tag35-13. This is a concrete cross-source locator—not proof that the PDFs are identical releases, that every missing number is recovered, or that the mirror can be silently completed from another file. We preserve both entities and their source relationships.

The official response reports a completed family meeting and limits its covered/no-further-investigation wording to **LasVegas, at that time, this lead**. It does not refer toHQ1492,BA264,WF334, a recording inventory/item or a collector in the two inspected faces. No source cross-reference demonstrates that it resolves those requested chains. Conversely, it must not be described as an unacquired family response. [Earlier held-response qualification](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e451c84f5494eef39539807b9b984bdb2bcd8eec/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-may-response-qualification-2026-11-02/README.md).

Its6:13a.m.with no stated timezone remains beside the Dallas relay's6:05a.m.Pacific; no conversion or preferred time is adopted. The later May continuation's reference to waking at6AM is **not another measured call timestamp**.

## 3. Later May continuations identify contemporaneous notes

[Mirror71/footer000000770, internal2](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=71), has a continuation header naming NancyMay/RonaldMay and date**06/05/2002**; [72/footer771, internal3](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=72) has the same header/date. These are later report continuations, not the already-held September12,2001EC.

The internal2account says Nancy took notes during the reported call and wrote down airline telephone numbers. **The actual note set is a new specific retrieval target.** The passage does not say those notes were handed to the FBI, entered in a1Aenvelope, copied, retained or destroyed. No item/receipt/custody number is supplied. Do not merge this note set with Woodward's signed notes, Jefferson's Post-its, a tape or the reported pickup card.

That continuation also records a recollection of a call lasting one minute or less and a background male voice believed to be another crew member. These are later attributed recollections, not independently timed or heard speech. The numbers themselves, family biography and unrelated personal details are excluded here. The duration recollection must not be substituted for a billed-duration field or joined to a particular carrier row without evidence. Original notes, interview media, carrier/session/recipient records and applicable timing definitions could strengthen, qualify or contradict it.

The earlier EC's report that Ronald contacted the airline and this later account of Nancy placing calls before Ronald spoke remain in their own source roles. They may describe different portions of a contact chain; no contradiction, common-event reconstruction or preferred account is imposed.

## 4. A separate workplace-call report supplies contrary and unresolved evidence

[Mirror73/footer000000772](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=73) reports an attempted contact by ReneeMay to a redacted workplace recipient. It says the recipient's caller-IDdisplay indicated a call, no one answered, and the reported telephone had **neither an answering machine nor voicemail**. The source gives no call time. This is a report of an **unanswered attempt**, not a recovered saved message, authenticated caller-IDlog or additional conversation. The masked recipient is not identified from other records.

The record is relevant contrary evidence to assuming every apparent additional recipient implies another recorded/in-flight conversation. It does not establish that no other device/copy/answering service existed, resolve any tape request, or refute/validate the family-canvass conclusion about connected unknown calls. Caller-ID/native phone records, the actual canvass's inclusion rules and original contact records remain necessary. Adjacency or a common flight label does not join it toHQ1492/WF334/HQ366/the pickup.

The form visibly prints **Dateoftranscription06/28/2002** and **Investigationon06/10/2001**, while its body refers to09/11/2001. Preserve all three fields. A clerical date entry, a reproduction problem or a misunderstood field/version are untested possibilities. **Do not silently replace2001with2002 or conclude that an interview occurred before the attacks.** An original dated interview record/notes, native creation/transcription history and another authenticated release would discriminate.

## 5. The held tail is incomplete as an interview context

Physical70has footer768;71has770and internal2;72has771and internal3;73has772and a separate first-page form. Our footer-only census accepted70visible production labels on physical4–73. The first3faces are handwritten research notes without those footer labels, checked separately after their clips were blank. **No page bearing769was located in this held copy.** The first page of the later May FD302—candidate footer769—remains a specific missing context target; adjacency does not itself authenticate its production number or content. [Full footer map](CITATIONS.md#nov04-52f046fddeb6f4a9c8bc).

This is a limitation of the particular public reproduction, not evidence of FBI deletion, withholding, unlawful disposal or deliberate omission. A native/released counterpart, original item index and complete version/page accounting could test whether the compiler selected pages, a page was omitted, a first page exists elsewhere or another relationship applies.

The initial expectation that the last three footers would be769–771was **our planning error**. Direct checks confirm the prior pickup/HQ1492/WF334locators; no uniform off-by-one correction is warranted. Those prior reports and source bytes remain unchanged. Our tentative citation concern is not a new historical discrepancy.

## Actual searches, remaining work and verification

Three existing official-source ordinary-text indexes (49/78/21rows) were searched for the exact public controlsHQ1492,HQ3060,BA264,IN3221, allowing embedded spaces. They yielded no matching locator. Two new official-domain queries—HQ1492withrecording andBA264withtape/FBI—returned empty search results; no new source was opened/downloaded. [Exact scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b4b78bc28131127b1f54). These narrow misses do not show nonexistence, no follow-up, destruction, withholding or archive-wide exhaustion. No extra broader query, person/contact search or repeated failed route was attempted.

The immediate targets remain collector/card/callback/contact records; exact lead responses and final/negative/inconclusive disposition; any original/copy media with evidence inventory/FD340/1Aandtransfer/examination records; original written/web intake and routing/history crosswalks; and the missing May firstpage/note set/native contact records. No particular envelope/item/alias relationship is backfilled. [Action/holdings crosswalk](CITATIONS.md#nov04-16bbbedbeec5921d5b00) · [Open TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7e14401447d40c662960).

All five work tracks/eighteenfullcriteria remain open or partial. Existing technical-owner work is not duplicated and no independent audit acceptance is claimed. Original sources, conflicting values and prior findings remain preserved with an actual additive [Git text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8c1de22477886dfb7074) and [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-dc8ba768cbc45207c167). # A reported FBI tape pickup and a separate pre-incident message intake

Original-record plan **TEL01–TEL04** remains open. A newly read narrative says a **tape had been picked up by a female FBI agent** and that a message had been left to identify the agent. This supplies a concrete collector/receipt/custody target beyond the [earlier access offer](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/189851483980fff2946921fbaa0d189d70e8ddae/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf1899-voicemail-access-2026-11-02/README.md) and [recording request](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2f363700f6b7010a7927a4794ba9972f09385dbe/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-hq1492-recording-request-2026-11-02/README.md). **It is a report of pickup, not the tape, a signed receipt or verified acquisition outcome.** None of these records has been joined to the same message or caller.

A separately read intake, **HQ366**, describes a message **“JUST BEFORE THE INCIDENT”**, says there was **“NO INDICATION OF IMPENDING DANGER IN MESSAGE,”** and marks **“Lead Required? NO.”** Its relationship to the pickup narrative is unverified. Preserve both accounts without treating the NO flag as suppression or the pickup report as completed authentication.

## Exact sources and actual reading

[Physical 52, production footer 000000740](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=52), is the new pickup narrative. [Physical 55–56, footers 000000743–744](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=55), are the HQ366 intake/continuation. Three new full-size faces were read on **November 2, 2026**. [Physical 51, footer 000000739](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=51), was reused solely to check the boundary against a previously reviewed office interview; it is not a new witness reading.

The unchanged public mirror has **73 pages**, **2,997,662 bytes**, SHA-256 `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`, acquired **2026-10-27T13:40:38.310907+00:00**. This is a **third-party public reproduction**, not an independently authenticated FBI/native master, complete production or original media chain. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b30829c838072c9b03f8) records exact scopes and image hashes. Raw/full records, personal messages and contact/personnel identifiers remain outside the derivative.

## What the pickup narrative says

[Physical 52, second paragraph](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=52), reports that a tape **“was picked up by a female FBI Agent”** at an office **“early in the day.”** It says the agent's card was at the office and **“A message has been left to find the identity of the Agent.”** The collector is not identified in the visible narrative. No masked party or agent identity is reconstructed.

This is a source's report within a narrative, not a firsthand collection certification or evidence-inventory record. The actual pickup date/time, location/recipient association, agent, original/copy status, tape/device format, evidence identifier, transfers and final result remain unverified. The card, callback/identification record, original contact report, collector's notes and evidence receipt are specific retrieval targets; no contact or new request was made here.

The first paragraph says the contact was in reference to **BA264** on **September 11, 2001**. The page header gives **265D-NY-280350**; a bottom handwritten notation is read as **IN3221**. Those strings remain unexpanded: BA264 is not identified here as an aircraft flight, and IN3221 is not assigned an office or alias function. The contact-date wording and relative pickup phrase do not authenticate an exact pickup chronology.

## A pre-hijacking belief is not a measured call time

[Physical 52, third/fourth paragraphs](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=52), reports a greeting message attributed to someone said to be on **Flight 77**. The recipient believed it was left **before the plane was hijacked because the caller was “calm.”** Possible faint background noise or other useful sounds are described as the reason for contacting the FBI.

Preserve that account and its basis. Calmness alone does not establish hijacking status or time. No audio was acquired/listened to, no acoustic finding was tested, and no caller/aircraft/device/carrier event or clock was authenticated. The underlying message, preserved metadata and carrier/session/clock joins would test it. A genuinely pre-hijacking greeting would have a different evidentiary role from a conversation describing a hijacking; the canvass's actual inclusion rules must be obtained before assuming either belongs to the same call population.

## What HQ366 records—and what NO does not establish

[Physical 55, Event](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=55), reports a Flight 77 message described as **“JUST BEFORE THE INCIDENT”**, possible useful background information, and **“SHE'LL SAVE AND AWAIT CONTACT FROM FBI.”** It also says there was no indication of impending danger in the message. Those are intake descriptions, not authenticated message content, chronology or a laboratory finding.

| HQ366 field | Printed value / qualified reading |
| --- | --- |
| Header date, p55 | **09/12/2001** |
| Information Received, p55 | **09/11/2001, 7:50 PM** |
| Event Date / Time, p55 | **09/11/2001 / time blank** |
| Typed control, p55 | **HQ366** |
| Case, p55 | **265D-HQ-1348101 (PENDING)** |
| Handwritten bottom notation, p55 | Read as **Ser342** |
| Lead Required?, p56 | **NO** |

The receipt field is not a time the message was left. The handwritten notation has no verified author/date/function or alias relationship. PENDING is historical source text, not current case status.

[Physical 56](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=56) marks **NO** but supplies no reasoned disposition or actual collection/testing result. It does not establish that no handling occurred elsewhere, that the narrative pickup concerned this intake, or that the FBI declined an existing request for analysis. Ordinary triage, other handling, inaccurate records and an uncompleted investigation remain untested possibilities; none is selected. Original intake-review rules, cross-references and actual contact/collection/disposition records would discriminate.

The two new units describe potentially similar messages, but masked identities and absent native cross-references prevent joining them. Neither apparent similarity nor differing work-status wording establishes a contradiction about one proven event. Do not count them, their control labels or earlier HQ1492/WF334/WF1899 forms as independent corroboration or additional confirmed calls.

## Boundary correction and remaining work

An earlier navigation cue tentatively called physical 52 a continuation. It has its own printed **1**, different narrative format/header and different subject context from physical 51's previously read office FD302. **No continuation or subject linkage is adopted.** Adjacency in a released compilation is insufficient; the native packet index is still needed. Our tentative cue is preserved as a processing limitation, not a conflicting historical witness statement.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c884c870c2a278f24f9e) retains the tape/card/collector/receipt/media/custody/analysis targets, original HQ366/source/review/disposition records, and all five tracks and 18 original requirements. Original message identity, relevance, timing and final outcome remain untested; inaccuracies, misidentification, related/unrelated messages and actual successful or unsuccessful handling remain possible. No fabricated call, deliberate noncollection, concealment, motive or historical cause is established.

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8da86c1858d78d61305f) has four related analyst entries about two new source units. [verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ea1bc0f91de3a620ec02) checks declared source/image/derivative identities, manually entered locators, predecessor preservation and retained TODOs; it does not independently transcribe or authenticate the reported pickup, message, caller, clock or custody. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-41501909e3d5ab3fe4be) states the actual modes run. The [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-02f19dcd1278c25f824d) and actual additive [text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-6a02efba91e3cdf498a4) preserve prior versions. No original criterion or whole-goal completion follows from source reading or publication.
