# Sweeney telephone reports: preserve service descriptions, separate clocks and missing recording custody

November 2, 2026, America/New_York; reading continued November 3 UTC. **TEL01–TEL04 / AUD016-A1, TC05-B105 and TC06-B110.** Two newly read bodies in the held official FBI release describe Brian Sweeney contacts differently from the already held May 13, 2004 Commission briefing. This review source-pins those accounts and their limits. It acquires no new carrier export or original recording and does not authenticate caller identity, aircraft, service, clock or historical cause.

## What the records actually say

The [official FBI part02, physical 61/footer 000000603](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=61) reports a live conversation with Sweeney's mother. The next [physical 62/footer 000000604](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=62) concerns a message on his wife's answering machine. These are different reported contacts, not duplicate descriptions of one established call. Recipient names appear unmasked in these public records, but personal biographies, addresses, telephone numbers and message content are unnecessary here and excluded from this derivative.

| Source / exact locator | Service description | Time and its stated basis | Qualification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FBI physical 61/footer603, first substantive event paragraph | Phone aboard a plane, **“possibly from his cell phone”** | **8:58 a.m.**, read on the recipient's **kitchen-stove clock** at the call | The report also says he **may have been** on United Flight175. It is not a carrier record or clock-calibration certificate. |
| FBI physical 62/footer604, final substantive paragraph | Message described as **“made from his cell phone aboard the plane”** | Message left at **approximately 8:58 a.m.**, as recorded by the **answering machine** | This is the interview report's device/service description; no underlying handset, carrier return or original message was examined here. |
| [Official May13 briefing, physical 3, GTE Airfone table, third data row](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00216.pdf#page=3) | **GTE Airfone Calls**, seat-bank **31 AB**, Brian Sweeney, wife recipient | **8:58:45**, length **27 sec**, under **all Times Eastern AM** | A Commission memorandum of the DOJ briefing and reconstruction, not a native event-to-recipient join. |
| Same briefing, physical 3, fourth data row | **GTE Airfone Calls**, **31 AB**, Brian Sweeney, parents recipient | **9:00:02**, length **60 sec**, same Eastern label | The row is attributed to the parents, but the worksheet linking that row to the particular earlier interview is not held. |

The [briefing's first page](https://catalog.archives.gov/medialz/legislative/rg-148/2524787/t-0148-911MFR-00216.pdf#page=1) says all Flight175 calls were made through Airfone. It attributes the reconstruction to GTE records and engineers, passenger cellular-record examination, and recipient/family interviews. Its second page says DOJ added caller names to other spreadsheet versions using recipient interviews. Those statements provide a reported methodology and a competing service classification. They do not make the event-to-person assignments independent of the interviews or verify the underlying implementation.

**Preserve the discrepancy:** the early reports use qualified or affirmative cell-phone language; the later briefing assigns these contacts to Airfone. The live-call report's kitchen-clock8:58 remains beside the later parents-row9:00:02; the answering-machine approximately8:58 remains beside the wife-row8:58:45. Do not silently correct either interview with the later chart, join contacts on a surname alone, treat the two early8:58 strings as one calibrated instant, or adopt a preferred service or time. These are source-described contacts and candidate associations, not verified common-event identities. No numerical clock offset or impossible-overlap result is calculated.

The live-call report also attributes to the caller a belief that the plane was somewhere over Ohio and says the recipient saw the second impact on television after their call. Those are reported statements, not independently timed aircraft-position or live-broadcast evidence. They are retained as contextual qualifications, not converted into a different flight identity, foreknowledge or an authenticated clock. The source's uncertain Flight175 association stays uncertain.

## The recording lead is specific; the custody evidence is still missing

Physical62 describes an answering-machine message. It does **not** supply an actual audio file, tape, evidence-item identifier, FBI receipt, copy/examination log or retention disposition in the paragraph inspected. Seek those records, including a copy with its source device/settings and any time-check documentation. A recorded-message assertion is not proof of FBI acquisition or present survival. The separate photograph handoff described on physical61 is **not** an audio handoff; its private image and biography are excluded.

The pages display case **265A-NY-280350**, **REQ. #35-13**, footers **000000603 / 000000604**, and distinct upper date strings **9/28/01 / 10/2/01**, each above lower **9/11/01**. The date roles are not labeled on the inspected faces; this review does not declare them transcription/interview dates or a historical date contradiction. Bottom handwritten302-like controls were inspected in two detail crops but are not adopted as reliable exact identifiers or assigned an evidence/serial role. Use the official PDF, footer and visible case/request references rather than a guessed handwritten locator.

## Additional recording references: useful provenance, repeated substance

The same bounded reading covered [physical38–40/footer444–446](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=38), a September12 Gonzalez report, and [41–43/footer447–449](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-02-of-08/at_download/file#page=41), a September12 Minter report. The Gonzalez report prints **DL267; CE66; AND CE233**; the Minter report prints **DL267 AND CE66**. Shared printed references are retrieval leads, not proof that two reports refer to an identical tape or independently corroborate its contents.

Gonzalez's physical39/internal2 describes the emergency-button **Rockwell telephone recording device**, a recording capacity of **up to four minutes**, overall contact of **approximately23–25 minutes**, provision of the recorded portion to the FBI, and a separate unrecorded portion. This is a report of provision, not an actual intake receipt, original recording or completed examination. Minter's physical43/internal3 qualifies whether some details were heard directly or learned later; she did not hear the end and estimates being on the call over20minutes. Preserve those limits when attributing words or using an interview to validate a transcript.

These repeat previously reviewed Ong recording/interview substance and **are not new independent corroboration**. The approximately7:59 initial-recipient and approximately8:20 supervisor descriptions also overlap the already published interview comparison. They are not newly measured start times. Physical27/footer401 overlaps the already read retrospective playback account. No new playback, audio comparison, flight-clock reconstruction or native-media authentication was performed. Different callers sharing the surname Sweeney, including the separately reviewed AA11 attendant account, are not merged with the Brian Sweeney reports here.

## What could distinguish the explanations

An interview/device-label error, a later compilation/recipient-row error, approximate or differently set clocks, a different event/leg association, or an additional contact are **untested possibilities**, not adopted explanations. Repeated names, matching minute labels and an official summary do not decide among them. The strongest present limitation is that these records are recollections and a later investigative reconstruction; the needed underlying joins and time bases are missing. A genuine contrary carrier return, authenticated receipt or corrected source could change the assessment.

Keep these as TODOs without stopping other available work:

1. Agency-held originals and attachments corresponding to footers603/604; interview notes, source-date fields, amendments and document-control crosswalk.
2. Answering-machine recording, source/copy/extraction details, FBI intake/evidence/custody/analysis records, and retained derivatives or disposition.
3. Native Airfone/cellular returns and the exact worksheets matching the two Sweeney-attributed rows to the contacts; retain failed, negative and unassigned records too. Request the basis for the cell-phone descriptions and the later Airfone classification, without presuming either true.
4. Device settings and contemporaneous checks of the kitchen clock, answering-machine time and carrier timestamps. An uncalibrated household clock is not an aircraft clock.
5. DL267/CE66/CE233 report-to-media crosswalk, Rockwell export/configuration and actual receipt/copy/examination/retention records. Do not transfer a code from one report to another because some codes overlap.

No new request, contact, fee or filing occurred. The court's selected UA93 asset comparison is complete and was not rerun; denied routes and the failed automated-transcription method were not retried. FAA signed disposition/correction, telephone native joins, steel decisions/attribution, and the recorder/materials work assigned to the technical owner remain open under all five tracks/eighteen substantive criteria. Publication does not complete those criteria or establish wrongdoing.

## Scope and preservation

The official FBI PDF remains **5,561,167 bytes /78 pages**, SHA-256 **055a7ed1294c42b976cc98a65dd0a8a80d677723fa4300f0fea38fb41590bb51**, acquired October31,2026 UTC. The May13 UA175 PDF remains **515,215 bytes /3 pages**, SHA-256 **e581efca33a84473d2f08f577f328123759b176d2513c47adc1ba958bd9c980c**, acquired October29 UTC. Official delivery establishes the provenance of these released entities, not the historical truth of every assertion. Exact access times, URLs, page/paragraph/table locators and source forms are in [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-42574d849f2b15b75fbd) and [LOCATORS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f0c2c27cd211f6c3931e).

**Ten unique FBI faces** were inspected:27,38–43,61–63; two additional crops show only the controls on61/62. This is not full78-page reading. The independent allegation following the Gonzalez continuation on physical40 and the unrelated biography/report beginning on63 are excluded. No concealed/redacted text or masked identity was reconstructed. The already read May13 memorandum was reused; only its page3 table was re-inspected in this unit. All originals, earlier candidate readings and prior reports remain unchanged.

One local procedure error is preserved separately: the initial renderer was launched before the two media-path checks had returned terminal status. Both checks subsequently returned verified Intel paths and the producer completed before image adoption; source and image hashes were checked. This is an execution-order defect in this review, not a historical evidence anomaly. The later context render was awaited before reading. No claim of flawless preflight ordering is made.

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2154fc83a34eb0ed5a35), [READING-SCOPE.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-53e52e286e47f76e6ebf), [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3a070af3535939445b89), [OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d19e2c4dc78f84431f81) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e90c04776f37f30e8060) distinguish accepted source readings, unaccepted handwriting, reused evidence, preservation checks and missing native inputs. The exact additive [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0cac18d5a9f1a3e6f7f2) is against the immediately preceding preserved combined review, identified by its hash. Text comparison, PDF/source reading, input-hash checks and remote Git verification are separate operations. No historical call test or physical/independent replication is claimed.

# Ong-labelled records: a documented CD-to-tape conversion and unresolved transcript differences

November 2, 2026. TEL02/TEL04; AUD016-A1 / TC05-B105 / TC06-B110. The previously unexamined continuations identify recording-generation and engineering-record targets. They do not establish altered audio, an impossible call or a completed source-authentication test.

## What was acquired and read

The [official FBI September release, part 04](https://vault.fbi.gov/9-11%20Commission%20Report/9-11-interviews-2001-09-sep-04-of-08) was retrieved as an 18-page PDF: **601,286 bytes**, SHA-256 **854bf3408241640a4b5549fba79b7a1f8a9a2639267488b9db3132bd08ac3caf**. The [previous study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/abefdac15a5474b2cba2407dc7d0e6bcd58ead59/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-playback-custody-2026-10-31/README.md) had viewed selected browser images, without acquiring this binary or reading pages 3–16.

This unit visually read pages **1–17**: fourteen previously unread pages, plus three context pages. Page 18's separate playback record was not reread. This is a released reproduction, not native audio or a complete FBI production. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f3dcdc3c4fdeabd28cfe) records the actual acquisition and reading scope; [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1da28d74a35bec1c2e41) supplies physical/internal-page, footer and passage locators.

## Two source chains must stay distinct

**First record, physical 1–9, DL-336:** its cover has transcription date **09/12/2001**, but investigation/dictation fields **09/11/2001**. Page 2 opens a retrospective session at **12:28 p.m. Central Time**, asks about the morning and requests playback. Page 3 introduces the playback. After a recording-concluded marker on page 7, questions and recollection continue through page 8. These are different source roles and dates, not a reason to classify the whole report as contemporaneous airborne dialogue. Page 9 has a continuation header/footer with a visually empty body; no cause or missing-content finding follows.

**Second record, physical 10–14, lead controls DL267/CE66:** page 10 explicitly describes material obtained from American Airlines on September 11 and copied **from CD-ROM onto analog audio tape for transcription**. Its transcription/investigation/dictation fields show **09/12/2001**. This supplies a concrete conversion and custody target. It does not supply that CD or tape, file identity, conversion settings, duplication log or fidelity test. The different date fields are retained in their respective roles; neither date authenticates the call clock.

## What the first continuation adds

At physical **7 / internal 7 / footer 000000406**, after playback ends, a question describes another **five or ten minutes** of conversation and receives an affirmative reply. A speaker then reports that communications staff were checking why recording stopped despite the emergency button remaining on. This is an attributed account of an inquiry, not a completed technical explanation or independently measured duration.

The earlier [Charlotte EC review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/d5b279a04f2fe18b78548222aea75451bfa7f60a/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-sero-evidence-inventory-2026-10-31/README.md) preserves a later default-timer explanation, four-minute capture and approximately 25-minute call. Keep those accounts alongside the new passage. They could describe a developing inquiry, but that relationship has not been verified. Do not add the durations, select a controlling total or infer deliberate truncation. The actual engineering findings, effective configuration, audit history and source recordings remain missing.

At physical **8 / footer 000000407**, an interviewer assumes a cellular phone; the reply says the speaker did not know and had not determined it. The question is not evidence of service type. The final exchange also requests **two statements by fax** and receives a promise. The statements, fax transmission and recipient receipt remain concrete original-record targets; a promise is not delivery.

## Selected conflicting transcript readings

| Potentially corresponding passage | DL-336 record | DL267/CE66 transcription |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Injury description after the number-one reference | Physical 5 / footer 000000404: **our passenger is stabbed** | Physical 12 / footer 000000459: **our purser has been stabbed** |

These are the printed words, not independently heard speech. The reviewed records contain other dialogue and attribution differences; this is **not an exhaustive textual collation** or a sample-aligned audio comparison. Neither wording is selected or silently corrected. Copy generation, source coverage and transcription choices are untested alternatives to an underlying content change. Source media and segment mappings would distinguish them.

The second transcript also prints **Flight 12** near its opening and later **Flight 11** responses (physical 11–13). Keep the surrounding dialogue; an initial statement or speaker label is not independent aircraft identification. Physical 15–17 begin a separate report and are not an extension of this transcript.

## Evidentiary limits and next work

The useful advance is an item-level map to the **CD, analog rerecording, recorded interview/playback session, engineering check and promised statements**. It narrows TEL02's retrieval chain. The strongest competing interpretation is ordinary copying/transcription and an evolving technical inquiry. Neither that account nor fabrication is established by these paper records. Related transcripts and an inventory must not be counted as independent authentication of the call.

No listening, voice identification, native waveform comparison, carrier join, calibrated clock test or full cross-version collation was performed. The previously tested court audio representations remain preserved with their own limits; no master-to-court join is newly demonstrated. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-842a21b2154234d4b648) retain those originals and all five tracks/eighteen substantive requirements. None is newly accepted by acquiring and reading a scan.

Prior reports and conflicting records remain unchanged. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3ef442a616e1911e6b43), [source checks](CITATIONS.md#nov04-01ab95c73c58aa7216f1) and [addition diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f040ecd4b389b00250d4) accompany this redundant public note. Raw scans/images/text/audio, contacts, signatures, masked identities, private case material and correspondence are excluded. No outreach, new request, fee, filing, mailbox access, deletion, overwrite, private push or history rewrite occurred.

# Transfer, relay and declared audio editing: source records kept separate

November 2, 2026. TEL02/TEL04; AUD016-A1 / TC05-B105 / TC06-B110. Two source descriptions differ on how Ong's information reached airline operations. The hearing also explicitly announces recording edits and excerpt selection. These are documentary leads; the actual telephone routing and master-to-exhibit chain remain unverified.

## Transfer versus relay

The reproduced **Craig Marquis FD-302**, physical **4 / internal 1 / footer 000000134**, describes the call as **transferred to central dispatch**. Its transcription/dictation fields show **09/16/2001**, while the investigation field shows **09/11/2001**. These fields have different roles. [Released-file mirror](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=4).

In the **January 27, 2004 hearing**, printed/physical **106–107**, **Chair Thomas Kean's introduction** instead describes an inability to transfer the call or patch operations personnel into it, with Gonzalez relaying information on another line. That passage is the chair's introductory account, **not Gonzalez's own firsthand testimony**. Her remarks begin later on 107. [Archived Commission transcript](https://govinfo.library.unt.edu/911/archive/hearing7/9-11Commission_Hearing_2004-01-27.pdf#page=106).

Both descriptions remain unchanged. They do not establish the same telephone leg, phase or use of transfer terminology. Shorthand reporting, different phases, a misunderstanding or an actual routing/account difference are untested possibilities. No source is selected as controlling. The switch/bridge/conference logs, operator records, handset setup, original interview notes and matched recordings would distinguish the alternatives. Neither paper account alone establishes that Marquis directly heard Ong's voice.

## The hearing discloses editing and selection

**Kean's remarks on page 109**, before the calls are played, say:

| Recording described by the chair | Announced scope |
| --- | --- |
| Ong to Gonzalez | Entire **four and a half minutes** recorded, as described by the chair. |
| Gonzalez to operations | Approximately **20 minutes** in duration; **four minutes selected** for the hearing because of time constraints. |

In a separate paragraph, the chair announces a possible momentary blank and a small edit to protect a family member. That paragraph does not supply a specific file, call-leg or edited-interval mapping. This is affirmative evidence that editing/selection was disclosed. It is a source-reported purpose and scope, not a recovered edit decision list, proof of what was removed, authorization record, original-file comparison or complete audit. It does not establish that the same edits occur in the later court Flash files or explain their previously measured unmatched tail. No audio was listened to or compared here. [Page 109](https://govinfo.library.unt.edu/911/archive/hearing7/9-11Commission_Hearing_2004-01-27.pdf#page=109).

Keep **four and a half** beside the earlier FBI four-minute descriptions; keep **20** beside Gonzalez's own **approximately 23-minute** conversation account on page 108 and the separate earlier approximately 25-minute account. They concern differently described calls, recordings or estimates. Do not combine them into missing minutes or adopt one duration. A disclosed edit could explain a specific feature only after the actual source/export/segment relationship is established.

The hearing's page 110 also uses **purser** in the injury passage. That is another documentary representation alongside the [earlier passenger/purser comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/17aef02c7d79169234dc04c4a67f38425900ea30/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-transcript-generation-chain-2026-11-02/README.md). It does not authenticate the acoustic word or independently corroborate a shared source.

## Specific originals remain to retrieve

Marquis's report, **mirror 5 / internal 2 / footer 000000135**, says he was unsure but thought his conversation recorded and would check and notify the FBI. That is a proposed verification, not a recovered recording or completed notification. **Mirror 6 / internal 3 / footer 000000136** says he supplied a **copy of his notes and an annotated crew manifest**, referring to an **FD-340 envelope**. The passage does not identify its envelope number. Those actual items and their custody/receipt remain missing.

The report's approximate **7:25–7:49 a.m.** labels and first-page **Central Standard Time** remain as printed. Its inference that impact was near 7:49 is based on another reported call, not a calibrated event measurement. No zone correction, clock fit or independently measured duration is adopted.

## Handwriting is not enough to authenticate contemporaneous notes

The compilation's first three handwritten pages refer to other reports, ask retrieval questions and give editing/addition instructions. They have no author/date authenticated in this review. Page 1 points to original typed Cary statements in a **1A file**, with **265D NY 280350 CE / Serial 594**; that is a reference, not the statements themselves. Page 3 points to a report explaining the four-minute limit. These pages are consistent with research/retrieval notes. They are **not established as the contemporaneous call notes** described by Marquis, Woodward or Hanson. Adjacency does not make that join. Unrelated personal assertions are excluded.

## Actual scope, contrary evidence and limits

The held 73-page mirror was not downloaded again. Five new full pages **1–5** were read; previously read page **6** was reexamined as context. One new archived hearing PDF was acquired, **338,805 bytes / 144 pages**, SHA-256 **705c92312057c1efcfcc272f7480ead285b1dfdb055b37a1cfd937f5b2f84e10**. Only its **106–110** images were read. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8108ed1458ab9213b13a) and [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-70de23a18646fae214cb) separate source layers, page roles and observed claims.

Ordinary copying, imperfect recollection/terminology and expressly announced presentation editing remain competing possibilities. These records establish no fabrication, concealment, unlawful editing, calibrated delay or historical cause. Conversely, the stated editing purpose does not authenticate every derivative or erase differences. The originals could strengthen or weaken those interpretations.

No native call media, original notes, carrier/office bridge records, editing project, independent voice or clock test was acquired/performed. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ecd52d7e941334afe1b2) retain those inputs and every original five-track/eighteen requirement. Prior records and conflicting values remain unchanged. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-64596e684d4b464300cd), [source verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-075f57d642f971251824) and [addition diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8f3e90b312ddda782026) distinguish acquisition, reading, inference and publication. Raw scans/images/text/audio, signatures, contacts, masked identities, private case material and correspondence are excluded. <a id="supplement-c3a55b65083d40b9"></a>
### Two UA93 court assets: equal decoded text, different source bytes

**Source unit:** `telephone-ua93-court-variants-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3ebbc9cc17670e7a62b9).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY c3a55b65083d40b90aec07506d3fd4a96671c25c04db1f89d2e8b86d42ad6b35 -->
# Two UA93 court assets: equal decoded text, different source bytes

November 2, 2026, America/New_York; execution receipts extend into November 3 UTC. TEL02/TEL04, AUD016-A1, TC05-B105 and TC06-B110. This is a source-version and recording-locator test using an already-held public court archive. It does not recover or authenticate a native telephone recording.

## Why this pair was inspected

The [preceding FBI review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/5c2a489db46eb230900d41b05f1710497e636965/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-recording-custody-relays-2026-11-02/README.md) identifies a reported Gronlund-associated cassette receipt and September 11 handover at the Boston FBI office. Its actual tape/item/receipt/examination/retention chain remains missing. The already-held court prosecution index supplied no literal Gronlund row match; that does not establish what is in the separately distributed presentation archive.

The [court ZIP](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/flights.zip), linked from the [P200054 exhibit page](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/prosecution/flights/P200054.html), contains fourteen members. None is Gronlund-named. It contains the following **two different UA93 members**. They occur in the same held archive; no earlier/later chronology is inferred from their names or order.

| Archive member | Original bytes | SHA-256 | Decoded text definitions |
|---|---:|---|---:|
| UA93.swf | 3,464,811 | f104ab54492e05550c9e776894e0dbb0d9238039a8f2e9c40be41b1a122d7675 | 420 |
| UA 93 Calls.swf | 3,464,520 | 9b6703c286cc4e5be8de36728c10bc0fc60dc54e4b732898ba4d4fbfda2fec5e | 420 |

## Actual test result

The two sources differ in bytes and by **291 bytes of file length**. The existing passive reader recovered **420 text definitions / 379 distinct decoded strings** from each. The **exact multiset of decoded codepoint strings matches**, including every repeated occurrence, NUL and other codepoint. No whitespace, case or source-value normalization was used to obtain that result. The shared multiset digest is `9a4da7dc704250b0cb7d2649a8b47586653bdbe16bfce3ce3a50eb9c1419dede`.

This establishes equality only in that decoded representation. It does **not** establish equal font shapes, geometry, color, layering, buttons, actions, visited screens, execution order, actual trial display or historical accuracy. The definition IDs and offsets differ. A field location valid in one member cannot be applied blindly to the other. The exact source bytes remain separate and unchanged; no preferred version or cause of the difference is selected.

Both have SWF-version-5 headers, a declared 12 frames per second, 176 declared root frames and the same stage rectangle. Those are authoring metadata, not call duration, a carrier clock or proof of runtime behavior. Their complete numeric tag-count dictionaries differ: tag code **28** occurs **1,216** times in UA93.swf and **1,190** times in UA 93 Calls.swf. This test does not interpret those instructions or simulate their effect. Equal counts for other tag types would not establish equal tag bodies.

### Gronlund is a stored term, not an authenticated tape

The decoded definitions contain Gronlund, Glick and Beamer terms in both members. For example, a Gronlund-matching definition appears at **UA93.swf: character ID 679, uncompressed tag offset 282713**, and its raw decoded-string hash matches **UA 93 Calls.swf: ID 1754, offset 3415889**. Other Gronlund-associated definitions occupy different IDs/offsets too. [RESULTS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b5c98e1f569c5a3a15eb) preserves selected safe term coordinates and source hashes; it excludes the full text table, recipient/contact/phone fields and compound text contents.

An exact term hit is not independent voice/caller identification, proof that a particular text was displayed, or a unique join between the court data and the Boston cassette. These objects may contain a court summary of information derived from several sources; the source-to-exhibit construction/attribution records remain unacquired. Multiple definitions, duplicate labels or two source variants are not multiple calls or independent corroboration.

### No standard SWF sound tags in these two members

The complete recursive tag traversal found **zero** occurrences of the selected sound-definition/playback/stream tag codes **14, 15, 17, 18, 19, 45 and 89** in either member. Neither inspected object therefore supplies an identified Gronlund recording through those standard sound structures. No sound payload was extracted, decoded or listened to.

This is not a claim that the full archive, other court holdings or the FBI lack the recording. Other members were inventoried but not decoded in this unit. External media, unused/opaque data, script references and runtime behavior were not evaluated. The objects contain actions, which were counted but neither interpreted nor executed. The cassette's historical existence or fate cannot be inferred from this bounded result.

## What actually ran

The unchanged court ZIP was verified at **28,304,524 bytes**, SHA-256 `3a1e08b2276dcf0f1bdf5a7071113314776aca4660d73d0cf42fd91f92296801`. Only **UA93.swf** and **UA 93 Calls.swf** were read and preserved as separately named local derivatives; no executable launcher or Flash/ActionScript ran. Both full passive extractions and tag inventories remain local on the verified Intel store. No new download, external query, OCR, ASR, audio/voice/caller/clock inference or native carrier decoding occurred.

The initial source lookup used a guessed flights.zip filename and failed; the preserved acquisition script identifies the actual court-flights.zip path, which passed the media guard and byte checks. That was our path error, not missing historical evidence. A prior protocol had expressly left the Calls variant uninspected; the bounded reading-history check found no completed comparison of this pair. Completed Lyles/Ong/AA77 tests were not repeated as new findings.

The equality calculation treats raw decoded strings as a multiset, not a set. Fixed comparator controls require a reordered identical input to match and reject changed multiplicity, case changes and removal of a NUL. These controls test the comparison rule; they are not an independent SWF implementation or historical authentication. The same existing font/tag parser underlies both extraction and reproduction, so parser dependence remains a limitation. The court's original authoring project and a verified actual runtime/display are not available in this test.

## Next records, preservation and scope

The most useful inputs remain the actual Boston cassette/evidence receipt and source-to-court-recording construction/copy records. For presentation versions, obtain the authoring projects, exact build/delivery/version manifest, source-to-field mapping and any external media references with their source/custody. The present equality result cannot decide why the bytes, IDs or tag counts differ or whether those differences affect a particular visited screen.

All earlier FBI handling times, source-specific 9:03/9:11 status reports, 9:36/10:45 endpoint accounts, provider/recording assertions and earlier telephone conflicts remain unchanged. No historical discrepancy is resolved, no clock/leg/caller is selected and no inference of fabrication, concealment, motive or attack mechanism follows. A different build/export or assembly is a possible untested explanation, not an adopted one.

All five original work-plan tracks and eighteen full requirements remain open or partial. Existing Keyton/usage/court sources and completed technical-owner work remain recognized. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-09526c73e87ca9f44faa), [source pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-85f020bead4bdd8ca6b5), [method](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5a8f7153227c76070674), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-169ef0f6d15dfa802358), [verification](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d78066c33f56117bfbfd) and the [actual additive diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-424b20a1568cddf47da5) distinguish the performed source test from missing originals and unperformed empirical tests. Raw media, full text, contacts, recipient/personnel/signature information, masked identities, private case/correspondence and internal coordination are excluded from the public derivative. No outreach/request/fee/mailbox, deletion, overwrite, private push, merge or history rewrite occurred.

### WF1899: a voicemail-access lead with unlocated collection and message identities

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 8f2176657a63cef6b70b692883cbe88d94419db3ccf59601ae177ecf887c43c3 -->
# WF1899: a voicemail-access lead with unlocated collection and message identities

A newly read FBI-form record, **WF1899**, reports that **Ted Olson had new voicemail at his former law firm** and that his former secretary would provide access to the FBI. Its follow-up instructs contact with the firm's office administrator **“FOR ACCESS TO MAIL OF OLSON.”** This supplies a concrete office-message/access/disposition target for the telephone investigation.

**The record does not identify who left the messages or say they were Barbara Olson's calls from Flight 77. It does not document completed access, collected audio or a final result.** The difference between **VOICE MAIL** in the intake and **MAIL** in the instruction remains preserved. No message medium, sender, caller, flight or carrier-row identity is selected from context alone.

## Exact source and reading

[Public reproduction, physical 63–65](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=63), production footers **000000761–763**. All three full-size page faces were newly read on **November 2, 2026**, after a bounded reuse check. The earlier contact-sheet view was navigation, not a full-page reading.

The unchanged source has **73 pages**, **2,997,662 bytes**, SHA-256 `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`, acquired **2026-10-27T13:40:38.310907+00:00**. It is a **third-party public reproduction of FBI-form records**, not an independently authenticated FBI master/native intake database or complete production. No new source download or public search occurred. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b51bc4e656291758be75) pins the page-image identities and actual scope; raw scans/images and private-office contact details are excluded from publication.

## What the intake and lead establish

[Physical 63, Event, footer 000000761](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=63), reports a September11 interview of Ted Olson, identifies Barbara Olson as his wife and a Flight 77 passenger, then says that on **09/13/01** he advised of **“NEW MESSAGES ON HIS VOICE MAIL AT HIS OLD LAW FIRM”** and that his former secretary **“WOULD PROVIDE ACCESS TO THESE CALLS TO THE FBI.”**

The paragraph's wife/flight context does not identify the senders or contents of those new messages. “New” is not a message timestamp or proof of when a call occurred. The future access statement is not actual delivery, an evidence receipt, a recording/transcript, speaker authentication or a completed investigative finding. The underlying source conversation and message records remain necessary.

[Physical 64, footer 000000762](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=64), marks **Lead Required? YES**. [Physical 65, Set Lead 1, footer 000000763](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=65), records **WF1899**, **WFO / Washington, DC**, and a direction to contact the **office administrator at Gibson Dunn & Crutcher** **“FOR ACCESS TO MAIL OF OLSON.”** The private contact name and masked assignee are not reproduced or reconstructed.

The instruction says **MAIL**; the intake says **VOICE MAIL**. It does not explicitly say to copy audio, export a voicemail database, collect email or seize physical mail. Possible shorthand, different requested material and an incomplete source description remain untested possibilities; none is chosen. The original task context, attachments and actual response would clarify the intended medium. The source's former-secretary and office-administrator roles are also not silently equated to one person.

This is a **contact/access directive**, distinct from [HQ1492's explicit request to obtain a recording](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2f363700f6b7010a7927a4794ba9972f09385dbe/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-hq1492-recording-request-2026-11-02/README.md). That comparison concerns wording and work stages. It does not join these records to one message, person, recipient or carrier event. Neither an offered access route nor two lead controls constitute independent confirmation of a call or evidence of failed follow-up.

## Preserve dates, clocks and identifiers

| Location / field | Literal value or qualified reading | Limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Physical 63, header Date | **10/16/2001** | Document-header role; creation/export history unverified. |
| Physical 63, Information Received | **09/13/2001, 10:30 AM** | Intake field, not authenticated message-left time. |
| Physical 63, Event Date / Time | **09/13/2001 / 10:30 AM** | Source event field; specific clock/field semantics unverified. |
| Physical 65, Assignment | **09/13/2001 at 2:15 AM** | Printed assignment label, not proof of completed contact. |
| Physical 63/65, typed control | **WF1899** | Identifier, not authentication of contents/outcome. |
| Physical 63, handwritten bottom notation | Read as **WF3089** | Author/date/function and any alias relationship unknown. |
| Physical 63, case | **265A-NY-280350-WF (PENDING)** | Source text; not verified present status. |

The **2:15 AM** assignment label is earlier on the printed date than the **10:30 AM** receipt/event labels. Preserve them without changing AM to PM, assuming a common clock, calculating a delay or selecting an explanation. Date/time differences could involve distinct field meanings, source entry/import history or other causes; those remain untested. None establishes when the reported messages were actually left. Native creation/edit/routing/assignment history, field/clock definitions and original submissions are the discriminating records.

Physical 63 also labels **Source: OLSON, TED**, **Method of Contact: Telephone/Radio**, **To: WFO**, **From: WFO**. These identify record context; they are not the original interview, a carrier record or an executed access receipt. Typed and handwritten identifiers are preserved without treating them as aliases, replacement controls, separate investigations or separate calls.

## What remains to be tested

Priority targets are the original source/contact report, native WF1899 and cross-reference/routing history; the actual office-contact interview/lead response and completion/disposition; any consent, access or collection documentation; original messages and metadata, export/copy/receipt/inventory/custody records; transcripts/examinations and final findings, including negative and inconclusive results. Relevance to a particular flight call requires independently supported message/caller/carrier/session/canvass joins.

These could show relevant passenger-related messages, unrelated messages, an inaccurate or incomplete intake, unavailable material, or a successful or unsuccessful collection. They are untested alternatives. This unit cannot establish a recovered Barbara Olson recording, a new confirmed flight call, fabricated calls, deliberate noncollection, missing evidence throughout the FBI file or wrongdoing. A recorded access opportunity and no outcome on these three pages are not a case-wide absence finding.

## Work completed and verification limits

Three full source faces were read; no whole 73-page audit, new OCR, audio/ASR, technical-owner experiment, profile search, private-case/mailbox access, outreach, request or fee occurred. [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a7006ab57c3379a03c79) has four related analyst entries from one unit, not four independent confirmations. Private personnel/contact identifiers, raw/full source text and images remain outside the derivative.

[verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1a7093ca8759b5f78a1e) checks declared source/image/derivative bytes, manually entered page/field/value consistency, all 18 TODOs and exact predecessor preservation. It does not independently transcribe pixels, authenticate audio, identify callers/messages, calibrate clocks or prove access/collection/disposition. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0f892c51875915c42ac4) records actual runs. The original PDF and preceding review remain unchanged; [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-e6dfe197c61f2ee65b6e) is the actual additive text diff. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c800b528aec580249d4d) records the request-by-request disposition.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ca1f73f84bb486a3fd57) carries forward the new WF1899 originals and all five tracks/18 original requirements, including previous HQ1492/WF334/serial3255/print/engineering targets. No original-record criterion or whole investigation is completed by this reading or publication. Missing inputs remain TODOs; publication is separate from native authentication and independent audit.

### WF334 name check: a court-presentation candidate, not a verified message

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 6d58b01ca5b5c1a01e7507c3f66f3805d46bc167f42b2e6af62ddc4e9ba38c81 -->
# WF334 name check: a court-presentation candidate, not a verified message

The held court presentation contains **“Reuben, Todd”** and separate **“Reuben” / “Todd”** text definitions. The previously reviewed FBI-form tip **WF334** reports **“TODD RUBEN,”** described there as a passenger on **Flight #77**. Preserve both spellings. This is a specific identity-check lead; **the records have not been joined to the same person, message or call**. It is not yet a demonstrated contradiction about one established individual.

## Exact source trail

The [official court page for prosecution exhibit P200054](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/prosecution/flights/P200054.html) describes a Flight 77 summary of crew identities, passenger seat assignments and telephone calls, and expressly also includes information about the other three hijacked flights. The complete held HTML is **2,117 bytes**, SHA-256 `866887c38f78c1f5b68ab73a695ae5345812eaf53a3e34da240f5345a1cd96c5`. Exact saved-file lines: **25** (prosecution exhibits), **45** (P200054), **51** (description), **73** (ZIP link). These are local HTML line numbers, not transcript pages. The separate HTML receipt lacks an access timestamp; none is invented.

The [court archive](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/flights.zip) was acquired **October 27, 2026, 13:40:38.310051 UTC**, as a 200 response. The preserved ZIP is **28,304,524 bytes**, SHA-256 `3a1e08b2276dcf0f1bdf5a7071113314776aca4660d73d0cf42fd91f92296801`. Its exact **AA77.swf** member is **5,162,821 bytes**, SHA-256 `844dfb545c8414f9e7da1edc89ffe090a09d00d721a2c65c820e15f5fe201a06`. This unit verifies the held bytes and re-decodes the member without executing Flash. No new download or repeated source query occurred. The HTML's HTTP download link and acquired HTTPS URL are recorded separately, without assuming response equivalence.

[The prior WF334 review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6e9ce8fa287fa4410241bc8fba303cefd6476467/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf334-recording-lead-2026-11-02/README.md) pins the other record to [physical 68–70](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=68), production footers **000000766–768**, in a 73-page third-party public reproduction of FBI-form records. The literal name is on **physical 68, Event paragraph, footer 000000766**. PDF SHA-256: `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`. This unit reuses that full-page reading; it does not newly authenticate an FBI master or reread all 73 pages.

## Exact decoded locators

Offsets start at zero at the beginning of the **uncompressed AA77.swf**, including its file header. The member is **FWS**, already uncompressed. IDs are dictionary-character identifiers, not frames, people, calls or page numbers. Body lengths exclude the tag header. Text comes from glyph indices and embedded font mappings, not assumed literal ASCII substrings in the binary.

| Text | Character ID | Tag offset, bytes | Body length, bytes | Font ID |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `Reuben, Todd` | 331 | 61,801 | 49 | 158 |
| `Reuben, Todd` | 930 | 192,906 | 49 | 126 |
| `Reuben` | 1786 | 3,576,769 | 41 | 1012 |
| `Todd` | 1787 | 3,576,816 | 37 | 1014 |
| `American Airlines Flight #77` | 433 | 113,604 | 81 | 126 |

All five are root-level **DefineText**, tag **11**. [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-c5c24608daa2ade6bc8d) also preserves four navigation definitions: IDs **422** (Flight 93), **427** (Flight 11), **429** (Flight 77), **431** (Flight 175), with exact glyph, offset, bounds and font locators. Their presence fits the court page's stated multi-flight scope and cautions against inferring a specific flight/call relationship from the filename alone.

The four name definitions belong to **one presentation**. They are not four independent confirmations or four calls. Independent origin of the court summary and investigative accounts is not established.

## What this can test

The name similarity supplies a candidate for checking WF334's spelling and identification basis. It does **not** establish that the names identify the same person, that this person left the alleged message, that it came from Flight 77, or that it falls within the previously reviewed family-canvass scope. No court seat, native manifest row, recipient, device, carrier event or session is assigned here.

The title and names coexist in the asset; their display-list/action relationship and actual runtime display were **not** tested. Nearby unrelated personal/profile fields were not interpreted. A prosecution summary is not a native airline manifest or a judicial finding authenticating every assertion.

An inaccurate tip, clerical spelling variation, a different person, an earlier or misattributed message, and a genuine relevant message remain untested possibilities. None is selected. The original tip identification basis, contact/disposition, recording/custody, independent passenger/flight record and carrier/canvass join would discriminate between them.

Nothing here adds a verified canvass recipient or refutes the prior four-unknown-call attribution account. It does not establish fabricated calls, concealed evidence or coordinated wrongdoing. The prior header/receipt/event/assignment values, typed **WF334** and handwritten **WF8406** remain preserved in their original record roles.

## Reproduction and limits

The earlier decoder was reused unchanged: SHA-256 `62f254468e5bbc265d3a04a44c735a09da65339c3a13e1f79046b6e503410617`. It passively re-decoded **548 text definitions**, producing exactly the same JSON-serialized result as the preserved extraction. This unit examines and publishes **nine selected definitions**, including four name definitions; it does not substantively review every name, call or screen.

Run `python3 verify.py --court-zip /path/to/court-flights.zip` to verify archive/member bytes and reproduce the selected font/text records. `python3 verify.py` alone checks the published bundle. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0e6674e44ebb30da35b5) records actual runs. Reproduction using the same decoder is not independent method validation, identity authentication, audio analysis, carrier reconciliation or runtime-display verification.

Two initial local comparisons failed: numeric keys in raw Python font/tag dictionaries differ from JSON string keys; the first correction overlooked the tag dictionary. Complete JSON-serialized equality was subsequently tested. These are our comparison errors, preserved locally, **not historical source anomalies**.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3db7b4726453b89f4f74) carries forward all five tracks and 18 original requirements plus the identity/message/court-source targets. Earlier records, assessments and conflicts remain unchanged. [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5fd85ea78d777c1d187e) is the actual additive text diff against the preceding combined review. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-864996f85230cd74627f) separates completed source work from unperformed authentication. Neither this bounded check nor publication completes an original-record criterion.

### WF334: a reported saved-message lead and an unlocated follow-up outcome

<details>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 25a529fdf45379b845e0d5326db14c56b546ffed321150d6696e5d271664c742 -->
# WF334: a reported saved-message lead and an unlocated follow-up outcome

A newly read intake/lead record says a masked recipient had saved an in-flight message attributed in the text to **“TODD RUBEN,”** described as a passenger on **Flight #77**. It assigns contact about the message’s contents under **WF334**. This is a concrete recording/disposition target, **not a recovered or authenticated message**.

The three pages supply no recording, completed contact report, carrier match or disposition. They do not establish another genuine Flight 77 call or a fabricated call. The reported name is preserved literally; no spelling correction, passenger-list match, recipient relationship or masked identity is supplied.

## Source and actual reading

The [public mirror, physical 68–70](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=68), is an already-held reproduction of FBI-form records. The PDF has **73 pages**, **2,997,662 bytes**, SHA-256 **`e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`**; it was acquired October 27, 2026. This unit newly examined all three selected page faces on November 2. Production footers are **000000766–768**. Analyst ordinals do not purport to be printed internal page numbers.

This is a third-party public representation, not an independently authenticated agency master, complete FBI production or evidence-custody chain. [SOURCES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-45897a56e995791f8dfb) records exact access/hash/page scopes. Raw scans, images, full tip text, personal/contact/profile/signature fields and masked identities are excluded from the derivative.

A source-reuse check caught an old suggestion to examine official part 01 pages 47–49: those pages were already reviewed, so that work was not repeated or counted as new. Initial thumbnail control/name associations were also wrong; the full-page reading supplies the adopted values. Our tentative readings are preserved locally as rejected processing notes, **not as alternative historical evidence**.

## What the record reports and requests

[Physical 68, Event paragraph](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=68), alleges an in-flight message and ends with **“SHE HAS MESSAGE SAVED.”** That records the allegation; it does not supply the device, media, content, recording time, caller identity or validation. The recipient/source fields are masked, and no relationship to the named person is inferred.

[Physical 69](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=69) marks **Lead Required? YES**. [Physical 70, Set Lead 1](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=70), records **WF334**, **WFO / Washington, DC**, assignment **09/12/2001 at 7:20 AM**, and a request to contact a masked person **regarding contents of the message on an answering machine**.

That wording does not explicitly order acquisition of the recording. An assignment is distinct from actual contact, collection, authentication and a completed result. No outcome appears within these three pages; this limited scope does not establish that the FBI never followed up or that the record is absent elsewhere.

The immediate underlying targets are the original ICF and source/contact record, lead-response/interview and disposition, any original message/device or preserved copy, evidence inventory/receipt, and any transcription or analytical finding—including negative and inconclusive results. No contact or new request was made here.

## Preserve the date and control fields

| Source field | Printed value | Scope limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Header, physical 68 | **12/15/2001** | Role in the create/edit/export history is unverified. |
| Information Received, physical 68 | **09/11/2001, 7:49 PM** | An intake-receipt field; not an authenticated time the reported message was left. |
| Event Date/Time, physical 69 | **09/11/2001 / time blank** | No message time or calibrated event clock supplied. |
| Assignment, physical 70 | **09/12/2001, 7:20 AM** | Records an assignment label, not a verified completed action. |
| Typed control, physical 68 and 70 | **WF334** | A source identifier, not proof of the lead’s contents or outcome. |
| Handwritten bottom notation, physical 68 | Read as **WF8406** | Writer, date, function and any alias/cross-reference relationship remain unknown. |

Keep these values side by side. No timezone, processing-delay calculation, date correction or conclusion about a post-crash call is derived. The label **PENDING** in the printed case **265A-NY-280350-WF (PENDING)** is not a verified current case status. Native ICF history and routing/assignment/receipt records would distinguish the field roles.

WF334 and the handwritten notation are not counted as two independent leads. The annotation does not authenticate an alias or replacement number. Any retrieval should preserve both appearances and seek their original inventory/cross-reference relationship.

## Why this matters to the call-attribution review

The previously examined [final-report note 57, printed page 455](https://www.9-11commission.gov/report/911Report_Notes.pdf#page=7), reports FBI/DOJ belief that four unknown connected records concerned the Olson office. Its stated context includes family canvassing that identified May’s parents and Ted Olson as recipients. The [earlier telephone review](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/639bd09f9f8116707df2be5fb04d87472afd58cb/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-originals-2026-10-27/README.md) already preserves the uncertain row-to-conversation mapping.

WF334 gives a specific alleged saved-message lead whose verification and disposition can be checked against that account. **It does not by itself refute the canvass or establish an additional call.** It cannot be assigned to one of the unknown rows, to a device or to a caller/session merely from a name and flight label.

If an authenticated message and carrier/session evidence established another relevant Flight 77 recipient, the scope and attribution inference would need reassessment. An inaccurate or unrelated tip, a misattributed or earlier message, an inconclusive follow-up, and a genuine additional message remain untested possibilities. No one is selected as the explanation. The actual message, contact/disposition and full canvass scope/inclusion rules are the discriminating records.

No association to the separately reviewed operator calls is made from a similar first name or message terminology. This record’s literal name and flight description remain its own unverified allegation.

## Work completed and limits

Three full source faces were read; no whole 73-page census, new OCR, hidden extraction, audio/ASR, voice identification or native clock test occurred. Two exact official-domain queries returned no usable result; three held official text indexes also produced no exact locator. These bounded misses do not establish nonexistence, withholding or destruction. No recording, original lead response or disposition was acquired.

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b2b120624ec7f6422c9b) contains five related analyst entries from one intake/lead unit, not five independent confirmations. The strongest limitation is that a recorded allegation and assignment can remain inaccurate or unverified; copies and handling stages do not independently corroborate the claimed call. No fabrication, concealment, intelligence affiliation, motive or historical cause is established.

All five tracks and 18 original requirements remain open or partial. [OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-81616da7261eabc5e597) preserves them and the new WF334/message/disposition targets; earlier actual analyses remain performed. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d8327929ef8ca38afffd), [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f083af6618c766ee6a27) and [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-735d61ee86753a1c34c6) separate manual source reading and byte/navigation checks from unperformed authentication. Prior sources, reports, conflicts and history remain unchanged. Publication is not main-branch adoption, independent audit or goal completion.
<!-- END REPORT BODY 25a529fdf45379b845e0d5326db14c56b546ffed321150d6696e5d271664c742 -->

### WF334: static court-animation links clarify the name's presentation context

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY ab23e621a3d48fbeace27b2dbbca98f5f0835837fbf100798656582adbfc28d5 -->
# WF334: static court-animation links clarify the name's presentation context

The court asset's **“Reuben, Todd”** text is explicitly referenced by a sprite instantiated as **“Names.”** That container is placed in the same authored root frame as the **American Airlines Flight #77** title. A separate **“Reuben” / “Todd”** text pair has a traceable route through a container instantiated as **“Photos,”** with a matching **Reuben** frame label.

This advances the [previous dictionary-only name check](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/926139fc0a26c869387a85030352613b8f0674d7/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf334-court-name-2026-11-02/README.md): there are actual static placement links. **It still does not authenticate WF334's reported message, establish that its “TODD RUBEN” is the same person, or prove a call or recipient.** Both spellings and all earlier uncertainties are retained. The source-to-caller join remains a TODO.

## Exact structural source locators

Source: [court flights ZIP](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/flights.zip), acquired **2026-10-27T13:40:38.310051+00:00**; **28,304,524 bytes**, SHA-256 `3a1e08b2276dcf0f1bdf5a7071113314776aca4660d73d0cf42fd91f92296801`. Exact member **AA77.swf**, **5,162,821 bytes**, SHA-256 `844dfb545c8414f9e7da1edc89ffe090a09d00d721a2c65c820e15f5fe201a06`. The member declares **SWF version 5**; that is a format version, not a creation date. No court archive was downloaded again.

Offsets below are zero-based **tag-header bytes** in this uncompressed member, including the file header. **Local frame ordinals belong to different timelines**: they are not seconds, call times, execution order or proof of joint visibility.

| Link or definition | Byte offset | Local authored frame / role |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Text ID930, `Reuben, Todd` | 192,906 | Dictionary definition |
| Sprite985 definition, 66 frames | 198,740 | Container dictionary definition |
| `Reuben` frame label inside sprite985 | 201,070 | Sprite985 frame46 |
| Place text930 at depth2, flags3 | 201,090 | Sprite985 frame46; replaces the character at that depth |
| Place sprite985, instance name `Names` | 202,128 | Root frame37 |
| Place Flight77 title433 | 113,691 | Root frame37 |
| Text1786 `Reuben`, text1787 `Todd` | 3,576,769 / 3,576,816 | Dictionary definitions |
| Place text1786 and text1787 | 3,577,511 / 3,577,528 | Both sprite1788 frame29 |
| Sprite1788 definition, 35 frames | 3,576,859 | Container dictionary definition |
| Sprite2011 definition, 69 frames | 5,106,155 | Container dictionary definition |
| `Reuben` frame label inside sprite2011 | 5,108,358 | Sprite2011 frame54 |
| Place sprite1788 inside sprite2011 | 5,108,371 | Sprite2011 frame54 |
| Place sprite2011, instance name `Photos` | 5,109,018 | Root frame37 |

[OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-09bc281d8b995ebd1489) preserves **18 selected explicit placements**, relevant sprite definitions, exact flags/depths/local frames, selected instance names, prefix bytes/body hashes and the two labels. It also retains the separate name ID331 route through sprite778, instantiated as **Pointers**. Repeated references, name variants, labels and containers in this **one** asset are not independent confirmations or extra people/calls.

The [court P200054 index](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/prosecution/flights/P200054.html) calls it a Flight77 summary and explicitly includes the other three flights. Prior saved-HTML lines **45/51/73** identify the exhibit, description and ZIP link; its SHA-256 is `866887c38f78c1f5b68ab73a695ae5345812eaf53a3e34da240f5345a1cd96c5`. The prior HTML review is reused, not counted as newly acquired evidence. Its absent access timestamp remains absent.

## The conflicting records remain separate

The [public FBI-form reproduction, physical68, Event paragraph, footer000000766](https://www.les-crises.fr/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/Team7-Box13-Flight-Call-Notes-and-302s.pdf#page=68), reports literal **TODD RUBEN**, **Flight #77**, and a saved message. The prior three-page review preserves typed **WF334**, handwritten **WF8406**, header/receipt/event/assignment fields and the assigned follow-up. No spelling correction, alias selection or date reconciliation has occurred. Mirror SHA-256 remains `e3cf374351cb753d1297433f86bb5807c56cb42a6dc69837a9df02dd0d54f4aa`; this third-party reproduction is not an authenticated FBI master.

The new structural evidence supports treating the court name as a **specific Flight77-presentation candidate**, rather than relying only on a filename or global-menu text. It does **not** prove that a human actually viewed these frames, that the local child timelines were selected together, that the two source names identify one person, or that an airline manifest or caller was authenticated. The instance name **Photos** does not identify or authenticate an actual photograph; none was examined here.

No native passenger/seat/flight record, original tip identification basis, lead-response outcome, recording/custody chain, carrier event or canvass join was acquired. This cannot be counted as a verified additional message or a refutation of the prior call-attribution account. Inaccurate tips, clerical spelling differences, different people, earlier/misattributed messages and genuine relevant messages remain untested alternatives. Original identification/contact/disposition and independent carrier/media records are the discriminators. No fabrication, concealment, motive or coordinated wrongdoing is established.

## Method and reproducible limits

The original passive tag/font parser is reused unchanged, SHA-256 `62f254468e5bbc265d3a04a44c735a09da65339c3a13e1f79046b6e503410617`. The added [structural reader](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0511240dd19da625a3d4) reads sprite IDs/frame counts, placement prefixes and selected frame labels. It records explicit-character references; it does not simulate actions, button behavior, clipping, transforms or move-only state. Root-frame placement is an authored structural relationship, not runtime visibility.

The field layout was checked against **Adobe's SWF v9 specification**, [printed/physical50–51](https://me3tweaks.com/guides/files/gui_editing_2/swf_file_format_spec_v9.pdf#page=50) and [243](https://me3tweaks.com/guides/files/gui_editing_2/swf_file_format_spec_v9.pdf#page=243). This later edition describes earlier placement/sprite structures. It is a **third-party mirror**; publisher-master identity was not independently authenticated. Local PDF text was read. Two web screenshot attempts failed and were not repeated; no successful visual specification check is claimed. Raw specification PDF stays outside the public payload. Access **2026-11-02T22:06:04.221958+00:00**, **2,061,625 bytes**, SHA-256 `78555c7f7cea97923445e08ddbccf36a166c0e130c2e4b2f17d68e2b953d58cb`.

Actual structural scope: **98 sprites**, **18,330 PlaceObject2 tags**, **3,643 explicit-character placements**, **4,282 ShowFrame tags**, **402 DoAction tags**, **253 DefineButton2 definitions**. All98 declared sprite frame counts and the root's **159** match their local ShowFrame counts. These are file-structure counts, not call counts, persons or proof percentages. Only the selected name/title/container relationships were substantively examined; this is not a complete call/roster census. Actions and buttons remain unexecuted and semantically untraced.

Six fixed byte-vector controls and one truncated-input control test the new prefix reader, including ID-absent updates and replacement flags. They passed; those controls check parsing, not historical identity. [verify.py](CITATIONS.md#nov04-92d0f4b6a8aa22ec0639) reproduces the selected result from the exact held ZIP and checks published bytes, prior-review preservation and all18 TODOs. Run `python3 verify.py --court-zip /path/to/court-flights.zip`; without that argument it checks the bundle and parser controls only. [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b8e435c6c42218884b66) states the actual runs. Same-reader reproduction and internal frame consistency do not substitute for an independent runtime implementation or the original records.

[OPEN-TODOS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ba2af2ad524b0efcdc8f) keeps every original five-track requirement and prior WF334/serial3255/print/engineering target open. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1a3f6edb5e91a3f8c73b) and the actual additive [Git text diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-21d16c8cdab2da48e6e3) record exactly what changed; the preceding report, conflicting values and source bytes remain untouched. No original-record acceptance criterion or the whole investigation is completed by this step or publication.
