### Flight77 court exhibit: the zero entry's actual structural context

**Source unit:** `telephone-zero-entry-context-2026-11-02` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2ed8c5691d608eb2d9a6).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY fa03c3f3f844096ab22e58272d5d8601518578029e485a57526d49525ef43cfc -->
# Flight77 court exhibit: the zero entry's actual structural context

2026-11-02. TEL01–TEL04; AUD016-A1 / TC05-B105 / TC06-B110. **New work in an already-held primary exhibit; no new source download, witness, carrier record or historical call verification.**

The original court-distributed file puts **09:18:58, “0 SECONDS ” and “DEPARTMENT OF JUSTICEUNCONNECTED CALL” in an authored structure that also contains Barbara Olson's name**. A separate structure labelled **Unknown** contains **09:14:57, “ON BUTTONPRESSED” and “UNCONNECTED CALL.”** This goes beyond the October27 text-dictionary check: the associations now have actual encoded character references, rather than just adjacent definitions. It does not prove that either event occurred as labelled, that a particular screen was displayed at trial, or that no other call reached the office.

## Original and scope actually tested

The [court's P200054 exhibit page](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/exhibits/prosecution/flights/P200054.html) describes summaries and links a [ZIP](https://coop.vaed.uscourts.gov/moussaoui/flights.zip) containing all four flights. The ZIP was acquired October27,2026 at13:40:38.310051UTC, not newly downloaded here: **28,304,524bytes**, SHA-256 `3a1e08b2276dcf0f1bdf5a7071113314776aca4660d73d0cf42fd91f92296801`. Its `AA77.swf` member has **5,162,821bytes**, SHA-256 `844dfb545c8414f9e7da1edc89ffe090a09d00d721a2c65c820e15f5fe201a06`. The filename does not make every stored object Flight77-specific; the file also holds navigation and other-flight material.

We reused the static text and placement readers from the [earlier Reuben-name structural check](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/2f78618d88ebcbd3e91811e9ba17f6457a6027ef/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-wf334-static-context-2026-11-02/README.md). That earlier selection did not test this zero entry. This unit selected22text IDs, their incoming explicit placement/button references and ancestor sprites. All548previously extracted text definitions reproduce unchanged. We parsed98sprite declarations and253button definitions for structure; only the selected references were substantively assessed. We did not review every stored text, execute Flash/ActionScript, simulate display state or inspect a trial recording. [Source entities and access dates](CITATIONS.md#nov04-cdf5a6b38356714ada9a) · [Selected encoded results](CITATIONS.md#nov04-f06f368e84b869f0f71a) · [Prospective scope and follow-on](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2224985b0eddc37e862a).

## 1. The zero entry has an encoded association with the Olson structure

All offsets below are **zero-based bytes in the uncompressed AA77.swf**. Frame numbers are **local authored ShowFrame ordinals**, not seconds, synchronized playback or evidence of actual joint visibility.

| Item | Text ID / definition offset | Actual encoded placement |
|---|---|---|
| Olson / Barbara |1684/3182196;1685/3182241 |sprite1704 local frame1, offsets3186267/3186303; further name definitions1689/1690 placed in that sprite at frame12, offsets3187111/3187128 |
|09:18:58 |1691/3183776 |sprite1704 frame20, offset3193352, depth396 |
|0 SECONDS (source has trailing space) |1692/3183822 |same sprite/frame, offset3193375, depth397 |
|masked destination already in source |1693/3183887 |same sprite/frame, offset3193398, depth398; no masked digits reconstructed |
|DEPARTMENT OF JUSTICEUNCONNECTED CALL |1694/3183950 |same sprite/frame, offset3193421, depth399 |

Sprite1704 is defined at3186221 and is placed inside sprite2011 (`Photos`) at local frame48, offset5108120. The parent has an **Olson** label at frame47, offset5108067; that preceding frame places sprite1686, not1704. Preserve that one-frame distinction: no runtime jump, sequencing, persistence or screen appearance was simulated. The shared named structure is a firmer exhibit association than dictionary proximity, but it is not carrier evidence or an authenticated human-to-network-event join.

## 2. The button-pressed entry belongs to a different authored structure

The09:14:57 / ON BUTTONPRESSED / UNCONNECTED CALL text definitions are2001/5075936,2002/5075982 and2003/5076055. Their explicit placements are in sprite2010 at local frame19, offsets5105096/5105119/5105165. A button definition2009, offset5077604, also refers to all three; its over/down state flags and later placement at sprite2010 frame23 are encoded, not executed.

Sprite2010 is placed inside `Photos`2011 at frame69, offset5109000, alongside its **Unknown** label at5108986. That association supports retaining this as the exhibit's separate unknown/button-pressed entry. It does not identify a person, establish an additional conversation or explain why the May2004 briefing table has seven rows while its text says eight calls. The earlier eight-minute-label crosswalk already existed; it is not a new independent finding here.

The other selected unknown entry,09:25:48 /159 SECONDS, has IDs1997/1998, definition offsets5075706/5075752, and placements in sprite2010 frame10 at5103551/5103574. This source association does not settle **159 versus154seconds**.

## 3. Summary counts are paired with names in actual button records

| Stored pair |Text IDs |Button IDs / definition offsets |Root placement frames / offsets |
|---|---|---|---|
|2CALLS / RENEEMAY |2129/2130 |2134/5160536 and2139/5161492 |144/5160748 and151/5161702 |
|1CALL / BARBARAOLSON |2136/2137 |2138/5161103 and2143/5162253 |148/5161315 and155/5162465 |
|5CALLS / UNKNOWNCALLER |2140/2141 |2142/5161861 and2144/5162550 |152/5162087 and159/5162777 |

Each name/count pair is referenced together within a button definition, including up and over/down state records. Two button definitions reuse each pair. **These repeated presentation objects are not additional call records or independent corroboration.** The3pairs also do not independently validate a census of eight attempts, completed conversations, network legs or persons. Their action bodies remain opaque and unexecuted; this test did not prove a button's interactive destination or the screen a trial viewer saw.

## Conflicting records remain side by side

- **Connection:** the September20,2001 *American Airlines Airphone Usage* reader pages43–44/internal1–2 reports an unnamed operator-routed caller “was subsequently connected” to the Solicitor General's Office. The May20,2004 briefing page2 and the court label classify09:18:58 as unconnected. The early report supplies no unique switch/leg join; the named caller and event cannot simply be equated. [Early report rendering](https://www.scribd.com/document/24392516/T7-B19-Key-302s-Fdr-Entire-Contents-FBI-302s) · [May briefing](https://www.scribd.com/document/18886083/T7-B12-Flight-93-Calls-General-Fdr-52004-DOJ-Briefing-on-Cell-and-Phone-Calls-From-AA-77-408).
- **Duration/counts:**159seconds in the briefing/court versus154seconds in the [Commission final-report note57, printed455](https://www.9-11commission.gov/report/911Report_Notes.pdf#page=7); eight calls in the briefing text versus seven displayed rows. Neither is resolved by tracing this exhibit. These are reused observations, not newly discovered discrepancies.
- **Recipient accounts:**Keyton says the later call was direct, not collect; Voss believed both were collect. The official-hosted Keyton counterpart is already held at FBI September part02 physical69; Voss's previously reviewed mirror is physical51. [Official counterpart and source comparison](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/fb0d56eeaa07cfc9348306d1cedbcbea059fc5c8/investigation/broader-evidence/fbi-keyton-acis-originals-2026-10-31/README.md). Their reports do not uniquely identify the unknown connected rows.
- **May time descriptions:**6:05a.m.Pacific in the Dallas relay versus6:13a.m.with no printed zone in the already-held LasVegas family response remain unreconciled. [Preserved relay/response qualification](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/e451c84f5494eef39539807b9b984bdb2bcd8eec/investigation/broader-evidence/telephone-may-response-qualification-2026-11-02/README.md). This unit performed no new time comparison or conversion.

Different event/leg definitions, source versions, clerical error or mistaken reconstruction are untested possibilities; intentional manipulation is also untested. None is selected as the explanation. A zero-duration named entry cannot by itself establish that no other conversation occurred. Conversely, a named exhibit label does not authenticate that person's attempted call. The [official staff report, physical94 note265](https://www.archives.gov/files/research/9-11/staff-report-sept2005.pdf#page=94), expressly leaves which unknown records represent the two reported conversations uncertain. That qualification is material contrary evidence to an assumption of claimed complete identification.

## Holdings, TODOs and test limits

[HOLDINGS.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2192ac0fc151840d3d89) distinguishes held released records from missing native inputs. The zero label, Keyton released form, September20 reader rendering and May-family released response are **held components**, not wholly missing categories. The complete Request14 submission/response/release chain, native operator/carrier events, installed definitions/clocks, office routing, actual underlying recordings/notes, and unique source-to-call joins remain open. A statement that a lead was covered is not a global investigation closure. [Open dependencies](CITATIONS.md#nov04-26f6918b79f8c57a9fc0).

The mechanical checks reproduce the frozen548-definition extraction, verify ZIP/member hashes, check all98sprite frame counts, recheck43selected explicit placement prefixes directly from encoded tag fields, and validate the limited button layout controls. They do not constitute an independent historical decoder or experimental replication. Parsing all253buttons does not mean their actions or every semantic label were reviewed.

The legacy format interpretation uses Adobe-authored documentation hosted by a mirror, not an authenticated publisher master: [SWF v9 specification, printed50–51,234–237,243](https://me3tweaks.com/guides/files/gui_editing_2/swf_file_format_spec_v9.pdf). The later edition documents legacy tags; this does not certify SWF5 playback. An initial portable-check failure resulted from sorting Python numeric keys before JSON string normalization; the source and frozen expected digest were unchanged, and the corrected check passed. A failed local SOURCES.json path lookup was also our error, not missing historical evidence.

Run `python3 -B verify.py --zip /path/to/court-flights.zip` to check bundle integrity and reproduce the source trace; without `--zip`, only the public bundle hashes are checked. [Actual execution result](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b1b56ad244b1ae86498d). Earlier source/research files and public history remain unchanged; [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5ef4c7d8c5c4fbaa3430) is the real additive text diff against the immediately preceding preserved combined review. No historical discrepancy was adjudicated and none of the18full goal criteria is accepted complete by this publication. Raw scans/SWF, contact/card data and private records are excluded.

<a id="supplement-2067d4599e340e25"></a>
### FAA and NMCC communications records: unresolved bridge and connection joins

**Source unit:** `faa-nmcc-bridge-scope-2026-11-04` · [unchanged original body](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8412481129165980c2bd).

<details>
<summary>Full owning report, including limits, failed checks and TODOs</summary>

<!-- BEGIN REPORT BODY 2067d4599e340e2585eee61f8b873b78feb6fb365f1a1e2f8978b032f785bfdf -->
# FAA and NMCC communications records: unresolved bridge and connection joins

November 4, 2026. **The released NMCC-related chronology supplies specific FAA references, but does not identify them as the FAA headquarters bridge or verify that a requested connection occurred.** Reading it beside the reproduced May 2003 answer exposes the precise joins still needed. It does not resolve the earlier disagreement or supply a later correction or personnel decision.

## The actual records compared

The [official DOT/OIG report](https://www.oig.dot.gov/sites/default/files/cc2006085.pdf) is **3,632,256 bytes**, SHA-256 **`8dde0e97d6e6adac23afea241cfff77c9a0fb4095087959f54874fc6640dc616`**. Appendix5 is physical page30. It reproduces FAA’s answer; it is not the native authoring file, original transmittal or receipt.

The [official NARA release, ISCAP2012-156 document21](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2012-156-doc-21.pdf) is **23 pages / 1,669,674 bytes**, SHA-256 **`bf6ca550673b48850b6e863bda7f846c67aaf75091553b4b8b9a28cd4a6c0fc2`**. Its text title reads **“CHRONOLOG FOR AMERICAN BOMBINGS.”** This source was acquired by the existing NR149 study and reused here; it is one record, not a second independent corroboration. Its January21,2021 declassification notice does not authenticate its creator, creation date, underlying recording or clock.

Both complete saved entities were hash-verified before and after the selected reading. [Source/version scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-528e8cd4b5841e5ba74a) and [exact page/line pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-254fd86436f099b4299b).

## Preserve the event, actor and conference fields

| Source and exact text-layer locator | What it records | What remains unproved |
|---|---|---|
| DOTphysical30 lines12–21 | FAA’s answer describes several bridges; the Air Force liaison immediately joining the **FAA headquarters** bridge and contacting **NORAD on a separate line**. | Actual membership, connection logs and calibrated times; the reproduced claim is not independently validated here. |
| DOTphysical30 lines23–25 | The answer attributes **9:24a.m.** formal Flight77 notification to NORAD logs, beside a claim of earlier continuous sharing. | Identity/version of those logs and the actual event/recipient join. |
| NARAphysical2 lines25–27 | Label **111535Z SEP01**; Navy CDRGardner says FAA reported **“Fl757”** missing from **Dulles to LA**. | Direct FAA audio, a bridge identifier, an accepted flight-number identification or a link to the claimed9:24notification. Do not silently rewrite Fl757. |
| NARAphysical15 lines11–17 | Winfield is still on an air-threat conference; **“l l 1917Z SEP01”** precedes an instruction to get FAA on a **second line**. Gardner **initiates** a call to get FAA on with the President and STRATCOM. | A completed connection, first FAA contact, Air Force liaison membership, or identity with the FAA headquarters bridge. Preserve letter-like glyphs and inaudible text. |
| NARAphysical1 lines8–10,30–34 | Opening labels **111115EDT / l l1515Z SEP01**, plus a partial FAA reference ending **inaudible**. | Earlier coverage, recovered missing words, uninterrupted recording, a complete roster or an independently calibrated clock. |

The source records therefore contain **different reported actions and communication contexts**. A later request for a particular second line cannot establish that FAA had been absent from all earlier communications. Equally, a report attributed to FAA does not authenticate the reproduced claim that the liaison immediately joined a particular bridge and separately contacted NORAD. The required identity and source-generation joins are unknown. No preferred historical account or clock value is selected. [Typed assertion crosswalk](CITATIONS.md#nov04-34f04e468ac22a2a5ac1).

## Keep the OIG findings and outcomes separate

[DOTphysical9 lines17–36](https://www.oig.dot.gov/sites/default/files/cc2006085.pdf#page=9) distinguishes reliance on an inaccurate notification timeline from its finding that executives later learned the liaison/bridge answer was inaccurate and failed to correct it. [Physical10 lines6–31](https://www.oig.dot.gov/sites/default/files/cc2006085.pdf#page=10) retains differing knowledge dates, expected correction and the liaison’s contrary account. Those findings remain visible; the NMCC chronology neither validates nor erases them.

Recommendations **1–2 on page12** address correcting the answer and **considering** administrative action. Recommendations **3–4 on page13** address recording preservation and response procedures. The report also retains the mitigating context of more than6,000materials supplied. A corrected answer’s delivery, signed action/no-action determination, recommendation implementation and investigation closure remain separate outcomes. None was recovered in this comparison.

## Reading, tests and remaining work

This unit read **eight selected embedded-text pages**: NARA1,2,15 and DOT9,10,12,13,30. Other DOTpages were keyword-located only. No native page image, audio, new OCR, timestamp parser, clock alignment, source redownload or physical test was performed. MuPDF emitted **“No default Layer config”** while locating/reading the DOTfile; the warning is retained and no visual/layout equivalence is claimed. Exact pin and source-hash checks establish this text representation, not historical truth.

The next discriminating records are the actual bridge/line IDs and participant/connection logs, source recordings and chronology generation/custody records, independently anchored clocks, and the distinct correction/decision/follow-up records. A completed connection record could strengthen a particular join; a different channel, failed attempt or contrary source could weaken it. Neither is presumed. [Open inputs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8a83969d903766923978).

All **18 original requirements across five tracks remain open or partial**. Source reuse and this completed field comparison do not close them. Earlier sources, conflicting labels/counters and failed checks are unchanged. [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-0dec9250e20eaaaec159) appends this comparison to the immediately preceding preserved record without removing historical text. [Checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-771bbf6844907501662d) and [verification limits](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a201352b25401e3f135f). Only original research notes, selected released-government text, source links and hashes are published; raw PDFs/media, private correspondence, personal/contact fields, strategy, credentials and chat-coordination material are excluded.

# A released NMCC account identifies concrete configuration and circulation records to pursue

November 4, 2026. **The OT-2 account attributes conference problems to preset configuration, a missing FAA directory number and secure-terminal baud-rate incompatibility.** It also describes FAA requests reaching a national-level conference. Both accounts must remain visible. Neither proves a common session, an absence of all FAA communications, deliberate exclusion, or who later knew about the problems.

## Actual source and scope

The [official NARA release, ISCAP2012-156 document26](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/pdf/2012-156-doc-26.pdf) is **1,156,759 bytes**, SHA-256 **`fa723f99b4b8bf580034d986ddf746f6c04aabb717e2aa2c58dbe3329cfdc025`**. The existing NR150 study acquired this record; this unit reuses that same entity, rather than claiming a new download or independent corroboration. The whole saved PDF hash was verified. This unit read **physical pages1,2,3,4,6 as embedded text**; no native page images, roster/contact fields, audio or hardware were examined.

The cover’s date extracts as **“4-Oct-0 l”**; its final letter-like glyph has not been visually confirmed. The companion study describes it as an October4,2001 cover; that interpretation and the literal extraction remain separate. The cover identifies NMCC/Joint Staff/OT-2 context, a Deputy Director of Operations line and two attachments. It does not establish native creation time, a physical signature, a verified addressee or receipt by any FAA executive. Do not date the entire20-page packet from this cover. [Exact source text/page/line pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-61e55e754a639492a328).

## Preserve the specific, potentially different contexts

| Released account / physical page and extraction lines | What it supplies | What remains unverified |
|---|---|---|
| **3:5–9** | “No FAA in Preset Conf”; direction to add FAA; no Red Switch FAA number available. | Which preset/version/endpoint, actual connection state and whether it describes the FAA headquarters bridge. |
| **3:23–32** | Transition from SEC to Air Threat Conference; services dropped due to preset configuration; inability to keep FAA in a secure conference attributed to **STU-Ill baud rates**. | The service-drop and FAA-compatibility clauses are separate statements. No configuration, handshake, repair or session records were tested here. |
| **4:51–53** | FAA passed flight-authorization requests into the national-level conference. | Source channel, session, participant and calibrated time, or how this participation relates to the preset/secure-connection account. |
| **3:67–74** | A need to capture taskings/assignments/responses; insufficient phone/work space and phone management. | Actual subsequent equipment, recording, policy, procurement, acceptance or implementation. |
| **6:3–15** | Integrated chronology calls itself a compilation; ATCcomments can be paraphrased and its times are described as accurate within a matter of minutes. | No numerical ±error bound, independently calibrated clock or visually verified row-colour/source assignment. This warning cannot automatically be applied to separate document21. |

These are **attributed source accounts**, not independently reproduced equipment failures. A technical configuration problem is a concrete alternative to an intentional-exclusion hypothesis, but it does not resolve every conflicting account. Conversely, later reported FAA participation does not establish that an earlier requested connection succeeded. The original source strings and unproven joins remain in [OBSERVATIONS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b49d5e03b7fd38d05cb5).

## Five specific retrieval chains

1. **Presets and connection events:** actual SEC/ATC rosters/configuration versions, Red Switch directory entries, and add/drop/initiation/establishment/termination records with conference and line identifiers.
2. **Secure-terminal compatibility:** corresponding FAA/NMCC endpoint models, settings/versions, interoperability tests, handshake/fault/repair/incident records and associated call segments.
3. **Report circulation and knowledge:** original cover/attachments and their version, approval, distribution, transmission and receipt trail. A sender/date does not prove that named FAA executives possessed this account when preparing or learning about the May2003answer.
4. **Source generation and channel mapping:** original ATC/SEC/NOIWON recordings/logs/notes, keeper/custody/clock records and row-to-source mapping. Literal text and source roles must survive the join.
5. **Actual implementation:** issued procedures and equipment configuration/installation/test/acceptance/follow-up records, distinguished from a stated need or recommendation.

[RECORD-TARGETS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-960852dc87c089f5e379) gives each target’s exact passage, candidate office route, discriminating test and custody uncertainty. These are research targets, not submitted requests or assertions that every record existed or survives. A failed attempt, contrary configuration or documented correction could weaken a proposed link; no outcome is presumed.

## The later correction question remains separate

The [reproduced May2003answer and DOT/OIG findings](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/597fb92d0d4da93fba6938452db95500ab74e76f/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-nmcc-bridge-scope-2026-11-04/README.md) remain unchanged. The QFR describes several bridges, the liaison on the FAA headquarters bridge and a separate NORAD line. DOT later distinguishes inaccurate notification information from its finding that executives learned of a liaison/bridge error and did not correct it. The military configuration account neither establishes their knowledge nor erases that noncorrection finding.

DOT’s page12 recommendations to correct the answer and consider administrative action remain distinct from page13 recording/procedure recommendations. No actual corrected-answer delivery, signed consideration/action/no-action decision or recommendation disposition was obtained here. [Unchanged exact DOTpins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-7f49d90d24df1aca1537).

All **18 original requirements across five tracks remain open or partial**. This source-backed record-target refinement does not complete them. No peer timestamp/UA175/engagement analysis, new media operation, clock calibration, source signature/custody authentication or configuration experiment was performed. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-5d1b52e275f7ab24d339), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-988d16e09d4d279ee456), [verification scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-6512d93efa922a865d01), and [actual additions-only predecessor diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-2d82ba7b0c4b55e3b121). Earlier sources, conflicting values and failed checks remain preserved. Only original research notes, selected released-government text, links and hashes are published; raw PDFs/media, personal/contact fields, private correspondence, strategy, credentials and chat coordination are excluded.

# FAA contact-role and conference entries: service and timing joins remain unresolved

November4,2026. **A released event-log copy contains an FAA point-of-contact role with a DSN-labeled field. A separate pass-down fragment records FAA initiating a hijack conference.** These are specific original-record leads. Neither establishes the availability of a RedSwitch endpoint, successful membership on a particular bridge, or knowledge of the later inaccurate answer.

## Exactly what was found

The [official NARA document3](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/2012-156-doc-3.pdf#page=2), physical2/extractionline14, contains the literal prefix **“FAA POC”** and service label **“DSN.”** The public derivative retains those institutional labels and omits the person’s name and contact value. It is **not a verbatim quotation of the entire line**. The role label does not establish the person’s employer or assignment, or identity with an unnamed/redacted liaison in another record. [Minimized observation](CITATIONS.md#nov04-b74ca9aac8d52598c03d).

The complete released DOC3entity is **604,018bytes**, SHA-256 **`7246f587794b3fa57f5f582f65ae968610acd54e7fb674758897ebb7dbd9c4ff`**. This is a released copy containing note/chat-style material, not an authenticated native electronic export or source creation/clock history. Its field cannot simply be dated from adjacent messages or assumed available when a conference first opened.

The [official NARA document1](https://www.archives.gov/files/declassification/iscap/2012-156-doc-1.pdf#page=1), physical1/extractionlines47–54, says **“FAA INITIATES A HIJACK CONFERNCE”** and describes an air-threat conference convened at NORAD’s request. The original **CONFERNCE** spelling and extracted time glyphs **1.UOZ / 1.1:451.** remain uncorrected. No reliable clock value is assigned from them. The complete DOC1entity is **603,753bytes**, SHA-256 **`5add378cc48144943ba4293dbc1f4fc682d07d166cf70320ea4c54b7bd268560`**. It is a two-page released fragment, not proof of a complete originating log. [Exact noncontact text and source pins](CITATIONS.md#nov04-1f36de47ac309cd69819).

The existing NR151study acquired these two source entities; this unit reuses them. Two files are not automatically two independent observations. Whole-file hashes were verified without a new download, image/clock test or source alteration.

## Preserve the unresolved comparison

The [OT2account](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/6d148a319c76e579b59dece22395501d66b64463/investigation/broader-evidence/faa-ot2-config-scope-2026-11-04/README.md) reports no FAA in an initial preset, no RedSwitchFAA number available, and secure-conference compatibility problems. The new role/service field remains beside those statements. **No common service, endpoint, conference, writing time or availability has been established.** The records have not been adjudicated as contradictory or reconciled.

A directory/contact-role entry does not establish a completed call; conference initiation does not establish the full roster or a common FAAheadquarters/NMCC/NORADbridge. The same sources can support more than one explanation, including different sessions, timing, source generations or recording errors. Those possibilities remain untested. The contact’s identity is not used to reconstruct redactions or attribute wrongdoing. [Claim/source comparison](CITATIONS.md#nov04-067df4af4f9bf7ba0fb5).

Four next chains are now specific: the entry’s original creation/version/shift/custody; duty-role and functional directory/service records; actual call/bridge/participant/configuration events; and report/QFRdistribution, correction and decision receipts. A documented later entry, inapplicable service or failed connection could weaken a proposed join. An authenticated endpoint/session/receipt could strengthen it. [Targets and tests](CITATIONS.md#nov04-3ee97fe3c16ebe3ae165). Nothing was requested or sent.

## Scope, handling and remaining requirements

Four operational text pages received literal FAA/F.A.A.locator searches; relevant event/role windows were inspected. A previously unread contact-only third page received **institutional/service-label presence testing only**. That limited text query does not establish a complete directory, absence of FAA, or the meanings of uninspected fields.

A prototype context screen missed DSN-format contact details in a local preview. The raw preview/context file and its erroneous no-contact status flags are excluded from publication; an additive handling correction preserves the failure locally. The final public candidate was manually reviewed and tested as a fixed role/service-only derivative. **No personal name or contact value appears in this package.** No person was contacted and no identity join was attempted. [Verification scope](CITATIONS.md#nov04-8f193b3997a93ab75210).

The OIG’s distinct notification and known-liaison-error/noncorrection findings remain preserved. Actual corrected-QFRdelivery, signed consideration/action/no-action and recommendation disposition remain unacquired. All **18 original requirements across five tracks remain open or partial**. Existing source values, uncertain clocks, metadata/counters and failed checks are unchanged. [TODOs](CITATIONS.md#nov04-510de7265d61bbf4d7a4), [checklist](CITATIONS.md#nov04-badfb7653758f2d9801a) and [additions-only predecessor diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-de278733f159cf61aa77). Raw PDFs/media, personal/contact values, private case material/correspondence, strategy, credentials and chat coordination are not published.

# A separate FBI steel artifact and a conservation-record trail

**November 4, 2026.** Two institutional sources add useful original-record targets: an FBI-held commemorative steel artifact reportedly transferred in **2010**, and the museum’s described conservation documentation. Neither supplies an authenticated original recovery chain or identifies a WTC 7 scientific specimen. A large difference between two published debris totals is preserved separately.

Scope: **STL01–STL03, AUD011/AUD016, B188/H005**. All eighteen original five-track requirements remain open or partial. Earlier steel/telephone findings, conflicting records and missing originals remain unchanged.

## What the sources establish

The FBI’s [artifact page](https://www.fbi.gov/history/artifacts/911-world-trade-center-steel-beam), web-reader lines **48 and 54**, describes a steel piece reportedly received from the Port Authority of New York and New Jersey in **2010**. Its original function is tentative: **“an elevator system or a subway rail.”** The page also describes it as coming from **“one of the eight buildings.”** These are the agency’s public descriptions, not independently authenticated origin, receipt or examination findings. No artifact identifier, accession number, original recovery marking, evidence-item number or building-specific source is supplied in the read paragraphs.

The page therefore supplies a **separate holder/transfer lead**: the FBI artifact accession/property record and Port Authority release/selection/dispatch record could identify the piece and its previous handling. A 2010 commemorative transfer is not a documented 2001 investigative seizure. Proximity on a history page to general investigation totals does not join this artifact to an original evidence item, a forensic test or a particular building. The tentative elevator/rail description must not become a confirmed structural-column or WTC 7 attribution.

The museum’s [conservation page](https://www.911memorial.org/visit/museum/collection/conservation), saved parser-text lines **82, 85 and 88**, describes outside conservators helping preserve Hangar 17 artifacts in 2002, and later work involving condition documentation and analysis. That identifies another potential records chain: object-level condition reports, treatment histories, material analyses and their associated photographs. These are **possible retrieval targets, not acquired reports**. This general page does not establish which artifacts were treated, whether a given report survives, whether a test concerned explosives, or whether the FBI piece passed through the museum. No common accession or object identity was established.

The same page, lines **90 and 92**, describes care to preserve historically significant damage and to limit treatment changes. This is relevant contrary evidence to an assumption that conservation necessarily erased damage or suppressed evidence. It is a statement of practice, not independent proof of every treatment’s compliance. Actual before/after records and dates are needed to separate recovery damage, later handling, stabilization and subsequent deterioration. No physical, chemical, fracture or temperature test was performed here.

## Conflicting numerical accounts — unresolved

| Source actually read | Original published value | Locator and scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FBI artifact description | **“180 million tons”** | Web-reader line 54; describes debris recovered from Ground Zero. |
| Museum [FAQ](https://911memorial.org/911-faqs) | **“1.8 million tons”** | Saved parser-text line 494; recovery-duration paragraph describes debris cleared from the site. |

The published numeric values differ by a factor of **100** if compared in the same unit. Their measurement definitions, source worksheets and intended scope have not been established. Neither value is selected or silently corrected; no cause for the difference is adjudicated. Editorial error, different source definitions and other explanations remain untested. The underlying statistics and publication/edit histories would discriminate between them.

This is a **public-description discrepancy**, not an independent debris measurement and not evidence that steel was fabricated, removed unlawfully or concealed. Its importance is lower than an authenticated specimen/transfer/testing record. It does not establish a defect in NIST’s calculations or a collapse mechanism. A total-debris figure must not be substituted for the mass of structural steel or the number of samples.

## Provenance and actual reading

The FBI article was read successfully through the web reader before an ordinary anonymous downloader returned **403**. That later route was stopped. A selected-line transcription from the earlier reader result is retained locally with its own hash; **no FBI HTTP-entity hash or historical native-document authentication is claimed**. The preserved web-reader labels are representation-specific lines, not original pagination. No image or high-resolution link was followed.

Two museum HTML entities were acquired with HTTP **200**, preserved separately, and parsed to text. Relevant conservation sections and the FAQ recovery paragraph were inspected. The hashes below identify the captured representations, not native 2002/2010 documents, original wire bytes or physical objects.

| Captured representation | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- |
| FBI selected-line transcription of the web-reader result | `6003dc5065f5ebbbb7ec34143ac82df91faac306d172c008ce66903e43b5a2c2` |
| Museum conservation HTTP HTML entity, 80,873 bytes | `f642240183905a1a6db663daa581c364596adf64c6e4694c69b7e46afbe1566b` |
| Museum FAQ HTTP HTML entity, 236,662 bytes | `f1b24e8dcf5ba7d7fe62a4b089f453a8aa7d860842dd75ff9747f052d5588bbe` |

Exact URLs, access/request dates, representations, separate parsed-text hashes and reading scopes are in [SOURCE-PINS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-79cd00fbb12997bf09f1). No linked collections policy, contract, accession file, native inventory, condition report, analysis result, image, audio or video was acquired/read. Earlier source failure and handling limits remain in the native research record. The 2012 historic-resources report was inadvertently reopened before its earlier review was recognized; it contributes no new claim here and was not downloaded again. The scoped duplication search is not a universal novelty census.

## Missing records stay TODOs

[OPEN-TODOS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-00eb14775ca272425b59) retains four precise next chains:

1. **FBI and Port Authority:** the 2010 artifact accession/property/transfer record, original artifact ID, selection and dispatch/receipt records, recovery/marking photographs and any earlier inventory aliases. Inspect actual identifiers before proposing a specimen match.
2. **Museum Collections/Conservation and Port Authority:** object-specific condition/treatment/analysis reports, source-image associations, original and later label versions, conservator project files and corresponding object/loan/intake records. Applicability to any selected steel piece must be demonstrated; no laboratory result is presumed.
3. **Each relevant holder:** dated recovery, cutting, handling, cleaning, treatment, storage and transfer events with the actual item key, preserving uncertain matches and contrary records. Keep memorial preservation and forensic sampling separate.
4. **FBI/museum publication records and original statistical source:** source worksheets, definitions and edit/version records for the two debris totals. Do not equate a corrected webpage with a changed historical specimen or laboratory result.

Nothing was sent, requested from a person, tested physically, deleted or overwritten. Existing FAA, telephone, recorder, materials, steel, Beverly Hills certification/attachment and native-data TODOs remain open. Publication of this review does not complete them.

The research original and redundant public derivative remain separately named. [CHECKLIST.md](CITATIONS.md#nov04-ec5646dd9f38d4f7ea96), [CLAIMS.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-d5e229bd647a1f425c1e), [VERIFICATION.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-933debb5f5cdc4569d58), [BUNDLE-HASHES.json](CITATIONS.md#nov04-924be235c666a4cb3aaa) and [REVISION.diff](CITATIONS.md#nov04-39a9eb11e2590412b4d5) retain scope, uncertainties and an exact additions-only diff against the immediately preceding preserved combined review. No PDF/layout verification is claimed. Remote publication verification is a separate step from historical authentication.

# A specific photograph lead for the filmed N10-C3B marking

November 4, 2026. **A previously acquired NIST-linked archive listing names a specific photograph, [SPANDRELS N10-C3B_1.jpg](CITATIONS.md#nov04-85ffbec898dfcdeaa20a), whose filename contains the marking previously read in the steel movie.** A fresh read-only metadata response confirms the same file record. This narrows a retrieval target; no image-to-movie, building-origin or custody match has been authenticated.

## What is established

Row **files[805]**, zero based, of the preserved 1,000-item listing gives file ID **1BN8cuz5mbLP_zHp9EvOjBILdwIYPMsQe**, JPEG MIME type, reported size **748,330 bytes**, and object creation/modification **2019-11-26T06:03:27.620Z**. The new metadata response agrees on all seven compared fields: ID, title, type, size, URL and both dates. [Selected row](CITATIONS.md#nov04-288620abb8feda4284e5) and [current metadata](CITATIONS.md#nov04-a106176b7ae82317ca95).

The complete listing serialization is **353,962 bytes**, SHA-256 **`e1c4a3399ddb73df2022b64b14db371d0865264dd24ddb9b808516fbadb3df33`**. That hash identifies the saved listing, **not the photograph**. The image itself was neither downloaded nor viewed in this unit; its SHA-256 remains unknown. Connector metadata explicitly reports **access_not_verified**. An anonymous web-tool open did not retrieve the image-view page, and no HTTP status or access denial is invented from that tool limitation. The archive linkage is the already-documented [NIST archive page](https://www.nist.gov/world-trade-center-investigation/photos-videos-and-simulations) and its collected-steel repository; current anonymous image access remains unverified.

The [preceding movie study](https://github.com/AveLyra/911-conspiracy-investigation/blob/da9fd9dfb7d1af87c88143b0148914439420c4cd/investigation/broader-evidence/steel-tape2-3-source-2026-11-03/README.md) recorded **N10-C3B** at decoder-input index **8440**, reported presentation **563.229897 seconds**. Its lower **B-1/8-1** reading remains uncertain. Those are earlier qualified observations, not new image readings. The verified Intel-store guard now reports that the share is not mounted; no movie, frame, image or recovery copy was read, rerendered or repopulated.

## A report locator, with its limits preserved
